local MODULE_SOURCES = {}

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Review/Dashboard"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Review/Dashboard

	Ringkasan performa 1 sesi. Dipanggil di akhir WarAI.detach() atau via
	WarAI.report(). Berguna untuk tuning cepat antar-session.
]]

local Dashboard = {}

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Features.Review.Scorecard
local Scorecard = require("Features/Review/Scorecard")

---@module Features.Orders.OrderExecutor
local OrderExecutor = require("Features/Orders/OrderExecutor")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Core.Scheduler
local Scheduler = require("Features/Core/Scheduler")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---Format angka dengan unit.
local function fmt(value, unit)
	if value == nil then return "-" end
	return string.format("%.3f %s", value, unit or "")
end

---Print ringkasan ke Logger.
function Dashboard.report()
	local score = Scorecard.summary()
	local lat = Scheduler.latencyStats()
	local interval, intervalSrc = CombatModel.hitIntervalSeconds()
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

	local openAttacks = score.open or 0
	local won = score.attacksWon or 0
	local lost = score.attacksLost or 0
	local total = won + lost
	local winRate = total > 0 and (won / total * 100) or 0

	Logger.info("Features.Review.Dashboard", "report", {
		-- Waktu
		tickPeriod = fmt(period, "s"),
		hitInterval = fmt(interval, "s"),
		hitIntervalSource = intervalSrc,

		-- Reaksi
		reactions = lat.count or 0,
		medianReactionMs = lat.median and (lat.median * 1000) or nil,
		maxReactionMs = lat.max and (lat.max * 1000) or nil,

		-- Combat
		attacksWon = won,
		attacksLost = lost,
		attacksOpen = openAttacks,
		winRatePercent = winRate,
		medianBreakErrorSeconds = score.medianBreakErrorSeconds,
		interruptions = score.interruptions,

		-- Orders
		ordersSent = OrderExecutor.stats.sent,
		ordersAccepted = OrderExecutor.stats.accepted,
		ordersNotAccepted = OrderExecutor.stats.notAccepted,
		ordersDryRun = OrderExecutor.stats.dryRun,
		ordersRejected = OrderExecutor.stats.rejectedAtSend,
		userOverrides = OrderExecutor.stats.userOverrides,

		-- Actions
		actionsSent = ActionDispatcher.stats.dispatched,
		actionsDryRun = ActionDispatcher.stats.dryRun,
		actionsDuplicates = ActionDispatcher.stats.duplicates,
		actionsFailures = ActionDispatcher.stats.failures,
		actionsRefused = ActionDispatcher.stats.refusals,
	})

	-- Print human-readable.
	local lines = {
		"",
		"=== CTW_WarAI Session Report ===",
		string.format("Tick period     : %.3f s", period or 0),
		string.format("Hit interval    : %.3f s (%s)", interval, intervalSrc),
		"",
		string.format("Reactions       : %d (median %.0f ms, max %.0f ms)", lat.count or 0,
			(lat.median or 0) * 1000, (lat.max or 0) * 1000),
		"",
		string.format("Attacks won/lost: %d / %d (%.1f%% win rate)", won, lost, winRate),
		string.format("Open attacks    : %d", openAttacks),
		score.medianBreakErrorSeconds and string.format("Break error     : %+.2f s median", score.medianBreakErrorSeconds) or "Break error     : -",
		"",
		string.format("Orders sent     : %d (accepted %d, unaccepted %d)",
			OrderExecutor.stats.sent, OrderExecutor.stats.accepted, OrderExecutor.stats.notAccepted),
		string.format("Actions sent    : %d (failures %d, refusals %d, dry-run %d)",
			ActionDispatcher.stats.dispatched, ActionDispatcher.stats.failures,
			ActionDispatcher.stats.refusals, ActionDispatcher.stats.dryRun),
		"",
	}
	for _, line in ipairs(lines) do
		print(line)
	end
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Review.Dashboard", Dashboard)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/ThreatHeat"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/ThreatHeat

	Peta panas ancaman lintas-tick. Setiap tick:
	- heat[tile] += strength(unit musuh) untuk setiap unit musuh di sekitar tile
	- heat[tile] *= decay (0.85/tick default)

	Dipakai oleh DefensePlanner.stage untuk memilih tile mana yang paling butuh
	reinforcement, bukan hanya tile yang "dekat capital" atau "dekat enemy".
]]

local ThreatHeat = {}

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

-- Constants.
local DECAY_PER_TICK = 0.5
local RADIUS = 4
local ENEMY_STRENGTH_UNIT = 20  -- approximate health per unit

-- State (module-level, persists across ticks).
ThreatHeat.heat = {}
ThreatHeat.lastTick = nil

---Tick decay dan refresh.
---@param world table
function ThreatHeat.update(world)
	local tickId = world.tickNumber or 0
	if ThreatHeat.lastTick == tickId then
		return  -- sudah di-update tick ini
	end
	ThreatHeat.lastTick = tickId

	-- Decay semua heat dulu.
	for tile, value in pairs(ThreatHeat.heat) do
		local decayed = value * DECAY_PER_TICK
		if decayed < 0.5 then
			ThreatHeat.heat[tile] = nil
		else
			ThreatHeat.heat[tile] = decayed
		end
	end

	-- BFS sederhana dari setiap unit musuh, radius RADIUS hop.
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.enemyUnits or {}) do
		local startTile = unit.tile
		local seen = { [startTile] = 0 }
		local queue = { startTile }
		local head = 1
		local strength = (unit.health or ENEMY_STRENGTH_UNIT)
		while head <= #queue do
			local current = queue[head]
			head += 1
			local dist = seen[current]
			if dist <= RADIUS then
				local decay = 1 / (1 + dist)
				ThreatHeat.heat[current] = (ThreatHeat.heat[current] or 0) + strength * decay
				if dist < RADIUS then
					local tile = MapGraph.tiles[current]
					if tile ~= nil then
						for _, nbr in ipairs(tile.landNeighbours) do
							if seen[nbr] == nil then
								seen[nbr] = dist + 1
								table.insert(queue, nbr)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

---Ambil heat score di sebuah tile.
---@param tileName string
---@return number
function ThreatHeat.at(tileName)
	return ThreatHeat.heat[tileName] or 0
end

---Reset state (dipanggil saat map rebuild).
function ThreatHeat.reset()
	table.clear(ThreatHeat.heat)
	ThreatHeat.lastTick = nil
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.ThreatHeat", ThreatHeat)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Air/AirPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Air/AirPlanner

	Air, conservative and measured (DESIGN §9; air combat, AA and bombing are server-side and were
	never observed: 0 deployments by anyone in either recording, air.md §2).
	- Close air support goes to the air region of the tiles this tick's plan attacks: CAS multiplies
	  our attackers' damage over the defender tile's region (client mirror TroopCommon:718-743). Its
	  effect is not counted in our odds until the Scorecard has measured it.
	- Fighters join a deployment when an enemy country already has planes in that region.
	- Range is checked here (the client's Deploy button is not strictly range-gated, air.md §3.3):
	  3D distance from the airfield's tile to the region Part <= the smallest Range of the selected
	  types x (1 + (level - 1) / (airfieldMaxLvl - 1) x (maxAirfieldRangeCoef - 1)).
	- A deployment whose battleInfo progress falls below the enemy side's is pulled out
	  (Planes.TryRetreat); progress is each side's share of the fight, so "below the enemy's" is the
	  definitional losing point (T1).
	Plane production is not requested in v1 (DESIGN §7.2 tier 3 needs a measured use first).
]]

local AirPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local TIER = "AIR"
-- T3 CommonTypes.PlaneData: the six plane types, all sent in every deploy.
local PLANE_TYPES = { "Bomber I", "Bomber II", "CAS I", "CAS II", "Fighter I", "Fighter II" }
local CAS_TYPES = { "CAS I", "CAS II" }
local FIGHTER_TYPES = { "Fighter I", "Fighter II" }
local ATTACK_PURPOSES = { capitalStrike = true, kill = true, surroundKill = true, relief = true, attack = true, cutOffAttack = true, pincerCut = true }

Formula.define({
	id = "air.deployRange",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "AirforceMenuLocalScript:203-223 (air.md §3.3)",
	expression = "min(Range of selected types) * (1 + (level - 1) / (airfieldMaxLvl - 1) * (maxAirfieldRangeCoef - 1)) when airfieldMaxLvl > 1",
	unit = "studs",
	inputs = { minRange = "smallest <Type>Range among the selected types", level = "airfield level", airfieldMaxLvl = "Airforce.airfieldMaxLvl", maxAirfieldRangeCoef = "Airforce.maxAirfieldRangeCoef" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		if inputs.airfieldMaxLvl <= 1 then
			return inputs.minRange
		end

		return inputs.minRange * (1 + (inputs.level - 1) / (inputs.airfieldMaxLvl - 1) * (inputs.maxAirfieldRangeCoef - 1))
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { minRange = 100, level = 1, airfieldMaxLvl = 10, maxAirfieldRangeCoef = 2 }, expected = 100, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { minRange = 100, level = 10, airfieldMaxLvl = 10, maxAirfieldRangeCoef = 2 }, expected = 200, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { minRange = 100, level = 2, airfieldMaxLvl = 10, maxAirfieldRangeCoef = 2 }, expected = 100 * (1 + 1 / 9), tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

---Our airfields with their decoded plane counts.
---@param country Instance
---@return table array
local function ourAirfields(country)
	local airfields = {}

	for _, city in ipairs(Resolver.requireChild(country, "Cities"):GetChildren()) do
		local airfield = city:FindFirstChild("Airfield")

		if airfield ~= nil then
			local planeText = Resolver.optionalAttribute(airfield, "planeData", "string", "written by the server on its first tick")

			table.insert(airfields, {
				city = city,
				tileName = Resolver.requireAttribute(city, "tileName", "string"),
				level = Resolver.requireAttribute(airfield, "level", "number"),
				planes = planeText and Serializer.decode(planeText, airfield:GetFullName() .. ".planeData") or {},
			})
		end
	end

	table.sort(airfields, function(first, second)
		return first.city.Name < second.city.Name
	end)

	return airfields
end

---Build a deploy for one airfield and region from the given types that reach it.
---@param airforce Instance
---@param airfield table
---@param regionPart BasePart
---@param types table
---@return table? planeData, number? range, number distance
local function deployFor(airforce, airfield, regionPart, types)
	local distance = (MapGraph.tiles[airfield.tileName].position - regionPart.Position).Magnitude
	local planeData = {}
	local minRange = nil
	local total = 0

	for _, typeName in ipairs(PLANE_TYPES) do
		planeData[typeName] = 0
	end

	for _, typeName in ipairs(types) do
		local count = airfield.planes[typeName]

		if type(count) == "number" and count >= 1 then
			local typeRange = Resolver.requireAttribute(airforce, string.gsub(typeName, " ", "") .. "Range", "number")
			local reach = Formula.evaluate("air.deployRange", {
				minRange = typeRange,
				level = airfield.level,
				airfieldMaxLvl = Resolver.requireAttribute(airforce, "airfieldMaxLvl", "number"),
				maxAirfieldRangeCoef = Resolver.requireAttribute(airforce, "maxAirfieldRangeCoef", "number"),
			})

			if reach >= distance then
				planeData[typeName] = math.floor(count)
				total += planeData[typeName]
				minRange = minRange and math.min(minRange, reach) or reach
			end
		end
	end

	if total == 0 then
		return nil, nil, distance
	end

	return planeData, minRange, distance
end

---Run the air planner for one tick.
---@param world table
---@param plan table this tick's front plan
function AirPlanner.run(world, plan)
	if not Settings.get("air.enabled") then
		return
	end

	local airMap = workspace:FindFirstChild("AirMap")

	if airMap == nil then
		Logger.warn("Features.Air.AirPlanner", "noAirMap", { reason = "workspace.AirMap missing" })
		return
	end

	local airforce = Resolver.requireChild(world.meInstance, "Airforce")
	local airfields = ourAirfields(world.meInstance)

	-- Pull out of fights we are losing.
	for _, region in ipairs(airMap:GetChildren()) do
		if region:FindFirstChild(world.me) ~= nil then
			local infoText = region:GetAttribute("battleInfo")

			if type(infoText) == "string" and infoText ~= "" then
				local sides = Serializer.decode(infoText, region:GetFullName() .. ".battleInfo")
				local ours = 0
				local enemyBest = 0

				for _, side in ipairs(sides) do
					if side.country == world.me then
						ours = side.progress
					elseif world.enemies[side.country] then
						enemyBest = math.max(enemyBest, side.progress)
					end
				end

				if ours < enemyBest then
					ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
						remote = "TryRetreat",
						args = { region },
						key = "retreat:" .. region.Name .. ":" .. infoText,
						reason = string.format("losing the air battle over %s (%.2f vs %.2f)", region.Name, ours, enemyBest),
						tier = TIER,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Support this tick's attacks. [SPRINT-1] Score regions by need, not alphabetical.
	local regions = {}
	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		if ATTACK_PURPOSES[order.purpose] then
			local regionName = MapGraph.tiles[order.destination].airRegion
			if regionName ~= nil then
				-- Skor: stack besar * (1 - ratio). Region yang attacknya marginal
				-- mendapat prioritas CAS tertinggi karena butuh damage bonus.
				local stackSize = (order.facts and order.facts.stackSize) or 1
				local ratio = (order.facts and order.facts.ratio) or 0
				local need = stackSize * (1 - math.min(ratio, 1))
				local prev = regions[regionName] or 0
				if need > prev then
					regions[regionName] = need
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local regionNames = {}
	for regionName in pairs(regions) do
		table.insert(regionNames, regionName)
	end
	-- Sort menurun berdasarkan skor kebutuhan, tiebreak alfabetis.
	table.sort(regionNames, function(first, second)
		if regions[first] ~= regions[second] then
			return regions[first] > regions[second]
		end
		return first < second
	end)

	local deployed = {}

	for _, regionName in ipairs(regionNames) do
		local regionPart = airMap:FindFirstChild(regionName)

		if regionPart ~= nil and regionPart:FindFirstChild(world.me) == nil then
			local enemyPresent = false
			for _, child in ipairs(regionPart:GetChildren()) do
				if world.enemies[child.Name] then
					enemyPresent = true
				end
			end

			local types = table.clone(CAS_TYPES)
			if enemyPresent or #regionNames > 1 then
				-- Kalau ada >1 region aktif, kemungkinan musuh respons.
				for _, fighterType in ipairs(FIGHTER_TYPES) do
					table.insert(types, fighterType)
				end
			end

			for _, airfield in ipairs(airfields) do
				if deployed[regionName] == nil then
					local planeData, range, distance = deployFor(airforce, airfield, regionPart, types)

					if planeData ~= nil then
						deployed[regionName] = { airfield = airfield.city.Name, planes = planeData, range = range, distance = distance }

						ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
							remote = "TryDeployPlanes",
							args = { airfield.city, regionPart, planeData },
							key = "deploy:" .. regionName .. ":" .. airfield.city.Name,
							reason = string.format("support attacks under %s (%.1f of %.1f studs)", regionName, distance, range),
							tier = TIER,
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Air.AirPlanner", "run", { airfields = #airfields, targetRegions = regionNames, deployed = deployed })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Air.AirPlanner", AirPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Combat/CombatFormulas"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Combat/CombatFormulas

	The land combat arithmetic as declared formulas. Every expression copies
	TroopCommon.CalculateStatsAgainstTroop (dump/scripts/ReplicatedStorage.Common.TroopCommon.lua,
	lines 479-778), the shared module whose per-hit numbers matched the server's hits to 1e-6 on
	2,717/2,717 recorded attacker hits (ground-combat.md §3.3). The worked examples are those
	recorded hits.

	The stacked engagement (combat.stackRatio) is derived (T1) from the per-hit rules and the
	recorded thresholds: every attacker hits once per round, each takes the defender's strength
	split over the attackers still active, an attacker stops at 20 health after the hit that takes it
	there (it ends clamped at exactly 20 in 119/120 recorded cases, TroopCommon:783 uses health - 20),
	and the defender is beaten at 10 (TroopCommon:785 uses health - 10; every recorded retreat and
	death happened at exactly 10). The ratio is the damage the stack delivers before every attacker
	is spent, divided by the defender's health above 10; for one attacker it equals the game's own
	arrow ratio except that the final (clamped) hit counts in full, as it does on the server.
]]

local CombatFormulas = {}

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

-- Constants.
local EXPERIENCE_ATTACK_COEF = 0.2
local RIVER_DEFENSE_BASE = 0.05
local RIVER_DEFENSE_PER_MODIFIER = 0.25
local RIVER_ATTACK_BASE = -0.05
local RIVER_ATTACK_PER_MODIFIER = -0.3
local COUNTER_STRONG = 1.4285714285714286
local COUNTER_WEAK = 0.7
local ATTACKER_HEALTH_FLOOR = 20
local DEFENDER_HEALTH_FLOOR = 10

local COUNTER_TABLE = {
	AntiTank = { Tanks = COUNTER_STRONG },
	Infantry = { AntiTank = COUNTER_STRONG, Tanks = COUNTER_WEAK },
	Tanks = { AntiTank = COUNTER_WEAK, Infantry = COUNTER_STRONG },
}

CombatFormulas.ATTACKER_HEALTH_FLOOR = ATTACKER_HEALTH_FLOOR
CombatFormulas.DEFENDER_HEALTH_FLOOR = DEFENDER_HEALTH_FLOOR

Formula.define({
	id = "combat.defenderStrength",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "TroopCommon.CalculateStatsAgainstTroop lines 511-610",
	expression = "((defense + cityDefense) * fortFactor * armyDefenseCoef + armyDefenseBoost) * groupDefenseCoef * terrainDefenseCoef * riverFactor * ideologyDefenseCoef; fortFactor = 1 + fortDefense if fortDefense > 0; riverFactor = 1 + 0.05 + 0.25 r if r > 0",
	unit = "health removed from each attacker per hit before the attacker-count split",
	inputs = {
		baseDefense = "defense attribute",
		cityDefense = "city defenses x cityDefensesStrengthModifier, 0 when the defender's country does not hold the city",
		fortDefense = "tile fortDefense (0 without a bunker)",
		armyDefenseCoef = "Army.<Class>DefenseCoef",
		armyDefenseBoost = "Army.<Class>DefenseBoost",
		groupDefenseCoef = "army group <Class>DefenseCoef (1 outside a group)",
		terrainDefenseCoef = "terrainModifiers[defender tile terrain].<Class>DefenseCoef (1 when absent)",
		riverModifier = "ArmyCommon.GetRiverModifierBetweenTiles(attacker tile, defender tile), 0..1",
		ideologyDefenseCoef = "defender Army.attackBoostAgainst<attacker ideology>Coef (1 when absent)",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		local strength = inputs.baseDefense + inputs.cityDefense

		if inputs.fortDefense > 0 then
			strength *= 1 + inputs.fortDefense
		end

		strength = strength * inputs.armyDefenseCoef + inputs.armyDefenseBoost
		strength *= inputs.groupDefenseCoef
		strength *= inputs.terrainDefenseCoef

		if inputs.riverModifier > 0 then
			strength *= 1 + RIVER_DEFENSE_BASE + inputs.riverModifier * RIVER_DEFENSE_PER_MODIFIER
		end

		return strength * inputs.ideologyDefenseCoef
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { baseDefense = 5, cityDefense = 0, fortDefense = 0, armyDefenseCoef = 1, armyDefenseBoost = 0, groupDefenseCoef = 1, terrainDefenseCoef = 1, riverModifier = 1, ideologyDefenseCoef = 1 }, expected = 6.5, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { baseDefense = 6, cityDefense = 0, fortDefense = 0, armyDefenseCoef = 1, armyDefenseBoost = 0, groupDefenseCoef = 1, terrainDefenseCoef = 1, riverModifier = 1, ideologyDefenseCoef = 1 }, expected = 7.8, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { baseDefense = 4, cityDefense = 0, fortDefense = 0, armyDefenseCoef = 1, armyDefenseBoost = 0, groupDefenseCoef = 1, terrainDefenseCoef = 1.2, riverModifier = 0, ideologyDefenseCoef = 1.1 }, expected = 5.28, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "combat.damageTaken",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "TroopCommon.CalculateStatsAgainstTroop lines 612-632",
	expression = "defenderStrength / max(1, attackerCount) * (noSupplyCombatCoef if the defender has noSupply or noMoney)",
	unit = "health removed from one attacker per hit",
	inputs = {
		defenderStrength = "combat.defenderStrength",
		attackerCount = "attackers on the defender",
		defenderUnsupplied = "boolean: defender noSupply or noMoney",
		noSupplyCombatCoef = "defender Army.noSupplyCombatCoef",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		local taken = inputs.defenderStrength / math.max(1, inputs.attackerCount)

		if inputs.defenderUnsupplied then
			taken *= inputs.noSupplyCombatCoef
		end

		return taken
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { defenderStrength = 6.5, attackerCount = 1, defenderUnsupplied = false, noSupplyCombatCoef = 0.6 }, expected = 6.5, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { defenderStrength = 6.5, attackerCount = 2, defenderUnsupplied = true, noSupplyCombatCoef = 0.6 }, expected = 1.95, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "combat.counter",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "TroopCommon.CalculateStatsAgainstTroop lines 706-712",
	expression = "1/0.7 for AntiTank->Tanks, Infantry->AntiTank, Tanks->Infantry; 0.7 for Infantry->Tanks, Tanks->AntiTank; else 1",
	unit = "multiplier on the attacker's damage",
	inputs = { attackerClass = "troop class", defenderClass = "troop class" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		local row = COUNTER_TABLE[inputs.attackerClass]
		local value = row and row[inputs.defenderClass]

		if value == nil then
			return 1
		end

		return value
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { attackerClass = "AntiTank", defenderClass = "Tanks" }, expected = 1.4285714285714286, tolerance = 1e-12 },
		{ inputs = { attackerClass = "Infantry", defenderClass = "Tanks" }, expected = 0.7, tolerance = 1e-12 },
		{ inputs = { attackerClass = "AntiTank", defenderClass = "Infantry" }, expected = 1, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "combat.attackerDamage",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "TroopCommon.CalculateStatsAgainstTroop lines 633-765",
	expression = "((attack * (1 + 0.2 E) * armyAttackCoef + armyAttackBoost) * groupAttackCoef * terrainAttackCoef * riverFactor * counter * cas * ideologyAttackCoef) * (noSupplyCombatCoef if the attacker has noSupply or noMoney); riverFactor = 1 - 0.05 - 0.3 r if r > 0",
	unit = "health removed from the defender per hit of this attacker",
	inputs = {
		baseAttack = "attack attribute",
		experience = "hidden experience E (attribute is floor(E))",
		armyAttackCoef = "Army.<Class>AttackCoef",
		armyAttackBoost = "Army.<Class>AttackBoost",
		groupAttackCoef = "army group <Class>AttackCoef (1 outside a group)",
		terrainAttackCoef = "terrainModifiers[attacker tile terrain].<Class>AttackCoef (1 when absent)",
		riverModifier = "river modifier attacker tile -> defender tile",
		counter = "combat.counter",
		casModifier = "casTroopModifier over the defender tile's air region (1 without planes)",
		ideologyAttackCoef = "attacker Army.attackBoostAgainst<defender ideology>Coef (1 when absent)",
		attackerUnsupplied = "boolean: attacker noSupply or noMoney",
		noSupplyCombatCoef = "attacker Army.noSupplyCombatCoef",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		local damage = inputs.baseAttack * (inputs.experience * EXPERIENCE_ATTACK_COEF + 1)
		damage = damage * inputs.armyAttackCoef + inputs.armyAttackBoost
		damage *= inputs.groupAttackCoef
		damage *= inputs.terrainAttackCoef

		if inputs.riverModifier > 0 then
			damage *= 1 + RIVER_ATTACK_BASE + inputs.riverModifier * RIVER_ATTACK_PER_MODIFIER
		end

		damage = damage * inputs.counter * inputs.casModifier * inputs.ideologyAttackCoef

		if inputs.attackerUnsupplied then
			damage *= inputs.noSupplyCombatCoef
		end

		return damage
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { baseAttack = 5.5, experience = 0.035, armyAttackCoef = 1.1, armyAttackBoost = 0, groupAttackCoef = 1, terrainAttackCoef = 1.25, riverModifier = 1, counter = 1, casModifier = 1, ideologyAttackCoef = 1, attackerUnsupplied = false, noSupplyCombatCoef = 0.6 }, expected = 4.950034375, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { baseAttack = 3.5, experience = 0.42, armyAttackCoef = 1, armyAttackBoost = 0, groupAttackCoef = 1, terrainAttackCoef = 1.25, riverModifier = 1, counter = 1.4285714285714286, casModifier = 1, ideologyAttackCoef = 1, attackerUnsupplied = false, noSupplyCombatCoef = 0.6 }, expected = 4.40375, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

---Run the stacked engagement round by round.
---@param attackerHealths table array of health
---@param attackerDamages table array of damage per hit (combat.attackerDamage)
---@param defenderStrengths table array of combat.defenderStrength per attacker, including the defender's no-supply factor
---@param defenderHealth number
---@return table
local function simulate(attackerHealths, attackerDamages, defenderStrengths, defenderHealth)
	local margin = defenderHealth - DEFENDER_HEALTH_FLOOR
	local healths = {}
	local active = {}
	local exhaustedRound = {}

	for index, health in ipairs(attackerHealths) do
		healths[index] = health
		if health > ATTACKER_HEALTH_FLOOR then
			table.insert(active, index)
		else
			exhaustedRound[index] = 0
		end
	end

	local delivered = 0
	local round = 0
	local breakRound = nil
	local healthsAtBreak = nil
	local lastExhaustRound = 0
	local stalled = false

	while #active > 0 do
		round += 1

		local count = #active
		local roundDamage = 0
		local roundLoss = 0

		for _, index in ipairs(active) do
			roundDamage += attackerDamages[index]
			roundLoss += defenderStrengths[index]
		end

		delivered += roundDamage

		local breaksNow = breakRound == nil and delivered >= margin

		if roundLoss <= 0 then
			stalled = true

			if breakRound == nil and roundDamage > 0 then
				breakRound = math.max(round, math.ceil(margin / roundDamage))
				healthsAtBreak = table.clone(healths)
			end

			break
		end

		local stillActive = {}
		for _, index in ipairs(active) do
			healths[index] -= defenderStrengths[index] / count

			if healths[index] <= ATTACKER_HEALTH_FLOOR then
				healths[index] = ATTACKER_HEALTH_FLOOR
				exhaustedRound[index] = round
				lastExhaustRound = round
			else
				table.insert(stillActive, index)
			end
		end

		if breaksNow then
			breakRound = round
			healthsAtBreak = table.clone(healths)
		end

		active = stillActive
	end

	local ratio
	if round == 0 then
		ratio = 0
	elseif margin <= 0 then
		ratio = math.huge
	elseif stalled then
		if breakRound ~= nil then
			ratio = math.huge
		else
			ratio = 0
		end
	else
		ratio = delivered / margin
	end

	return {
		ratio = ratio,
		breaks = breakRound ~= nil,
		breakRound = breakRound,
		healthsAtBreak = healthsAtBreak,
		tie = breakRound ~= nil and not stalled and breakRound == lastExhaustRound,
		rounds = round,
		deliveredDamage = delivered,
		defenderMargin = margin,
		exhaustedRound = exhaustedRound,
		finalHealths = healths,
	}
end

Formula.define({
	id = "combat.stackRatio",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "derived from combat.damageTaken / combat.attackerDamage and the 20 / 10 health floors (TroopCommon:783-785)",
	expression = "delivered damage of the stack before every attacker reaches 20 (attacker i takes defenderStrength_i / activeAttackers per round) / (defenderHealth - 10)",
	unit = "ratio; >= 1 means the defender is beaten before the stack is spent",
	inputs = {
		attackerHealths = "array of attacker health",
		attackerDamages = "array of combat.attackerDamage per attacker",
		defenderStrengths = "array of combat.defenderStrength per attacker with the defender's no-supply factor",
		defenderHealth = "defender health",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		return simulate(inputs.attackerHealths, inputs.attackerDamages, inputs.defenderStrengths, inputs.defenderHealth).ratio
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { attackerHealths = { 100 }, attackerDamages = { 5 }, defenderStrengths = { 6.5 }, defenderHealth = 100 }, expected = 65 / 90, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { attackerHealths = { 100, 100 }, attackerDamages = { 5, 5 }, defenderStrengths = { 6.5, 6.5 }, defenderHealth = 100 }, expected = 250 / 90, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { attackerHealths = { 60 }, attackerDamages = { 3 }, defenderStrengths = { 6 }, defenderHealth = 10 }, expected = math.huge },
	},
})

---Counter multiplier (traced twin of the combat.counter formula for tools and tests).
---@param attackerClass string
---@param defenderClass string
---@return number
function CombatFormulas.counter(attackerClass, defenderClass)
	return Formula.evaluate("combat.counter", { attackerClass = attackerClass, defenderClass = defenderClass })
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Combat.CombatFormulas", CombatFormulas)

---Full engagement timeline for a stack (same arithmetic as combat.stackRatio, which is evaluated
---and logged for the ratio).
---@param attackerHealths table
---@param attackerDamages table
---@param defenderStrengths table
---@param defenderHealth number
---@return table { ratio, breaks, breakRound, healthsAtBreak, tie, rounds, deliveredDamage, defenderMargin, exhaustedRound, finalHealths }
function CombatFormulas.engagement(attackerHealths, attackerDamages, defenderStrengths, defenderHealth)
	local result = simulate(attackerHealths, attackerDamages, defenderStrengths, defenderHealth)

	result.ratio = Formula.evaluate("combat.stackRatio", {
		attackerHealths = attackerHealths,
		attackerDamages = attackerDamages,
		defenderStrengths = defenderStrengths,
		defenderHealth = defenderHealth,
	})

	return result
end

return CombatFormulas

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Combat/CombatModel"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Combat/CombatModel

	Battle prediction on the live world. Reads every modifier the game's combat code reads and
	feeds them to the declared formulas in Features/Combat/CombatFormulas.

	=== HOTFIX-1 (2026-09-29) ===
	[Sprint-3] engage() cache was merged badly: a duplicate function header, a stray "..." and an
	orphaned final block prevented the module from compiling. The cache key + memo + calibrated
	breakSeconds are all kept; the body is now a single function.
]]

local CombatModel = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Stats
local Stats = require("Utility/Stats")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatFormulas
local CombatFormulas = require("Features/Combat/CombatFormulas")

-- Constants.
local RECORDED_HIT_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 1.0494
local ABSENT_COEFFICIENT = 1
local ABSENT_BOOST = 0
local ABSENT_FORT = 0
local EXPERIENCE_UPPER_BOUND_OFFSET = 1
local STATES_THAT_CANNOT_START_AN_ATTACK = { Defending = true, Retreating = true, Dead = true }

-- State.
CombatModel.measuredHitIntervals = {}
-- [SPRINT-3] Bias kalibrasi: log memperlihatkan actual break time ~20% lebih cepat
-- dari prediksi karena interval hit server-side lebih pendek saat CAS/experience.
-- Nilai ini dipakai untuk menyesuaikan breakSeconds yang dipakai decision layer.
CombatModel.breakTimeBias = 1.0
CombatModel.biasSamples = {}
CombatModel.biasCap = { min = 0.7, max = 1.3 }

---A new per-cycle context.
---@param world table the cycle's world view (Features/Front/WorldView)
---@return table
function CombatModel.newContext(world)
	return {
		world = world,
		countries = {},
		groups = {},
		fortWarned = {},
		-- [SPRINT-3] Engage cache untuk cycle ini.
		engageCache = {},
	}
end

---Seconds between two hits of one attacker: live median when measured, else the recorded median.
---@return number, string
function CombatModel.hitIntervalSeconds()
	local samples = CombatModel.measuredHitIntervals

	if #samples == 0 then
		return RECORDED_HIT_INTERVAL_SECONDS, "recorded median (ground-combat.md §3.3)"
	end

	return Stats.median(samples), string.format("live median of %d hit intervals", #samples)
end

---[SPRINT-3] Catat break error dari Scorecard untuk kalibrasi.
---@param predicted number
---@param actual number
function CombatModel.recordBreakOutcome(predicted, actual)
	if predicted == nil or predicted <= 0 or actual <= 0 then return end
	local ratio = actual / predicted
	table.insert(CombatModel.biasSamples, ratio)
	-- Window 20 sample terakhir.
	if #CombatModel.biasSamples > 20 then
		table.remove(CombatModel.biasSamples, 1)
	end
	-- Median sebagai bias.
	local sorted = table.clone(CombatModel.biasSamples)
	table.sort(sorted)
	local n = #sorted
	local med
	if n == 0 then
		med = 1.0
	elseif n % 2 == 1 then
		med = sorted[(n + 1) // 2]
	else
		med = (sorted[n // 2] + sorted[n // 2 + 1]) * 0.5
	end
	-- Clamp.
	if med < CombatModel.biasCap.min then med = CombatModel.biasCap.min end
	if med > CombatModel.biasCap.max then med = CombatModel.biasCap.max end
	CombatModel.breakTimeBias = med
	if n >= 3 then
		Logger.debug("Features.Combat.CombatModel", "biasUpdated", {
			samples = n, medianRatio = med, rawSamples = sorted,
		})
	end
end

---Cached country modifiers for one cycle.
---@param context table
---@param countryName string
---@return table
local function countryInfo(context, countryName)
	local info = context.countries[countryName]

	if info ~= nil then
		return info
	end

	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)

	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("CombatModel: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end

	local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
	local techs = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Techs")
	local government = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Government")

	info = {
		name = countryName,
		instance = country,
		army = army,
		ideology = Resolver.requireAttribute(government, "ideology", "string"),
		noSupplyCombatCoef = Resolver.requireAttribute(army, "noSupplyCombatCoef", "number"),
		cityDefensesStrengthModifier = Resolver.optionalAttribute(techs, "cityDefensesStrengthModifier", "number", "TroopCommon.lua:515 reads it with default 1") or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT,
		tileFortDefenseModifier = Resolver.optionalAttribute(techs, "tileFortDefenseModifier", "number", "TroopCommon.lua:412 reads it with default 1") or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT,
		cities = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Cities"),
		allies = Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Allies"),
		values = {},
	}

	context.countries[countryName] = info

	return info
end

---An Army attribute with the game's own default when absent.
---@param info table
---@param attributeName string
---@param absentValue number
---@return number
local function armyValue(info, attributeName, absentValue)
	local value = info.values[attributeName]

	if value == nil then
		local read = info.army:GetAttribute(attributeName)

		if read == nil then
			value = absentValue
		elseif type(read) ~= "number" then
			return error(string.format("CombatModel: %s.Army.%s is %s, expected number", info.name, attributeName, typeof(read)))
		else
			value = read
		end

		info.values[attributeName] = value
	end

	return value
end

---Army group coefficient of a troop (1 outside a group or when the group lacks it).
---@param context table
---@param info table
---@param armyName string?
---@param attributeName string
---@return number
local function groupValue(context, info, armyName, attributeName)
	if armyName == nil then
		return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	end

	local key = info.name .. "|" .. armyName .. "|" .. attributeName
	local value = context.groups[key]

	if value ~= nil then
		return value
	end

	local armies = info.army:FindFirstChild("Armies")
	local group = armies and armies:FindFirstChild(armyName)
	local read = group and group:GetAttribute(attributeName)

	if read == nil then
		value = ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	elseif type(read) ~= "number" then
		return error(string.format("CombatModel: army group %s/%s attribute %s is %s", info.name, armyName, attributeName, typeof(read)))
	else
		value = read
	end

	context.groups[key] = value

	return value
end

---Terrain modifier (ArmyCommon.terrainModifiers; absent = 1).
---@param terrain string?
---@param key string
---@return number
local function terrainValue(terrain, key)
	if terrain == nil then
		return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	end

	local modifiers = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").terrainModifiers[terrain]
	local value = modifiers and modifiers[key]

	if value == nil then
		return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	end

	return value
end

---City defence of a defender's country on a tile (TroopCommon.lua:512-522).
---@param info table defender's country
---@param tileName string
---@return number
local function cityDefenseOn(info, tileName)
	local cityName = MapGraph.tiles[tileName].cityName

	if cityName == nil then
		return 0
	end

	local cityFolder = info.cities:FindFirstChild(cityName)

	if cityFolder == nil then
		return 0
	end

	return Resolver.requireAttribute(cityFolder, "defenses", "number") * info.cityDefensesStrengthModifier
end

---Bunker factor input for one prediction (see the module header).
---@param context table
---@param tileName string
---@param defenderInfo table
---@param defenderIsOurs boolean
---@return number fortDefense to feed combat.defenderStrength
local function fortOn(context, tileName, defenderInfo, defenderIsOurs)
	local fort = MapGraph.tiles[tileName].instance:GetAttribute("fortDefense")

	if fort == nil or fort == ABSENT_FORT then
		return ABSENT_FORT
	end

	if type(fort) ~= "number" then
		return error(string.format("CombatModel: tile %s fortDefense is %s", tileName, typeof(fort)))
	end

	local withModifier = fort * defenderInfo.tileFortDefenseModifier
	local chosen
	if defenderIsOurs then
		chosen = math.min(fort, withModifier)
	else
		chosen = math.max(fort, withModifier)
	end

	if not context.fortWarned[tileName] then
		context.fortWarned[tileName] = true
		Logger.warn("Features.Combat.CombatModel", "fortInPrediction", {
			tile = tileName,
			fortDefense = fort,
			tileFortDefenseModifier = defenderInfo.tileFortDefenseModifier,
			used = chosen,
			defenderIsOurs = defenderIsOurs,
			reason = "bunker effect never observed (OQ3); the pessimistic client formula is used",
		})
	end

	return chosen
end

---Close air support multiplier over the defender tile's region for an attacking country
---(TroopCommon.lua:718-737).
---@param attackerInfo table
---@param defenderTileName string
---@return number
local function casOn(attackerInfo, defenderTileName)
	local regionName = MapGraph.tiles[defenderTileName].airRegion
	local airMap = workspace:FindFirstChild("AirMap")

	if regionName == nil or airMap == nil then
		return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	end

	local region = airMap:FindFirstChild(regionName)

	if region == nil then
		return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	end

	local own = region:FindFirstChild(attackerInfo.name)
	local modifier = own and (own:GetAttribute("casTroopModifier") or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT) or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT

	for allyName in pairs(attackerInfo.allies) do
		local allyDeployment = region:FindFirstChild(allyName)

		if allyDeployment ~= nil then
			local allyModifier = allyDeployment:GetAttribute("casTroopModifier") or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
			if allyModifier > modifier then
				modifier = allyModifier
			end
		end
	end

	return modifier
end

---Per-hit numbers of one attacker against one defender.
---@param context table
---@param attacker table unit snapshot (Game/Units.snapshot) or a hypothetical copy
---@param fromTile string tile the attacker attacks from
---@param defender table unit snapshot
---@param onTile string defender's tile
---@param ourSide string "attacker" when the attacker is ours, "defender" when the defender is ours, "neither"
---@return number damageDealt, number defenderStrength (with the defender's no-supply factor)
local function hitNumbers(context, attacker, fromTile, defender, onTile, ourSide)
	local attackerInfo = countryInfo(context, attacker.owner)
	local defenderInfo = countryInfo(context, defender.owner)
	local river = MapGraph.edgeRiver(fromTile, onTile)
	local defenderClass = defender.class
	local attackerClass = attacker.class

	local strength = Formula.evaluate("combat.defenderStrength", {
		baseDefense = defender.defense,
		cityDefense = cityDefenseOn(defenderInfo, onTile),
		fortDefense = fortOn(context, onTile, defenderInfo, ourSide == "defender"),
		armyDefenseCoef = armyValue(defenderInfo, defenderClass .. "DefenseCoef", ABSENT_COEFFICIENT),
		armyDefenseBoost = armyValue(defenderInfo, defenderClass .. "DefenseBoost", ABSENT_BOOST),
		groupDefenseCoef = groupValue(context, defenderInfo, defender.army, defenderClass .. "DefenseCoef"),
		terrainDefenseCoef = terrainValue(MapGraph.tiles[onTile].terrain, defenderClass .. "DefenseCoef"),
		riverModifier = river,
		ideologyDefenseCoef = armyValue(defenderInfo, "attackBoostAgainst" .. attackerInfo.ideology .. "Coef", ABSENT_COEFFICIENT),
	})

	if defender.noSupply or defender.noMoney then
		strength *= defenderInfo.noSupplyCombatCoef
	end

	local experience = attacker.experience
	if ourSide ~= "attacker" then
		experience += EXPERIENCE_UPPER_BOUND_OFFSET
	end

	local cas = ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	if ourSide ~= "attacker" then
		cas = casOn(attackerInfo, onTile)
	end

	local damage = Formula.evaluate("combat.attackerDamage", {
		baseAttack = attacker.attack,
		experience = experience,
		armyAttackCoef = armyValue(attackerInfo, attackerClass .. "AttackCoef", ABSENT_COEFFICIENT),
		armyAttackBoost = armyValue(attackerInfo, attackerClass .. "AttackBoost", ABSENT_BOOST),
		groupAttackCoef = groupValue(context, attackerInfo, attacker.army, attackerClass .. "AttackCoef"),
		terrainAttackCoef = terrainValue(MapGraph.tiles[fromTile].terrain, attackerClass .. "AttackCoef"),
		riverModifier = river,
		counter = Formula.evaluate("combat.counter", { attackerClass = attackerClass, defenderClass = defenderClass }),
		casModifier = cas,
		ideologyAttackCoef = armyValue(attackerInfo, "attackBoostAgainst" .. defenderInfo.ideology .. "Coef", ABSENT_COEFFICIENT),
		attackerUnsupplied = attacker.noSupply or attacker.noMoney,
		noSupplyCombatCoef = attackerInfo.noSupplyCombatCoef,
	})

	return damage, strength
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Combat.CombatModel", CombatModel)

---Predict a stack's fight against one defender.
---@param context table
---@param attackers table array of { unit = snapshot, fromTile = name }
---@param defender table snapshot of the defender
---@param defenderTile string
---@param ourSide string "attacker" | "defender" | "neither"
---@return table engagement result plus { breakSeconds, attackers = the input list, hitIntervalSource }
function CombatModel.engage(context, attackers, defender, defenderTile, ourSide)
	-- [SPRINT-3] Cache key: attacker ids + defender id + defender health bucket.
	local keyParts = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(attackers) do
		table.insert(keyParts, tostring(entry.unit.id))
	end
	table.insert(keyParts, tostring(defender.id))
	table.insert(keyParts, tostring(math.floor((defender.health or 100) / 5)))
	table.insert(keyParts, ourSide)
	local cacheKey = table.concat(keyParts, ",")
	local cached = context.engageCache and context.engageCache[cacheKey]
	if cached ~= nil then
		return cached
	end

	local healths = {}
	local damages = {}
	local strengths = {}

	for index, entry in ipairs(attackers) do
		local damage, strength = hitNumbers(context, entry.unit, entry.fromTile, defender, defenderTile, ourSide)
		healths[index] = entry.unit.health
		damages[index] = damage
		strengths[index] = strength
	end

	local result = CombatFormulas.engagement(healths, damages, strengths, defender.health)
	local interval, intervalSource = CombatModel.hitIntervalSeconds()

	-- [SPRINT-3] Break time dikalibrasi oleh bias.
	if result.breakRound then
		result.breakSeconds = result.breakRound * interval * CombatModel.breakTimeBias
	else
		result.breakSeconds = nil
	end
	result.hitIntervalSeconds = interval
	result.hitIntervalSource = intervalSource
	result.damages = damages
	result.strengths = strengths
	result.attackers = attackers

	-- [SPRINT-3] Simpan ke cache.
	if context.engageCache ~= nil then
		context.engageCache[cacheKey] = result
	end

	return result
end

---Enemy units that could attack a tile now.
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@param hostileCountries table set of country names hostile to the defender
---@return table array of { unit = snapshot, fromTile = name }
function CombatModel.potentialAttackers(world, tileName, hostileCountries)
	local attackers = {}

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

		if unit ~= nil and hostileCountries[unit.owner] then
			local alreadyAttacking = unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile == tileName

			if alreadyAttacking or not STATES_THAT_CANNOT_START_AN_ATTACK[unit.state] then
				table.insert(attackers, { unit = unit, fromTile = neighbourName })
			end
		end
	end

	return attackers
end

---Threat on one of our tiles: the worst stack of adjacent enemy units against the defender that
---is (or would be) on it.
---@param context table
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@param defender table our unit snapshot placed on tileName (may be hypothetical)
---@return table engagement result with .attackers; ratio 0 when no enemy can attack
function CombatModel.threat(context, world, tileName, defender)
	local attackers = CombatModel.potentialAttackers(world, tileName, world.enemies)

	if #attackers == 0 then
		return { ratio = 0, breaks = false, tie = false, attackers = attackers }
	end

	return CombatModel.engage(context, attackers, defender, tileName, "defender")
end

---A copy of a unit snapshot placed on another tile (for hypothetical placements).
---@param unit table
---@param tileName string
---@return table
function CombatModel.placedOn(unit, tileName)
	local copy = table.clone(unit)
	copy.tile = tileName
	return copy
end

return CombatModel

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Core/AntiIdle"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Core/AntiIdle

	Roblox disconnects a client after 20 minutes without input (error 278), even while its country
	is at war; replication then freezes (recording B, war-and-loop.md Summary 12). When the engine
	reports the player idle (LocalPlayer.Idled), a virtual right click answers it.
]]

local AntiIdle = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.Session
local Session = require("Game/Session")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

-- Services.
local virtualUser = game:GetService("VirtualUser")

-- State.
AntiIdle.answers = 0

---Answer the engine's idle notice.
---@param maid table
function AntiIdle.attach(maid)
	maid:give(Session.localPlayer.Idled:Connect(function(idleSeconds)
		if not Settings.get("antiIdle.enabled") then
			Logger.warn("Features.Core.AntiIdle", "idleIgnored", { idleSeconds = idleSeconds, reason = "antiIdle.enabled is off" })
			return
		end

		virtualUser:CaptureController()
		virtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
		AntiIdle.answers += 1

		Logger.info("Features.Core.AntiIdle", "idleAnswered", { idleSeconds = idleSeconds, answers = AntiIdle.answers })
	end))

	Logger.info("Features.Core.AntiIdle", "attached", {})
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Core.AntiIdle", AntiIdle)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Core/Guard"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Core/Guard

	The act-or-stop gate (DESIGN §3.1). The AI plans only while every condition holds and sends a
	remote only while, in addition, the user has switched sending on (dry-run first, DESIGN §10).
]]

local Guard = {}

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.Session
local Session = require("Game/Session")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

-- State.
Guard.lastVerdict = nil

---Evaluate the gate.
---@return table { canPlan, canSend, reason, country }
function Guard.check()
	local verdict = { canPlan = false, canSend = false, reason = nil, country = nil }

	if not Session.isCorrectPlace() then
		verdict.reason = string.format("not Conquer The World WW2 (PlaceId %s)", tostring(game.PlaceId))
	elseif not Session.replicatorPresent() then
		verdict.reason = "client disconnected (no ClientReplicator)"
	else
		local fresh, freshness = Tick.isFresh()

		if not fresh then
			verdict.reason = freshness
		else
			local country, countryReason = Session.ourCountry()

			if country == nil then
				verdict.reason = countryReason
			elseif not Settings.get("ai.enabled") then
				verdict.reason = "AI switched off (ai.enabled)"
				verdict.country = country
			else
				verdict.canPlan = true
				verdict.country = country
				verdict.canSend = Settings.get("ai.sendOrders") == true

				if verdict.canSend then
					verdict.reason = "planning and sending"
				else
					verdict.reason = "dry-run: planning, orders logged but not sent (ai.sendOrders off)"
				end
			end
		end
	end

	Guard.lastVerdict = verdict

	return verdict
end

---True when a remote may be sent right now (re-evaluates the gate).
---@return boolean, string
function Guard.canSend()
	local verdict = Guard.check()
	return verdict.canSend, verdict.reason
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Core.Guard", Guard)

]=]

-- >>> PART 2/3 START

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Core/Scheduler"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Core/Scheduler

	Decides when the AI thinks (DESIGN §3.3, user requirement: combat reactions are instant).
	- Combat cycles run in the same frame as the event that needs them.
	- Tick cycles run on each detected day tick.
	Each cycle runs under its own trace id; its cost goes to the Profiler.
]]

local Scheduler = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Profiler
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Stats
local Stats = require("Utility/Stats")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Game.Units
local Units = require("Game/Units")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Core.Guard
local Guard = require("Features/Core/Guard")

---@module Features.Front.WorldView
local WorldView = require("Features/Front/WorldView")

---@module Features.Front.FrontPlanner
local FrontPlanner = require("Features/Front/FrontPlanner")

---@module Features.Orders.OrderExecutor
local OrderExecutor = require("Features/Orders/OrderExecutor")

-- Constants.
local EMPTY_PATH_JSON = "[]"

-- State.
Scheduler.hooks = {}
Scheduler.scheduled = false
Scheduler.tickPending = nil
Scheduler.combatTriggers = {}
Scheduler.lastStates = {}
Scheduler.cycleCount = 0
Scheduler.lastCycle = nil
Scheduler.reactionLatencies = {}
Scheduler.running = false
Scheduler.context = { capitalTile = nil, enemies = {} }

---True when a land tile is ours or touches ours.
---@param tileName string
---@param me string
---@return boolean
local function nearOurLand(tileName, me)
	if Ownership.tileOwner[tileName] == me then
		return true
	end

	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]

	if record == nil then
		return false
	end

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
		if Ownership.tileOwner[neighbourName] == me then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

---Run the pending cycle.
local function runPending()
	Scheduler.scheduled = false

	if Scheduler.running then
		Scheduler.scheduled = true
		task.defer(runPending)
		return
	end

	local tickInfo = Scheduler.tickPending
	local triggers = Scheduler.combatTriggers
	Scheduler.tickPending = nil
	Scheduler.combatTriggers = {}

	local kind = "combat"
	if tickInfo ~= nil then
		kind = "tick"
	elseif #triggers == 0 then
		return
	end

	Scheduler.running = true
	Scheduler.cycleCount += 1

	local label = kind .. "_" .. tostring(Scheduler.cycleCount)

	local cycleOk, failure = pcall(Tracer.withTrace, "cycle." .. kind, function()
		local traceId = Tracer.context()
		local verdict = Guard.check()

		if Scheduler.hooks.onVerdict ~= nil then
			Scheduler.hooks.onVerdict(verdict)
		end

		if not verdict.canPlan or not Settings.get("front.enabled") then
			Logger.debug("Features.Core.Scheduler", "cycleSkipped", { kind = kind, reason = verdict.reason })
			Formula.flushSuppressed(traceId)
			return
		end

		local world = Profiler.run("Cycle_WorldView", WorldView.build, verdict.country, label)
world.tickNumber = (tickInfo ~= nil and tickInfo.tickNumber) or 0
		world.cycleKind = kind
		Scheduler.context = { capitalTile = world.capitalTile, enemies = world.enemies }
		OrderExecutor.pruneCommitments(world)

		local plan, analysis = Profiler.run("Cycle_FrontPlan", FrontPlanner.plan, world, OrderExecutor.blockedUnits(), OrderExecutor.commitments(), kind)
		local summary = Profiler.run("Cycle_Execute", OrderExecutor.execute, world, plan, verdict.canSend)

		if #triggers > 0 then
			local earliest = math.huge
			local names = {}

			for _, trigger in ipairs(triggers) do
				earliest = math.min(earliest, trigger.clock)
				names[trigger.event] = (names[trigger.event] or 0) + 1
			end

			local latency = os.clock() - earliest
			table.insert(Scheduler.reactionLatencies, latency)

			Logger.info("Features.Core.Scheduler", "reaction", {
				kind = kind,
				triggers = names,
				firstTrigger = triggers[1],
				latencySeconds = latency,
				orders = summary,
			})
		end

		if kind == "tick" and Scheduler.hooks.onTick ~= nil then
			Profiler.run("Cycle_TickHooks", Scheduler.hooks.onTick, world, analysis, plan)
		end

		if kind == "tick" then
			Formula.flushSuppressed("none")
		end

		Scheduler.lastCycle = {
			kind = kind,
			label = label,
			plan = plan:summary(),
			orders = summary,
			serverTime = world.serverTime,
		}

		if Scheduler.hooks.onPlanned ~= nil then
			Scheduler.hooks.onPlanned(world, plan, summary)
		end

		Formula.flushSuppressed(traceId)
	end)

	Scheduler.running = false

	if not cycleOk then
		Logger.warn("Features.Core.Scheduler", "cycleAborted", { kind = kind, label = label, message = tostring(failure) })
	end
end

---Ask for a cycle at the end of this frame.
local function schedule()
	if not Scheduler.scheduled then
		Scheduler.scheduled = true
		task.defer(runPending)
	end
end

---Queue a combat trigger (hot path, untraced twin of Scheduler.requestCombat).
---@param event string
---@param tileName string?
local function requestCombat(event, tileName)
	table.insert(Scheduler.combatTriggers, { event = event, tile = tileName, clock = os.clock(), serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() })
	schedule()
end

---Request a tick cycle.
---@param info table
function Scheduler.requestTick(info)
	Scheduler.tickPending = info
	schedule()
end

---Request a combat cycle for an event (hot path: called from world signals).
---@param event string
---@param tileName string?
function Scheduler.requestCombat(event, tileName)
	requestCombat(event, tileName)
end

---True when a unit has no tiles of an order left (currentPath absent or "[]").
---@param record table
---@return boolean
local function pathFinished(record)
	local remaining = record.model:GetAttribute("currentPath")
	return remaining == nil or remaining == EMPTY_PATH_JSON
end

---Classify a unit attribute change into a combat trigger. Hot path.
---@param record table
---@param attributeName string
---@param value any
local function onUnitChanged(record, attributeName, value)
	local me = Units.ourCountryName

	if me == nil then
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "state" then
		local previous = Scheduler.lastStates[record]
		Scheduler.lastStates[record] = value

		local ours = record.owner == me
		local hostile = not ours and record.owner ~= nil and Scheduler.context.enemies[record.owner] == true

		if ours then
			if value == "Retreating" then
				requestCombat("ourRetreat", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Dead" then
				requestCombat("ourDeath", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Idle" and previous == "Waiting" then
				requestCombat("orderDropped", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Idle" and previous ~= nil and previous ~= "Idle" and pathFinished(record) then
				requestCombat("ourUnitIdle", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Defending" then
				requestCombat("ourUnitAttacked", record.tile)
			end
		elseif hostile and nearOurLand(record.tile, me) then
			if value == "Attacking" then
				requestCombat("enemyAttack", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Retreating" then
				requestCombat("enemyRetreat", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Dead" then
				requestCombat("enemyDeath", record.tile)
			end
		end
	elseif attributeName == "dieOnNextRetreat" and value == true and Scheduler.context.enemies[record.owner] and nearOurLand(record.tile, me) then
		requestCombat("enemyFlagged", record.tile)
	end
end

---Connect the triggers.
---@param maid table
---@param hooks table { onTick(world, analysis, plan), onPlanned(world, plan, summary), onVerdict(verdict) }
function Scheduler.attach(maid, hooks)
	Scheduler.hooks = hooks

	maid:give(Tick.dayTick:connect(function(info)
		Scheduler.requestTick(info)
	end))

	maid:give(Units.unitChanged:connect(onUnitChanged))

	maid:give(Units.unitMoved:connect(function(record, fromTile, toTile)
		local me = Units.ourCountryName

		if me == nil then
			return
		end

		if record.owner == me then
			if fromTile == Scheduler.context.capitalTile then
				requestCombat("capitalLeft", fromTile)
			end
		elseif Scheduler.context.enemies[record.owner] and nearOurLand(toTile, me) then
			requestCombat("enemyAdvance", toTile)
		end
	end))

	maid:give(Units.unitAdded:connect(function(record)
		local me = Units.ourCountryName

		if me ~= nil and Scheduler.context.enemies[record.owner] and nearOurLand(record.tile, me) then
			requestCombat("enemyAppeared", record.tile)
		end
	end))

	maid:give(Units.unitRemoved:connect(function(record, tileName)
		local me = Units.ourCountryName
		Scheduler.lastStates[record] = nil

		if me == nil or record.superseded then
			return
		end

		if tileName == Scheduler.context.capitalTile then
			requestCombat("capitalEmptied", tileName)
		elseif nearOurLand(tileName, me) then
			requestCombat("unitGone", tileName)
		end
	end))

	maid:give(Ownership.ownerChanged:connect(function(tileName, previous, current)
		local me = Units.ourCountryName

		if me ~= nil and previous == me then
			requestCombat("tileLost", tileName)
		end
	end))

	for _, key in ipairs({ "ai.enabled", "ai.sendOrders", "front.enabled", "front.attack" }) do
		maid:give(Settings.onChanged(key, function()
			Scheduler.requestTick({ reason = "setting " .. key })
		end))
	end

	Logger.info("Features.Core.Scheduler", "attached", {})
end

---Refresh the event filters' facts for our country (attach, country change).
---@param countryName string?
function Scheduler.refreshContext(countryName)
	if countryName == nil or not Ownership.isAlive(countryName) then
		Scheduler.context = { capitalTile = nil, enemies = {} }
		return
	end

	Scheduler.context = {
		capitalTile = Ownership.capitalTile(countryName),
		enemies = Ownership.enemiesOf(countryName),
	}
end

---Latency statistics of combat reactions (seconds from the first trigger to the orders).
---@return table
function Scheduler.latencyStats()
	local samples = Scheduler.reactionLatencies

	if #samples == 0 then
		return { count = 0 }
	end

	return {
		count = #samples,
		median = Stats.median(samples),
		max = Stats.max(samples),
	}
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Core.Scheduler", Scheduler)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Core/Settings"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Core/Settings

	The registry of every user-adjustable value (derivation tier T4).
]]

local Settings = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

-- Constants.
local SUPPORTED_KINDS = { toggle = true, slider = true, dropdown = true }

-- State.
Settings.definitions = {}
Settings.byKey = {}
Settings.values = {}
Settings.listeners = {}

---Declare a setting.
---@param definition table
---@return table
function Settings.define(definition)
	assert(type(definition.key) == "string", "Settings.define: key is required")
	assert(Settings.byKey[definition.key] == nil, string.format("Settings.define: '%s' is already defined", tostring(definition.key)))
	assert(type(definition.tab) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): tab is required", definition.key))
	assert(type(definition.section) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): section is required", definition.key))
	assert(type(definition.label) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): label is required", definition.key))
	assert(SUPPORTED_KINDS[definition.kind], string.format("Settings.define(%s): unsupported kind '%s'", definition.key, tostring(definition.kind)))
	assert(type(definition.unit) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): unit is required", definition.key))
	assert(definition.default ~= nil, string.format("Settings.define(%s): default is required", definition.key))
	assert(type(definition.defaultSource) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): defaultSource (provenance of the default) is required", definition.key))
	assert(type(definition.effect) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): effect (what it changes) is required", definition.key))

	if definition.kind == "slider" then
		assert(type(definition.min) == "number" and type(definition.max) == "number", string.format("Settings.define(%s): slider needs min and max", definition.key))
		assert(type(definition.rounding) == "number", string.format("Settings.define(%s): slider needs rounding (decimal places)", definition.key))
		assert(definition.default >= definition.min and definition.default <= definition.max, string.format("Settings.define(%s): default outside [min, max]", definition.key))
	end

	if definition.kind == "dropdown" then
		assert(type(definition.choices) == "table" and #definition.choices > 0, string.format("Settings.define(%s): dropdown needs choices", definition.key))
		assert(table.find(definition.choices, definition.default), string.format("Settings.define(%s): default is not one of the choices", definition.key))
	end

	if definition.kind == "toggle" then
		assert(type(definition.default) == "boolean", string.format("Settings.define(%s): toggle default must be boolean", definition.key))
	end

	table.insert(Settings.definitions, definition)
	Settings.byKey[definition.key] = definition
	Settings.values[definition.key] = definition.default
	Settings.listeners[definition.key] = {}

	Logger.debug("Features.Core.Settings", "defined", {
		key = definition.key,
		kind = definition.kind,
		unit = definition.unit,
		default = definition.default,
		defaultSource = definition.defaultSource,
		min = definition.min,
		max = definition.max,
		choices = definition.choices,
	})

	return definition
end

---Read a setting. Unknown keys raise.
---@param key string
---@return any
function Settings.get(key)
	if Settings.byKey[key] == nil then
		return error(string.format("Settings.get: unknown setting '%s'", tostring(key)))
	end

	return Settings.values[key]
end

---Validate a candidate value against a definition.
---@param definition table
---@param value any
---@return string?
function Settings.validate(definition, value)
	if definition.kind == "toggle" and type(value) ~= "boolean" then
		return "toggle value must be boolean"
	end

	if definition.kind == "slider" then
		if type(value) ~= "number" then
			return "slider value must be a number"
		end

		if value < definition.min or value > definition.max then
			return string.format("slider value %s outside [%s, %s]", tostring(value), tostring(definition.min), tostring(definition.max))
		end
	end

	if definition.kind == "dropdown" and not table.find(definition.choices, value) then
		return string.format("'%s' is not one of the choices", tostring(value))
	end

	return nil
end

---Change a setting, validate it, log the change with its origin, and notify listeners.
---@param key string
---@param value any
---@param origin string
function Settings.set(key, value, origin)
	local definition = Settings.byKey[key]

	if definition == nil then
		return error(string.format("Settings.set: unknown setting '%s'", tostring(key)))
	end

	local rejection = Settings.validate(definition, value)
	if rejection ~= nil then
		return error(string.format("Settings.set(%s): %s", key, rejection))
	end

	local previous = Settings.values[key]
	if previous == value then
		return
	end

	Settings.values[key] = value

	Logger.info("Features.Core.Settings", "changed", {
		key = key,
		previous = previous,
		current = value,
		unit = definition.unit,
		origin = origin,
	})

	for _, listener in ipairs(Settings.listeners[key]) do
		listener(value, previous)
	end
end

---Register a listener for one setting.
---@param key string
---@param listener function
---@return function
function Settings.onChanged(key, listener)
	local listeners = Settings.listeners[key]

	if listeners == nil then
		return error(string.format("Settings.onChanged: unknown setting '%s'", tostring(key)))
	end

	table.insert(listeners, listener)

	return function()
		local index = table.find(listeners, listener)
		if index ~= nil then
			table.remove(listeners, index)
		end
	end
end

---Definitions grouped by tab, then by section.
---@return table array of { name, sections = array of { name, definitions } }
function Settings.layout()
	local tabList = {}
	local tabByName = {}

	for _, definition in ipairs(Settings.definitions) do
		local tab = tabByName[definition.tab]

		if tab == nil then
			tab = { name = definition.tab, sections = {}, sectionByName = {} }
			tabByName[definition.tab] = tab
			table.insert(tabList, tab)
		end

		local section = tab.sectionByName[definition.section]

		if section == nil then
			section = { name = definition.section, definitions = {} }
			tab.sectionByName[definition.section] = section
			table.insert(tab.sections, section)
		end

		table.insert(section.definitions, definition)
	end

	return tabList
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Core.Settings", Settings)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Core/SettingsCatalog"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Core/SettingsCatalog
	- Tab Debug dihapus.

local SettingsCatalog = {}
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

local SWITCHES = {

	{ key = "economy.enabled",         tab = "AI", section = "Economy",      label = "Economy",                     default = true, defaultSource = "auto-enabled by default", effect = "Production split, training, manpower, city defences." },

	{ key = "focus.enabled",           tab = "AI", section = "Economy",      label = "National focus",              default = true, defaultSource = "auto-enabled by default", effect = "Starts focuses, keeps a PP reserve for the user." },

	-- Navy and air: ON
	{ key = "navy.enabled",            tab = "AI", section = "Navy and air", label = "Navy",                        default = true, defaultSource = "auto-enabled by default", effect = "Watches transports, docks damaged fleets." },
	{ key = "navy.invasions",          tab = "AI", section = "Navy and air", label = "Naval invasions",             default = true, defaultSource = "auto-enabled by default", effect = "Reserved: v1 plans no invasions." },
	{ key = "air.enabled",             tab = "AI", section = "Navy and air", label = "Air",                         default = true, defaultSource = "auto-enabled by default", effect = "Deploys CAS, retreats from lost air battles." },

	-- Client: ON
	{ key = "antiIdle.enabled",        tab = "AI", section = "Client",       label = "Prevent idle kick",           default = true, defaultSource = "auto-enabled by default", effect = "Answers Roblox's idle notice with a virtual click." },
}

function SettingsCatalog.defineAll()
	for _, switch in ipairs(SWITCHES) do
		Settings.define({
			key = switch.key,
			tab = switch.tab,
			section = switch.section,
			label = switch.label,
			kind = "toggle",
			unit = "on/off",
			default = switch.default,
			defaultSource = switch.defaultSource,
			effect = switch.effect,
		})
	end
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Core.SettingsCatalog", SettingsCatalog)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/EconomyFormulas"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/EconomyFormulas

	Declared economy arithmetic (economy.md §3, REFERENCE R10, R13, R15).
]]

local EconomyFormulas = {}

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.factoryPrice",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "CityInfoLocalScript:288 mirror, matched on 19/19 (rec A) and 104/104 (rec B) purchases (economy.md §3.4)",
	expression = "factoryPrice * (1 + (cityFactories + cityQueue) * factoryPriceMultiplier + (countryFactories + allQueued) * factoryTotalPriceMultiplier)",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = {
		factoryPrice = "Techs.factoryPrice",
		cityFactories = "city factories",
		cityQueue = "city factoryBuildQueue",
		factoryPriceMultiplier = "Techs.factoryPriceMultiplier",
		countryFactories = "country factories",
		allQueued = "sum of factoryBuildQueue over the country's cities",
		factoryTotalPriceMultiplier = "Techs.factoryTotalPriceMultiplier",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.factoryPrice * (1 + (inputs.cityFactories + inputs.cityQueue) * inputs.factoryPriceMultiplier + (inputs.countryFactories + inputs.allQueued) * inputs.factoryTotalPriceMultiplier)
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { factoryPrice = 1000, cityFactories = 0, cityQueue = 0, factoryPriceMultiplier = 0.25, countryFactories = 0, allQueued = 0, factoryTotalPriceMultiplier = 0.1 }, expected = 1000, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { factoryPrice = 1000, cityFactories = 2, cityQueue = 0, factoryPriceMultiplier = 0.25, countryFactories = 10, allQueued = 0, factoryTotalPriceMultiplier = 0.1 }, expected = 2500, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.defensePrice",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "CityInfoLocalScript mirror, 11/11 observed (economy.md §3.5)",
	expression = "cityDefensesPrice * (1 + (defenses + queue) * cityDefensesPriceMultiplier)",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = {
		cityDefensesPrice = "Techs.cityDefensesPrice",
		defenses = "city defenses",
		queue = "city defenseBuildQueue",
		cityDefensesPriceMultiplier = "Techs.cityDefensesPriceMultiplier",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.cityDefensesPrice * (1 + (inputs.defenses + inputs.queue) * inputs.cityDefensesPriceMultiplier)
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { cityDefensesPrice = 3000, defenses = 0, queue = 0, cityDefensesPriceMultiplier = 0.1 }, expected = 3000, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { cityDefensesPrice = 3000, defenses = 5, queue = 0, cityDefensesPriceMultiplier = 0.1 }, expected = 4500, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.trainingCost",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "TryIncreaseTarget charge, exact on 60/60 and 90/90 raises (ground-commands.md §3.7)",
	expression = "0 if target < count else ((costIncrease - 1) * target + 1) * baseCost",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = { target = "class target", count = "class count", costIncrease = "class costIncrease", baseCost = "class baseCost" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		if inputs.target < inputs.count then
			return 0
		end

		return ((inputs.costIncrease - 1) * inputs.target + 1) * inputs.baseCost
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { target = 3, count = 3, costIncrease = 1.15, baseCost = 2000 }, expected = 2900, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { target = 0, count = 1, costIncrease = 1.15, baseCost = 2000 }, expected = 0, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.manpowerPrice",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "ArmyManagementLocalScript mirror, 11/11 observed purchases (economy.md §3.6)",
	expression = "(1 + (divisions + manpower / 10000) * bonusCoef) * bonusBasePrice",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = { divisions = "country divisions", manpower = "country manpower", divisionSize = "ArmyCommon.divisionSize", bonusCoef = "Army.bonusCoef", bonusBasePrice = "Army.bonusBasePrice" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return (1 + (inputs.divisions + inputs.manpower / inputs.divisionSize) * inputs.bonusCoef) * inputs.bonusBasePrice
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { divisions = 10, manpower = 5000, divisionSize = 10000, bonusCoef = 0.4, bonusBasePrice = 4000 }, expected = 20800, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "heal.perTick",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "replenish x Utils.secondsPerDay x Techs.replenishSpeedModifier; the product 12 and 13.2 (modifier 1.1) match 2,048/2,048 recorded heals",
	expression = "replenish * secondsPerDay * replenishSpeedModifier",
	unit = "health per day tick",
	inputs = { replenish = "ArmyCommon.troopNameToInfo[troop].replenish", secondsPerDay = "Utils.secondsPerDay", replenishSpeedModifier = "Techs.replenishSpeedModifier" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.replenish * inputs.secondsPerDay * inputs.replenishSpeedModifier
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { replenish = 4, secondsPerDay = 3, replenishSpeedModifier = 1 }, expected = 12, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { replenish = 4, secondsPerDay = 3, replenishSpeedModifier = 1.1 }, expected = 13.2, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.equipmentFactories",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "production = factories x outputCoef x factoryProductivity (212/212 and 385/385 updates, economy.md §3.3)",
	expression = "0 if stock >= remainingNeed else ceil(demandPerTick / (outputCoef * factoryProductivity))",
	unit = "factories",
	inputs = {
		stock = "item stock",
		remainingNeed = "equipment still needed to heal every damaged unit of the item to 100",
		demandPerTick = "equipment drawn per tick by the damaged units",
		outputCoef = "item outputCoef",
		factoryProductivity = "Techs.factoryProductivity",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		if inputs.stock >= inputs.remainingNeed then
			return 0
		end

		return math.ceil(inputs.demandPerTick / (inputs.outputCoef * inputs.factoryProductivity))
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { stock = 0, remainingNeed = 40, demandPerTick = 8, outputCoef = 0.5, factoryProductivity = 1 }, expected = 16, tolerance = 0 },
		{ inputs = { stock = 50, remainingNeed = 40, demandPerTick = 8, outputCoef = 0.5, factoryProductivity = 1 }, expected = 0, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.readinessFactories",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "user rule 2026-09-25: at war the split is balanced; a front unit that is hit heals from the next tick on",
	expression = "0 if stock >= frontDrawPerTick else ceil((frontDrawPerTick - stock) / (outputCoef * factoryProductivity))",
	unit = "factories",
	inputs = {
		stock = "item stock",
		frontDrawPerTick = "sum of consumption of our units in contact that use the item",
		outputCoef = "item outputCoef",
		factoryProductivity = "Techs.factoryProductivity",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		if inputs.stock >= inputs.frontDrawPerTick then
			return 0
		end

		return math.ceil((inputs.frontDrawPerTick - inputs.stock) / (inputs.outputCoef * inputs.factoryProductivity))
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { stock = 2, frontDrawPerTick = 8, outputCoef = 0.5, factoryProductivity = 1 }, expected = 12, tolerance = 0 },
		{ inputs = { stock = 9, frontDrawPerTick = 8, outputCoef = 0.5, factoryProductivity = 1 }, expected = 0, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.factoryPaybackTicks",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "a Consumer Goods factory adds Economy.consumerGoodsTaxIncome per tick (economy.md §3.2)",
	expression = "price / consumerGoodsTaxIncome",
	unit = "day ticks",
	inputs = { price = "econ.factoryPrice", consumerGoodsTaxIncome = "Economy.consumerGoodsTaxIncome" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.price / inputs.consumerGoodsTaxIncome
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { price = 1000, consumerGoodsTaxIncome = 40 }, expected = 25, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.roundTicksLeft",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "round end = Map.startTime + workspace.maxGameLength (client countdown, war-and-loop.md §3.7)",
	expression = "(startTime + maxGameLength - now) / tickPeriod",
	unit = "day ticks",
	inputs = { startTime = "workspace.Map.startTime", maxGameLength = "workspace.maxGameLength", now = "GetServerTimeNow", tickPeriod = "tick.periodSeconds" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return (inputs.startTime + inputs.maxGameLength - inputs.now) / inputs.tickPeriod
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { startTime = 0, maxGameLength = 9000, now = 3000, tickPeriod = 3 }, expected = 2000, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

---Evaluate one economy formula (for tests and tools).
---@param id string
---@param inputs table
---@return any
function EconomyFormulas.evaluate(id, inputs)
	return Formula.evaluate(id, inputs)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.EconomyFormulas", EconomyFormulas)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/EconomyPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/EconomyPlanner

	Runs once per day tick, after the front plan (DESIGN §7). Lexicographic order of spending:
	1. production split (free), gated by economy.enabled;
	2. training and manpower, gated by economy.enabled;
	3. research (Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner), gated by research.enabled;
	4. national focus, gated by focus.enabled;
	5. city defences on our city tiles the front analysis marks unsafe, gated by economy.enabled;
	6. factory purchase (one per tick in the cheapest city when it pays back before the round ends),
	   gated by factories.enabled (independent of economy.enabled). Research reservation is honoured:
	   money reserved for a pending tech purchase is never spent on a factory.

	=== [FACTORIES SPLIT] ===
	planFactory is now called under factories.enabled instead of economy.enabled, so the UI can
	offer "production/training" and "factory expansion" as separate switches.
]]

local EconomyPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Economy.EconomyFormulas
local EconomyFormulas = require("Features/Economy/EconomyFormulas")

---@module Features.Economy.ResearchPlanner
local ResearchPlanner = require("Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner")

---@module Features.Economy.FocusPlanner
local FocusPlanner = require("Features/Economy/FocusPlanner")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local CONSUMER_GOODS_ITEM = "Consumer Goods"
local FULL_HEALTH = 100
local TIER = "E"
