					end
					if bestTile ~= nil then
						path = { unit.tile, bestTile }
						how = string.format("one tile to fewer enemy neighbours (%d -> %d)", exposure, bestExposure)
					end
				end
				if path ~= nil then
					plan:assign(unit, path, "rest", TIER_ROTATION, "flagged unit next to an enemy dies when beaten", { health = unit.health, how = how })
				else
					plan:hold(unit, "rest", TIER_ROTATION, "flagged, no safe tile reachable")
					if DefensePlanner.trappedReported[unit.id] ~= unit.tile then
						DefensePlanner.trappedReported[unit.id] = unit.tile
						Logger.warn("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "flaggedTrapped", { unit = unit.id, tile = unit.tile, troop = unit.troopName, health = unit.health })
					end
				end
			else
				plan:hold(unit, "rest", TIER_ROTATION, "flagged: heals only while Idle")
			end
		end
	end
end

function DefensePlanner.gaps(world, plan, analysis)
	-- [SPRINT-2] CoastGuard: prioritaskan coastal threat sebelum gap biasa.
	if world.coastalThreats ~= nil and #world.coastalThreats > 0 then
		for _, site in ipairs(world.coastalThreats) do
			if plan:isFreeDestination(site.tile) and site.garrisonHealth == 0 then
				local candidates = {}
				for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
					if not unit.flagged and analysis.contact[unit.tile] == nil then
						table.insert(candidates, unit)
					end
				end
				if #candidates > 0 then
					-- Deadline: 2 tick dari sekarang (invasi mendarat ~2 tick).
					local deadline = (Tick.periodSeconds() or 3.0) * 2
					local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, site.tile, deadline)
					if best ~= nil and best.seconds < deadline then
						local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(best.unit, site.tile)
						local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, site.tile, placed)
						if not threat.breaks then
							plan:decide(TIER_RESERVES, "coastGuard:" .. site.tile, ranked,
								"enemy invasion force approaching")
							plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "coastGuard", TIER_RESERVES,
								string.format("block coast landing (%d enemy divisions at sea)", site.divisions),
								{ seconds = best.seconds, divisions = site.divisions, threatRatio = threat.ratio })
							Logger.info("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "coastGuarded", {
								tile = site.tile, divisions = site.divisions, ourEta = best.seconds,
							})
							break  -- 1 unit per site cukup untuk "block".
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	for _, gap in ipairs(analysis.gaps) do
		if plan:isFreeDestination(gap.tile) then
			local candidates = {}
			for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
				if not unit.flagged and analysis.contact[unit.tile] == nil then table.insert(candidates, unit) end
			end
			if #candidates == 0 then break end
			local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, gap.tile, gap.enemyEntrySeconds)
			if best ~= nil then
				local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(best.unit, gap.tile)
				local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, gap.tile, placed)
				if not threat.breaks then
					plan:decide(TIER_RESERVES, "coverGap:" .. gap.tile, ranked, "earliest arrival before the enemy could walk in")
					plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "coverGap", TIER_RESERVES, "our empty tile next to an enemy", {
						seconds = best.seconds, enemyEntrySeconds = gap.enemyEntrySeconds, threatRatio = threat.ratio, consequence = gap.consequence,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end
	-- === [FITUR 3] Pre-position untuk incoming enemy moves ===
	-- Baca unit musuh yang sudah bergerak menuju tile kita, dan cover lebih awal.
	local incoming = FrontAnalyzer.incomingMoves(world)

	for _, entry in ipairs(incoming) do
		if plan:isFreeDestination(entry.targetTile) and world.unitsByTile[entry.targetTile] == nil then
			local candidates = {}
			for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
				if not unit.flagged and analysis.contact[unit.tile] == nil then
					table.insert(candidates, unit)
				end
			end

			if #candidates == 0 then
				break
			end

			-- Batas waktu: 90% dari ETA musuh (buffer 10% untuk walk-in timing).
			local deadline = entry.eta * 0.9
			local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, entry.targetTile, deadline)

			if best ~= nil and best.seconds < entry.eta then
				local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(best.unit, entry.targetTile)
				local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, entry.targetTile, placed)

				if not threat.breaks then
					plan:decide(TIER_RESERVES, "preCover:" .. entry.targetTile, ranked,
						"enemy incoming move predicted")
					plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "preCoverGap", TIER_RESERVES,
						"pre-position for incoming enemy move",
						{
							seconds = best.seconds,
							enemyEta = entry.eta,
							threatRatio = threat.ratio,
							enemyUnitId = entry.unitId,
						})

					Logger.info("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "preCoveredGap", {
						tile = entry.targetTile,
						enemyUnit = entry.unitId,
						enemyEta = entry.eta,
						ourEta = best.seconds,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

function DefensePlanner.stage(world, plan, analysis)
	local supportTiles = {}
	local seen = {}
	local function addSupportAround(tileName)
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
			if not seen[neighbourName] and world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName) and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, neighbourName) == 0 then
				seen[neighbourName] = true
				table.insert(supportTiles, neighbourName)
			end
		end
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do addSupportAround(entry.tile) end
	if #supportTiles == 0 then
		local contactTiles = {}
		for tileName in pairs(analysis.contact) do table.insert(contactTiles, tileName) end
		table.sort(contactTiles)
		for _, tileName in ipairs(contactTiles) do addSupportAround(tileName) end
	end
	if #supportTiles == 0 then
		for tileName in pairs(world.ourTiles) do
			if not seen[tileName] and plan:isFreeDestination(tileName) then
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
					if not seen[tileName] and Tactics.isEnemyTile(world, neighbourName) then
						seen[tileName] = true
						table.insert(supportTiles, tileName)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		table.sort(supportTiles)
	end
	if #supportTiles == 0 then return end
	local assigned = 0
	local rounds = 0
	while #supportTiles > 0 do
		local movers = {}
		for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
			if not unit.flagged and analysis.contact[unit.tile] == nil and unit.state == "Idle" then
				local inPosition = false
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
					if analysis.contact[neighbourName] ~= nil or Tactics.isEnemyTile(world, neighbourName) then inPosition = true end
				end
				if not inPosition then table.insert(movers, unit) end
			end
		end
		if #movers == 0 then break end
		rounds += 1
		local field = Pathing.distanceField(supportTiles, function(tileName)
			return world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me and world.unitsByTile[tileName] == nil and plan.locks[tileName] == nil and plan.claims[tileName] == nil
		end)
		local routes = {}
		for _, unit in ipairs(movers) do
			local firstStep = nil
			local firstDistance = math.huge
			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
				local neighbourDistance = field.distance[neighbourName]
				if neighbourDistance ~= nil then
					local total = neighbourDistance + MapGraph.edgeLength(unit.tile, neighbourName)
					if total < firstDistance then firstDistance = total; firstStep = neighbourName end
				end
			end
			if firstStep ~= nil then
				local path = { unit.tile, firstStep }
				local step = firstStep
				while field.towardSource[step] ~= nil do
					step = field.towardSource[step]
					table.insert(path, step)
				end
				path = Tactics.truncatePath(path)
				table.insert(routes, { unit = unit, path = path, destination = path[#path], studs = firstDistance })
			end
		end
		table.sort(routes, function(first, second)
			local firstHeat = ThreatHeat.at(first.destination)
			local secondHeat = ThreatHeat.at(second.destination)
			local firstScore = first.studs - firstHeat * 0.1
			local secondScore = second.studs - secondHeat * 0.1
			if firstScore ~= secondScore then
				return firstScore < secondScore
			end
			return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
		end)
				local takenThisRound = {}
		local assignedThisRound = 0
		for _, route in ipairs(routes) do
			-- [BALANCE] stop kalau sudah mencapai kuota staging tick ini
			if assigned >= MAX_STAGE_PER_TICK then break end
			if not takenThisRound[route.destination] then
				takenThisRound[route.destination] = true
				plan:assign(route.unit, route.path, "reserve", TIER_RESERVES, "free unit moves to support the front", { destination = route.destination, studs = route.studs })
				assigned += 1
				assignedThisRound += 1
			end
		end
		if assignedThisRound == 0 then break end
		if assigned >= MAX_STAGE_PER_TICK then break end
		local remaining = {}
		for _, tileName in ipairs(supportTiles) do
			if not takenThisRound[tileName] then table.insert(remaining, tileName) end
		end
		supportTiles = remaining
	end
	Logger.debug("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "reservesStaged", { assigned = assigned, rounds = rounds, supportTilesLeft = #supportTiles })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", DefensePlanner)
]=]

-- === [CHANGED FASE-2+4] Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer ============================================
MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer

	=== [FASE-2] theaters() clustering (BFS radius 4 hop) ===
	=== [FASE-4] capital-distance sort in analysis.unsafe ===
	=== [REV-2] supplyPockets (pincer target discovery) ===
]]
local FrontAnalyzer = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")
local WorldView = require("Features/Front/WorldView")

local CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL = 0
local CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL_NEIGHBOUR = 1
local CONSEQUENCE_CITY = 2
local CONSEQUENCE_SUPPLY = 3
local CONSEQUENCE_OTHER = 4
local STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE = { Defending = true, Retreating = true, Dead = true }
local LOGGED_GAPS = 3
local MAX_PAIR_CANDIDATES = 16
local THEATER_RADIUS = 4

FrontAnalyzer.CONSEQUENCE = {
	capital = CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL, capitalNeighbour = CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL_NEIGHBOUR,
	city = CONSEQUENCE_CITY, supply = CONSEQUENCE_SUPPLY, other = CONSEQUENCE_OTHER,
}

local function consequenceOf(world, tileName)
	if tileName == world.capitalTile then return CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL, nil end
	if world.capitalTile ~= nil then
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[world.capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
			if neighbourName == tileName then return CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL_NEIGHBOUR, nil end
		end
	end
	if MapGraph.tiles[tileName].cityName ~= nil then return CONSEQUENCE_CITY, nil end
	local cut = SupplyModel.unitsCutByLosing(world, world.me, tileName)
	if #cut > 0 then return CONSEQUENCE_SUPPLY, cut end
	return CONSEQUENCE_OTHER, nil
end

-- === [FASE-4] BFS capital distances over own land ===
local function capitalDistances(world)
	local distances = {}
	local capitalTile = world.capitalTile
	if capitalTile == nil then return distances end
	distances[capitalTile] = 0
	local queue = { capitalTile }
	local head = 1
	while head <= #queue do
		local current = queue[head]
		head += 1
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
			if distances[neighbourName] == nil and world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me then
				distances[neighbourName] = distances[current] + 1
				table.insert(queue, neighbourName)
			end
		end
	end
	return distances
end

-- === [FITUR 3] REINFORCEMENT PREDICTION ======================================
-- Deteksi unit musuh yang sedang bergerak (state "Moving") menuju tile kita.
-- Kembalikan array dengan estimasi waktu tiba (ETA).

---Detect enemy units in "Moving" state heading toward our tiles.
---@param world table
---@return table array of { unitId, targetTile, eta, fromTile, unit }
function FrontAnalyzer.incomingMoves(world)
	local incoming = {}
	local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if unit.state == "Moving" and unit.targetTile ~= nil then
			local targetOwner = world.tileOwner[unit.targetTile]

			if targetOwner == world.me then
				-- Unit musuh menuju tile kita secara langsung.
				-- Hitung ETA dari currentPath.
				local eta = 0
				local currentPath = unit.currentPath

				if currentPath ~= nil and currentPath ~= "[]" and currentPath ~= "" then
					local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(httpService.JSONDecode, httpService, currentPath)
					if decodeOk and type(decoded) == "table" then
						local previousTile = unit.tile
						for _, tileName in ipairs(decoded) do
							if MapGraph.tiles[tileName] ~= nil and MapGraph.tiles[previousTile] ~= nil then
								local hop = Pathing.hopSeconds(world.paths, unit, previousTile, tileName)
								eta += hop
								previousTile = tileName
							end
						end
					end
				end

				table.insert(incoming, {
					unitId = unit.id,
					fromTile = unit.tile,
					targetTile = unit.targetTile,
					eta = eta,
					unit = unit,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	return incoming
end
-- === END FITUR 3 =============================================================

local MAX_PROJECTED_ROUTE_STEPS = 3

local function projectedEnemyRoutes(world)
	local projected = {}
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if enemy.state == "Idle" or enemy.state == "Waiting" then
			local route = { enemy.tile }
			local seen = { [enemy.tile] = true }
			local previousOwner = world.tileOwner[enemy.tile]
			local frontier = { { tile = enemy.tile, route = route, owner = previousOwner } }
			local emitted = 0
			for _ = 1, MAX_PROJECTED_ROUTE_STEPS do
				local nextFrontier = {}
				for _, node in ipairs(frontier) do
					for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[node.tile].landNeighbours) do
						local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
						if owner ~= nil and not world.enemies[owner] and not seen[neighbourName]
							and Pathing.movementCountries(world.paths, enemy.owner)[owner] then
							local nextRoute = table.clone(node.route)
							table.insert(nextRoute, neighbourName)
							seen[neighbourName] = true
							if owner == world.me and world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil then
								emitted += 1
								table.insert(projected, { unit = enemy, unitId = enemy.id, fromTile = enemy.tile, targetTile = neighbourName, route = nextRoute, steps = #nextRoute - 1 })
							end
							table.insert(nextFrontier, { tile = neighbourName, route = nextRoute, owner = owner })
						end
					end
				end
				frontier = nextFrontier
				if #frontier == 0 or emitted >= 2 then break end
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(projected, function(a, b)
		if a.steps ~= b.steps then return a.steps < b.steps end
		if a.targetTile ~= b.targetTile then return a.targetTile < b.targetTile end
		return a.unitId < b.unitId
	end)
	return projected
end

FrontAnalyzer.projectedEnemyRoutes = projectedEnemyRoutes

function FrontAnalyzer.analyze(world, plan)
	local analysis = { contact = {}, threats = {}, unsafe = {}, gaps = {}, consequence = {} }
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		local adjacentEnemies = FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, unit.tile)
		if #adjacentEnemies > 0 then
			analysis.contact[unit.tile] = unit
			local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, unit.tile, unit)
			analysis.threats[unit.tile] = threat
			if threat.breaks then
				local consequence, cutUnits = consequenceOf(world, unit.tile)
				analysis.consequence[unit.tile] = consequence
				table.insert(analysis.unsafe, {
					tile = unit.tile, unit = unit, threat = threat, consequence = consequence,
					cutUnits = cutUnits and #cutUnits or 0, flagged = unit.flagged,
				})
			end
		end
	end
	local capitalDist = capitalDistances(world)
	table.sort(analysis.unsafe, function(first, second)
		if first.consequence ~= second.consequence then return first.consequence < second.consequence end
		local firstDist = capitalDist[first.tile] or math.huge
		local secondDist = capitalDist[second.tile] or math.huge
		if firstDist ~= secondDist then return firstDist < secondDist end
		if first.threat.ratio ~= second.threat.ratio then return first.threat.ratio > second.threat.ratio end
		return first.tile < second.tile
	end)
	for tileName in pairs(world.ourTiles) do
		if world.unitsByTile[tileName] == nil then
			local entrySeconds, entrant = FrontAnalyzer.enemyEntrySeconds(world, tileName)
			if entrySeconds ~= nil then
				local consequence = consequenceOf(world, tileName)
				table.insert(analysis.gaps, {
					tile = tileName, enemyEntrySeconds = entrySeconds,
					enemyUnitId = entrant.id, consequence = consequence,
				})
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(analysis.gaps, function(first, second)
		if first.consequence ~= second.consequence then return first.consequence < second.consequence end
		if first.enemyEntrySeconds ~= second.enemyEntrySeconds then return first.enemyEntrySeconds < second.enemyEntrySeconds end
		return first.tile < second.tile
	end)
	local unsafeSummary = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		table.insert(unsafeSummary, {
			tile = entry.tile, troop = entry.unit.troopName, health = entry.unit.health, flagged = entry.flagged,
			ratio = entry.threat.ratio, breakSeconds = entry.threat.breakSeconds,
			attackers = #entry.threat.attackers, consequence = entry.consequence,
		})
	end
	local contactCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(analysis.contact) do contactCount += 1 end
	Logger.debug("Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer", "analysis", {
		contactTiles = contactCount, unsafe = unsafeSummary, gaps = #analysis.gaps,
		firstGaps = { table.unpack(analysis.gaps, 1, math.min(LOGGED_GAPS, #analysis.gaps)) },
	})
	return analysis
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer", FrontAnalyzer)

-- === [FASE-2] theater clustering ===
function FrontAnalyzer.theaters(world, analysis)
	local contactList = {}
	for tileName in pairs(analysis.contact) do table.insert(contactList, tileName) end
	table.sort(contactList)

	local function near(first, second)
		if first == second then return true end
		local seen = { [first] = 0 }
		local queue = { first }
		local head = 1
		while head <= #queue do
			local current = queue[head]
			head += 1
			if seen[current] < THEATER_RADIUS then
				for _, nbr in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
					if seen[nbr] == nil and world.tileOwner[nbr] == world.me then
						if nbr == second then return true end
						seen[nbr] = seen[current] + 1
						table.insert(queue, nbr)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local theaterOf = {}
	local theaters = {}
	for _, start in ipairs(contactList) do
		if theaterOf[start] == nil then
			local theater = { tiles = { start }, aggressors = {}, threatSum = 0 }
			theaterOf[start] = theater
			local stack = { start }
			while #stack > 0 do
				local current = table.remove(stack)
				for _, candidate in ipairs(contactList) do
					if theaterOf[candidate] == nil and near(current, candidate) then
						theaterOf[candidate] = theater
						table.insert(theater.tiles, candidate)
						table.insert(stack, candidate)
					end
				end
			end
			table.insert(theaters, theater)
		end
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		local theater = theaterOf[entry.tile]
		if theater ~= nil then
			theater.threatSum += entry.threat.ratio
			for _, attacker in ipairs(entry.threat.attackers) do
				theater.aggressors[attacker.unit.owner] = true
			end
		end
	end
	for index, theater in ipairs(theaters) do
		theater.countryCount = 0
		for _ in pairs(theater.aggressors) do theater.countryCount += 1 end
		theater.order = index
	end
	table.sort(theaters, function(first, second)
		if first.countryCount ~= second.countryCount then return first.countryCount > second.countryCount end
		if first.threatSum ~= second.threatSum then return first.threatSum > second.threatSum end
		return first.tiles[1] < second.tiles[1]
	end)
	return theaters
end

-- [SPRINT-1] supplyPockets dengan memoization reachSet.
local pocketCache = { tickId = -1, reachByKey = {} }

function FrontAnalyzer.supplyPockets(world, enemyCountryName, maxCut)
	-- Reset cache tiap tick baru.
	local tickId = (world.tickNumber or 0) .. "|" .. enemyCountryName
	if pocketCache.tickId ~= tickId then
		pocketCache.tickId = tickId
		pocketCache.reachByKey = {}
	end

	local function reachCached(sources, key)
		local cached = pocketCache.reachByKey[key]
		if cached ~= nil then
			return cached
		end
		local removed
		if key == "none" then
			removed = nil
		elseif string.find(key, "|", 1, true) then
			removed = {}
			for token in string.gmatch(key, "[^|]+") do
				removed[token] = true
			end
		else
			removed = key
		end
		local reached = SupplyModel.reachSet(world, enemyCountryName, sources, removed)
		pocketCache.reachByKey[key] = reached
		return reached
	end

	local sources = SupplyModel.sourcesOf(world, enemyCountryName)
	if #sources == 0 then return {} end
	local reached = reachCached(sources, "none")

	local candidates = {}
	for tileName, owner in pairs(world.tileOwner) do
		if owner == enemyCountryName and reached[tileName] then
			for _, nbr in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
				if world.tileOwner[nbr] == world.me then
					table.insert(candidates, tileName)
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(candidates)
	if #candidates == 0 then return {} end

	local unitsByTile = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.unitsOf[enemyCountryName] or {}) do
		local list = unitsByTile[unit.tile]
		if list == nil then list = {}; unitsByTile[unit.tile] = list end
		table.insert(list, unit)
	end

	local pockets = {}
	local function addToPocket(cutSet, units)
		local key = table.concat(cutSet, "|")
		local pocket = pockets[key]
		if pocket == nil then
			pocket = { cutSet = cutSet, units = {}, value = 0 }
			pockets[key] = pocket
		end
		for _, u in ipairs(units) do
			table.insert(pocket.units, u)
			pocket.value += (u.health or 100)
		end
	end

	local doPairs = (maxCut >= 2) and (#candidates <= MAX_PAIR_CANDIDATES)
	for unitTile, units in pairs(unitsByTile) do
		if reached[unitTile] then
			local found = nil
			for _, c in ipairs(candidates) do
				if c ~= unitTile then
					local without = reachCached(sources, c)
					if not without[unitTile] then found = { c }; break end
				end
			end
			if found == nil and doPairs then
				for i = 1, #candidates do
					for j = i + 1, #candidates do
						local ci, cj = candidates[i], candidates[j]
						if ci ~= unitTile and cj ~= unitTile then
							local key = ci .. "|" .. cj
							local without = reachCached(sources, key)
							if not without[unitTile] then found = { ci, cj }; break end
						end
					end
					if found then break end
				end
			end
			if found then addToPocket(found, units) end
		end
	end

	local list = {}
	for _, pocket in pairs(pockets) do table.insert(list, pocket) end
	table.sort(list, function(first, second)
		if first.value ~= second.value then return first.value > second.value end
		return table.concat(first.cutSet, "|") < table.concat(second.cutSet, "|")
	end)
	return list
end

function FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName)
	local found = {}
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]
		if unit ~= nil and world.enemies[unit.owner] then table.insert(found, unit) end
	end
	return found
end

function FrontAnalyzer.enemyEntrySeconds(world, tileName)
	local best = nil
	local bestUnit = nil
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]
		if unit ~= nil and world.enemies[unit.owner] and not STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE[unit.state] then
			local seconds = Pathing.hopSeconds(world.paths, unit, neighbourName, tileName)
			if best == nil or seconds < best then best = seconds; bestUnit = unit end
		end
	end
	return best, bestUnit
end

function FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, unit)
	local friends = WorldView.friendsOf(world, unit.owner)
	local exits = {}
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
		local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
		local free = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil or (plan ~= nil and plan.vacating[neighbourName] == true and unit.owner == world.me)
		if owner ~= nil and friends[owner] and free then
			local claimed = plan ~= nil and (plan.claims[neighbourName] ~= nil)
			if not claimed then table.insert(exits, neighbourName) end
		end
	end
	return exits
end

function FrontAnalyzer.safeLanes(world, plan, unit)
	local lanes = {}
	for _, exitName in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, unit)) do
		if plan.locks[exitName] == nil or plan.locks[exitName] == "lane:" .. unit.tile then
			if #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, exitName) == 0 then table.insert(lanes, exitName) end
		end
	end
	return lanes
end

function FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, unit)
	if unit.flagged or unit.liveFlag then return true, "carries dieOnNextRetreat" end
	if #FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, unit) == 0 then return true, "no free own or friendly neighbour" end
	return false, "has a free exit"
end

return FrontAnalyzer
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/FrontPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/FrontPlanner
	=== [FASE-1] reserve declared pre-T0, staged post-T4 ===
	=== [FASE-3] Defcon emergency mode ===
	=== [REV-5] A5 pincers injected between A2 and A34 ===
]]
local FrontPlanner = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")
local Plan = require("Features/Front/Plan")
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")
local RetreatManager = require("Features/Front/RetreatManager")
local DefensePlanner = require("Features/Front/DefensePlanner")
local AttackPlanner = require("Features/Front/AttackPlanner")
local ReserveManager = require("Features/Front/ReserveManager")
local ThreatHeat = require("Features/Front/ThreatHeat")
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local TheaterManager = require("Features/Front/TheaterManager")   -- [MULTI-FRONT] WAJIB ADA

local DEFCON_CAPITAL_RADIUS = 3
local DEFCON_MIN_AGGRESSORS = 3

local function lockSpawnTile(world, plan)
	local army = world.meInstance:FindFirstChild("Army")
	local cityName = army and army:GetAttribute("priorityCity")
	if type(cityName) ~= "string" or cityName == "" then return end
	local cities = world.meInstance:FindFirstChild("Cities")
	local cityFolder = cities and cities:FindFirstChild(cityName)
	local tileName = cityFolder and cityFolder:GetAttribute("tileName")
	if type(tileName) == "string" then plan:lock(tileName, "spawn:" .. cityName) end
end

local function detectDefcon(world, analysis)
	local aggressors = {}
	for tileName in pairs(analysis.contact) do
		for _, attacker in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName)) do
			aggressors[attacker.owner] = true
		end
	end
	local count = 0
	for _ in pairs(aggressors) do count += 1 end
	if count < DEFCON_MIN_AGGRESSORS then
		return false, { aggressors = count, reason = "below aggressor threshold" }
	end
	if world.capitalTile == nil then
		return false, { aggressors = count, reason = "capital unknown" }
	end
	local nearCapital = { [world.capitalTile] = 0 }
	local queue = { world.capitalTile }
	local head = 1
	while head <= #queue do
		local current = queue[head]
		head += 1
		if nearCapital[current] < DEFCON_CAPITAL_RADIUS then
			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
				if nearCapital[neighbourName] == nil and world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me then
					nearCapital[neighbourName] = nearCapital[current] + 1
					table.insert(queue, neighbourName)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local threatNearCapital = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		if nearCapital[entry.tile] ~= nil and entry.tile ~= world.capitalTile then
			table.insert(threatNearCapital, entry.tile)
		end
	end
	local active = #threatNearCapital > 0
	return active, {
		aggressors = count, threatTiles = threatNearCapital, radius = DEFCON_CAPITAL_RADIUS,
	}
end

function FrontPlanner.plan(world, blockedUnits, commitments, kind)
	local plan = Plan.new(world, blockedUnits, commitments)
	local analysis = Profiler.run("Front_Analyze", FrontAnalyzer.analyze, world, plan)

	-- [MULTI-FRONT] Bangun zona theater & tag setiap unit.
	local zones = TheaterManager.buildZones(world, analysis)
	TheaterManager.tagUnits(world, zones)
	world.theaterZones = zones

	-- [MULTI-FRONT] Urutkan target berdasarkan prioritas theater.
	if zones ~= nil then
		local function baseCompare(a, b)
			if a.consequence ~= b.consequence then
				return a.consequence < b.consequence
			end
			return a.tile < b.tile
		end
		table.sort(analysis.unsafe, TheaterManager.makeComparator(zones, baseCompare))
		table.sort(analysis.gaps, TheaterManager.makeComparator(zones, baseCompare))
	end

	-- [SPRINT-2] Update heat map.
	ThreatHeat.update(world)

	-- [FASE-1] declare reserve before any tier touches units
	local reserve = Profiler.run("Front_DeclareReserve", ReserveManager.declare, world, plan, analysis)

	-- [MULTI-FRONT] Terapkan kuota SETELAH reserve dideklarasikan.
	if zones ~= nil then
		local totalAvailable = #plan:availableUnits()
		local quotas = TheaterManager.computeQuotas(world, zones, totalAvailable)
		plan:applyTheaterQuotas(quotas)
		Logger.info("Features.Front.FrontPlanner", "theatersBuilt", {
			theaterCount = #zones.theaters,
			totalAvailable = totalAvailable,
			quotas = quotas,
		})
	end

	-- [FASE-3] defcon detection
	local defconActive, defconFacts = detectDefcon(world, analysis)
	if defconActive then
		Logger.warn("Features.Front.FrontPlanner", "defcon", {
			cycle = world.cycleLabel, facts = defconFacts,
			reason = "offensive tiers disabled while capital under threat",
		})
	end

	lockSpawnTile(world, plan)

	plan:beginTier("T0")
	Profiler.run("Front_RetreatLocks", RetreatManager.protect, world, plan, analysis)
	Profiler.run("Front_T0_Capital", DefensePlanner.capital, world, plan)

	-- [SPRINT-3] Adaptive tier: skip T1 jika tidak ada unsafe.
	if #analysis.unsafe > 0 then
		plan:beginTier("T1")
		Profiler.run("Front_T1_Unsafe", DefensePlanner.unsafe, world, plan, analysis, AttackPlanner)
	end

	plan:beginTier("T2")
	Profiler.run("Front_T2_Lanes", DefensePlanner.lanes, world, plan, analysis)

	plan:beginTier("T3")
	Profiler.run("Front_T3_Rotation", DefensePlanner.rotation, world, plan, analysis)

	-- [FASE-3] offensives only if not in defcon
	if Settings.get("front.attack") and not defconActive then
		-- [SPRINT-3] A0 hanya jika ada capital target.
		local hasCapitalTarget = false
		for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
			local capitalTile = world.enemyCapitals[enemyName]
			if capitalTile ~= nil then
				local occupant = world.unitsByTile[capitalTile]
				if occupant == nil or world.enemies[occupant.owner] then
					hasCapitalTarget = true
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if hasCapitalTarget then
			plan:beginTier("A0")
			Profiler.run("Front_A0_Capitals", AttackPlanner.capitals, world, plan)
		end

		if #world.enemyUnits > 0 then
			plan:beginTier("A2")
			Profiler.run("Front_A2_Kills", AttackPlanner.kills, world, plan)
		end

		local runPincer = false
		if kind == "tick" then
			local tickNum = world.tickNumber or 0
			if tickNum % 3 == 0 or plan.pendingPincer == true then
				runPincer = true
			end
		end
		if runPincer then
			plan:beginTier("A5")
			Profiler.run("Front_A5_Pincers", AttackPlanner.pincers, world, plan)
		end

		if #plan:availableUnits() > 0 then
			plan:beginTier("A3")
			Profiler.run("Front_A34_Captures", AttackPlanner.captures, world, plan)
		end
	elseif defconActive then
		Logger.info("Features.Front.FrontPlanner", "offensiveSkipped", {
			cycle = world.cycleLabel,
			reason = "defcon: capital under threat from multiple aggressors",
		})
	end

	plan:beginTier("AR")
	Profiler.run("Front_Review", AttackPlanner.reviewOngoing, world, plan)

	plan:beginTier("T4")
	Profiler.run("Front_T4_Gaps", DefensePlanner.gaps, world, plan, analysis)

	if kind == "tick" then
		Profiler.run("Front_T4_Stage", DefensePlanner.stage, world, plan, analysis)
		-- [FASE-1] stage reserve into capital ring
		Profiler.run("Front_ReserveStage", ReserveManager.stage, world, plan, reserve)
	end

	SupplyModel.logOurPrediction(world)

	Logger.info("Features.Front.FrontPlanner", "planned", {
		cycle = world.cycleLabel, kind = kind, summary = plan:summary(),
		unsafeTiles = #analysis.unsafe, gaps = #analysis.gaps, defcon = defconActive,
	})

	return plan, analysis
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.FrontPlanner", FrontPlanner)
]=]

-- === [CHANGED FASE-1] Features/Front/Plan =======================================================
MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/Plan"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/Plan

	=== [FASE-1] reserveUnits set + OFFENSIVE_TIERS gate in isAvailable ===
	=== [REV-6] TIER_RANK extended for A5 and R ===
]]
local Plan = {}
Plan.__index = Plan
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

local ORDERABLE_STATES = { Idle = true, Moving = true, Waiting = true, Attacking = true }
local TIER_RANK = { T0 = 1, T1 = 2, A1 = 2, T2 = 3, T3 = 4, A0 = 5, A2 = 6, A5 = 7, A3 = 8, A4 = 8, AR = 9, T4 = 10, R = 11 }
local UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK = math.huge
local OFFENSIVE_TIERS = { A0 = true, A2 = true, A3 = true, A4 = true, A5 = true, AR = true }

Plan.TIER_RANK = TIER_RANK

function Plan.new(world, blockedUnits, commitments)
	local self = setmetatable({}, Plan)
	self.world = world
	self.blockedUnits = blockedUnits
	self.commitments = commitments
	self.orders = {}
	self.byUnit = {}
	self.claims = {}
	self.vacating = {}
	self.locks = {}
	self.halts = {}
	self.decisionCount = 0
	self.tierCounts = {}
	self.currentTier = nil
	self.currentRank = UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK
	self.reserveUnits = {}
	-- [MULTI-FRONT] Kuota per-theater (kosong = tidak ada batasan)
	self.theaterQuota = {}
	self.theaterSpend = {}
	self.enforceTheaterQuota = false
	for unitId, commitment in pairs(commitments) do
		local unit = world.unitsById[unitId]
		local occupant = world.unitsByTile[commitment.destination]
		local attacksIt = occupant ~= nil and occupant.owner ~= world.me
		if unit ~= nil and not attacksIt then
			self.claims[commitment.destination] = unitId
			if unit.tile ~= commitment.destination and (unit.state == "Moving" or unit.state == "Waiting") then
				self.vacating[unit.tile] = true
			end
		end
	end
	return self
end

function Plan:declareReserve(reserve)
	self.reserveUnits = reserve or {}
end

---[MULTI-FRONT] Terapkan kuota per-theater untuk cycle ini.
function Plan:applyTheaterQuotas(quotas)
	self.theaterQuota = quotas or {}
	self.theaterSpend = {}
	self.enforceTheaterQuota = next(self.theaterQuota) ~= nil
end

function Plan:beginTier(tier)
	self.currentTier = tier
	self.currentRank = TIER_RANK[tier] or UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK
end

function Plan:lock(tileName, reason)
	if self.locks[tileName] == nil then self.locks[tileName] = reason end
end

function Plan:hold(unit, purpose, tier, reason)
	self.byUnit[unit.id] = { unitId = unit.id, fromTile = unit.tile, purpose = purpose, tier = tier, reason = reason, hold = true }
	self.tierCounts[tier] = (self.tierCounts[tier] or 0) + 1
end

function Plan:assign(unit, path, purpose, tier, reason, facts)
	if self.byUnit[unit.id] ~= nil then
		return error(string.format("Plan:assign: unit %s is already assigned (%s)", tostring(unit.id), self.byUnit[unit.id].purpose))
	end
	if path[1] ~= unit.tile then
		return error(string.format("Plan:assign: path of unit %s starts at %s, unit is on %s", tostring(unit.id), tostring(path[1]), unit.tile))
	end
	local destination = path[#path]
	local order = {
		unitId = unit.id, unitTroop = unit.troopName, fromTile = unit.tile, path = path,
		destination = destination, purpose = purpose, tier = tier, reason = reason, facts = facts,
		isAttack = self.world.unitsByTile[destination] ~= nil and self.world.unitsByTile[destination].owner ~= self.world.me,
	}
	table.insert(self.orders, order)
	self.byUnit[unit.id] = order
	self.tierCounts[tier] = (self.tierCounts[tier] or 0) + 1
	if not order.isAttack then
		self.claims[destination] = unit.id
		self.vacating[unit.tile] = true
	end
	-- [MULTI-FRONT] catat pemakaian kuota theater.
	if unit.theaterId ~= nil and unit.theaterId > 0 then
		self.theaterSpend[unit.theaterId] = (self.theaterSpend[unit.theaterId] or 0) + 1
	end
	return order
end

function Plan:halt(unit, tier, reason, facts)
	if self.byUnit[unit.id] ~= nil then
		return error(string.format("Plan:halt: unit %s is already assigned", tostring(unit.id)))
	end
	local entry = { unitId = unit.id, fromTile = unit.tile, purpose = "halt", tier = tier, reason = reason, facts = facts, halt = true }
	table.insert(self.halts, entry)
	self.byUnit[unit.id] = entry
	self.tierCounts[tier] = (self.tierCounts[tier] or 0) + 1
end

function Plan:decide(tier, decisionName, ranked, decidingFact)
	self.decisionCount += 1
	if #ranked == 0 then
		Logger.debug("Features.Front.Plan", "noCandidate", { tier = tier, decision = decisionName, reason = decidingFact })
		return
	end
	Formula.recordChoice(tier .. "." .. decisionName, ranked, decidingFact)
end

function Plan:summary()
	local byPurpose = {}
	for _, order in ipairs(self.orders) do
		byPurpose[order.purpose] = (byPurpose[order.purpose] or 0) + 1
	end
	local lockCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(self.locks) do lockCount += 1 end
	local reserveCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(self.reserveUnits) do reserveCount += 1 end
	return {
		orders = #self.orders, halts = #self.halts, byPurpose = byPurpose,
		byTier = self.tierCounts, locks = lockCount, decisions = self.decisionCount,
		reserve = reserveCount,
	}
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.Plan", Plan)

function Plan:isAvailable(unit)
	if unit.owner ~= self.world.me or self.byUnit[unit.id] ~= nil or self.blockedUnits[unit.id] or ORDERABLE_STATES[unit.state] ~= true then
		return false
	end
	if unit.army ~= nil then return false end
	-- [FASE-1] offensive tiers never touch reserve units
	if self.reserveUnits[unit.id] and OFFENSIVE_TIERS[self.currentTier] then
		return false
	end
	local commitment = self.commitments[unit.id]
	if commitment ~= nil then
		if unit.state == "Attacking" then
			return self.currentRank == TIER_RANK.T0
		end
		return self.currentRank < (TIER_RANK[commitment.tier] or UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK)
	end
	-- [MULTI-FRONT] batasi pemakaian unit per-theater.
	if self.enforceTheaterQuota and unit.theaterId ~= nil and unit.theaterId > 0 then
		local quota = self.theaterQuota[unit.theaterId]
		if quota ~= nil and (self.theaterSpend[unit.theaterId] or 0) >= quota then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

function Plan:isUnassigned(unit)
	return unit.owner == self.world.me and self.byUnit[unit.id] == nil and not self.blockedUnits[unit.id]
end

function Plan:availableUnits()
	local units = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(self.world.ourUnits) do
		if self:isAvailable(unit) then table.insert(units, unit) end
	end
	return units
end

function Plan:isFreeDestination(tileName)
	if self.claims[tileName] ~= nil or self.locks[tileName] ~= nil then return false end
	local occupant = self.world.unitsByTile[tileName]
	return occupant == nil or self.vacating[tileName] == true
end

return Plan
]=]

-- === [NEW FASE-1] Features/Front/ReserveManager =================================================
MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/ReserveManager"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/ReserveManager

	=== [FASE-1] Strategic reserve pool ===
	Units marked as reserve are exempt from every offensive tier (see Plan.OFFENSIVE_TIERS).
	Size = min(MAX_RESERVE, aggressorCount). Staging ring = BFS radius 3 from capital.
]]
local ReserveManager = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local TheaterManager = require("Features/Front/TheaterManager")  -- [MULTI-FRONT]
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")
local Tactics = require("Features/Front/Tactics")

local MIN_RESERVE_PER_AGGRESSOR = 1
local MAX_RESERVE = 4
local STAGING_RADIUS_HOPS = 3

function ReserveManager.declare(world, plan, analysis)
	local aggressors = {}
	for tileName in pairs(analysis.contact) do
		for _, attacker in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName)) do
			aggressors[attacker.owner] = true
		end
	end
	local aggressorCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(aggressors) do aggressorCount += 1 end
	if aggressorCount == 0 then
		plan:declareReserve({})
		return {}
	end
	local capitalTile = world.capitalTile
	if capitalTile == nil then
		plan:declareReserve({})
		return {}
	end
	local MIN_RESERVE_PEACE = 2
	local target
	if aggressorCount == 0 then
		target = 0  -- damai total tanpa ancaman = 0 reserve cukup
	else
		target = math.min(MAX_RESERVE, aggressorCount * MIN_RESERVE_PER_AGGRESSOR)
	end
	-- Kalau ada musuh tapi contact kosong (perang dingin), minimal 1-2 reserve.
	local atColdWar = (next(world.enemies) ~= nil) and (aggressorCount == 0)
	if atColdWar then
		target = MIN_RESERVE_PEACE
	end
	local capitalPosition = MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].position
	local candidates = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.owner == world.me
			and not unit.flagged
			and analysis.contact[unit.tile] == nil
			and plan:isAvailable(unit)
			and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile) then
			local distance = (MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].position - capitalPosition).Magnitude
			table.insert(candidates, { unit = unit, distance = distance })
		end
	end
	table.sort(candidates, function(first, second)
		if first.distance ~= second.distance then return first.distance < second.distance end
		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)
	local reserve = {}
	local declared = 0
	for index = 1, math.min(target, #candidates) do
		reserve[candidates[index].unit.id] = true
		declared += 1
	end
	plan:declareReserve(reserve)
	local names = {}
	for name in pairs(aggressors) do table.insert(names, name) end
	table.sort(names)
	Logger.info("Features.Front.ReserveManager", "declared", {
		aggressors = aggressorCount, aggressorCountries = names,
		target = target, declared = declared, capitalTile = capitalTile,
	})
	return reserve
end

function ReserveManager.stage(world, plan, reserve)
	local capitalTile = world.capitalTile
	if capitalTile == nil then return end
	local ringDistance = { [capitalTile] = 0 }
	local queue = { capitalTile }
	local head = 1
	while head <= #queue do
		local current = queue[head]
		head += 1
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
			if ringDistance[neighbourName] == nil and world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me then
				ringDistance[neighbourName] = ringDistance[current] + 1
				if ringDistance[neighbourName] < STAGING_RADIUS_HOPS then
					table.insert(queue, neighbourName)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local ringTiles = {}
	for tileName, distance in pairs(ringDistance) do
		if distance > 0 then table.insert(ringTiles, { tile = tileName, distance = distance }) end
	end
	table.sort(ringTiles, function(first, second)
		if first.distance ~= second.distance then return first.distance < second.distance end
		return first.tile < second.tile
	end)
	local moved = 0
	for unitId in pairs(reserve) do
		local unit = world.unitsById[unitId]
		if unit ~= nil and plan:isUnassigned(unit) and unit.state == "Idle" and ringDistance[unit.tile] == nil then
			local goal = nil
			for _, candidate in ipairs(ringTiles) do
				if candidate.tile ~= unit.tile and plan:isFreeDestination(candidate.tile) and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, candidate.tile) then
					goal = candidate.tile
					break
				end
			end
			if goal ~= nil then
				local path = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, function(tileName) return tileName == goal end, false)
				if path ~= nil then
					plan:assign(unit, path, "reserveStage", "R",
						"reserve repositions into the capital staging ring",
						{ goal = goal, fromTile = unit.tile })
					moved += 1
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if moved > 0 then
		Logger.info("Features.Front.ReserveManager", "staged", { moved = moved, ringTiles = #ringTiles })
	end
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.ReserveManager", ReserveManager)
]=]

-- === [NEW MULTI-FRONT] Features/Front/TheaterManager ============================================
MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/TheaterManager"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/TheaterManager

	Multi-front coordination. Menggunakan FrontAnalyzer.theaters() yang sudah ada
	untuk memecah peta menjadi beberapa "front" (theater), lalu:
	1. Menandai setiap unit kita dengan theaterId (0 = tidak di theater mana pun).
	2. Menghitung kuota unit per-theater berdasarkan distribusi ancaman.
	3. Menyediakan comparator agar FrontPlanner memproses target dari
	   theater paling berbahaya terlebih dahulu.

	SAFE-BY-DESIGN: kalau modul ini gagal, AI tetap jalan single-front seperti semula.
]]

local TheaterManager = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")

local MAX_THEATERS = 3
local MIN_UNITS_PER_THEATER = 2
local RESERVE_THEATER_ID = 0

---Bangun zona theater dari analysis.
---@param world table
---@param analysis table
---@return table? { tileToTheater = {tileName -> theaterId}, theaters = {...} }
function TheaterManager.buildZones(world, analysis)
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local theaters = FrontAnalyzer.theaters(world, analysis)
		if theaters == nil or #theaters == 0 then
			return nil
		end

		-- Kalau theater lebih dari MAX_THEATERS, ambil yang terbesar saja.
		if #theaters > MAX_THEATERS then
			table.sort(theaters, function(a, b)
				return #a.tiles > #b.tiles
			end)
			local capped = {}
			for i = 1, MAX_THEATERS do
				capped[i] = theaters[i]
			end
			theaters = capped
		end

		-- BFS dari tile kontak setiap theater ke seluruh tile kita.
		local tileToTheater = {}
		for theaterId, theater in ipairs(theaters) do
			local seen = {}
			local queue = {}
			local head = 1
			for _, tileName in ipairs(theater.tiles) do
				if not seen[tileName] then
					seen[tileName] = true
					tileToTheater[tileName] = theaterId
					queue[#queue + 1] = tileName
				end
			end
			while head <= #queue do
				local current = queue[head]
				head += 1
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
					if not seen[neighbourName] and world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me then
						seen[neighbourName] = true
						tileToTheater[neighbourName] = theaterId
						queue[#queue + 1] = neighbourName
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return { tileToTheater = tileToTheater, theaters = theaters }
	end)

	if not ok then
		Logger.warn("Features.Front.TheaterManager", "buildFailed", {
			message = tostring(result),
			fallback = "single-front mode",
		})
		return nil
	end

	return result
end

---Tandai setiap unit kita dengan theaterId.
function TheaterManager.tagUnits(world, zones)
	if zones == nil then
		for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
			unit.theaterId = RESERVE_THEATER_ID
		end
		return
	end
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		unit.theaterId = zones.tileToTheater[unit.tile] or RESERVE_THEATER_ID
	end
end

---Comparator: target dari theater ber-nomor kecil (lebih berbahaya) didahulukan.
function TheaterManager.makeComparator(zones, baseCompare)
	if zones == nil then
		return baseCompare
	end
	return function(a, b)
		local aId = zones.tileToTheater[a.tile] or 999
		local bId = zones.tileToTheater[b.tile] or 999
		if aId ~= bId then
			return aId < bId
		end
		return baseCompare(a, b)
	end
end

---Hitung kuota unit per-theater dari distribusi ancaman.
---@return table { [theaterId] = quota }
function TheaterManager.computeQuotas(world, zones, totalAvailable)
	local quotas = {}
	if zones == nil or totalAvailable <= 0 then
		return quotas
	end

	local weights = {}
	local totalWeight = 0
	for theaterId, theater in ipairs(zones.theaters) do
		local w = (theater.threatSum or 0) + 1
		weights[theaterId] = w
		totalWeight += w
	end

	if totalWeight <= 0 then
		return quotas
	end

	for theaterId, w in pairs(weights) do
		local share = math.floor(totalAvailable * (w / totalWeight))
		quotas[theaterId] = math.max(MIN_UNITS_PER_THEATER, share)
	end

	return quotas
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.TheaterManager", TheaterManager)
]=]

-- === unchanged modules below this line ===

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/RetreatManager"] = [=[
local RetreatManager = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")

function RetreatManager.protect(world, plan, analysis)
	local landingLocks = 0
	local exitLocks = 0
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.state == "Retreating" and unit.targetTile ~= nil then
			plan:lock(unit.targetTile, "lane:" .. unit.tile)
			landingLocks += 1
		end
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		local dies = FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, entry.unit)
		if not dies then
			for _, exitName in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, entry.unit)) do
				plan:lock(exitName, "lane:" .. entry.tile)
				exitLocks += 1
			end
		end
	end
	Logger.debug("Features.Front.RetreatManager", "locked", { landingLocks = landingLocks, exitLocks = exitLocks })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.RetreatManager", RetreatManager)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/Tactics"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/Tactics — REV-3 minimum viable stack + infantry prioritization.
]]
local Tactics = {}
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")
local WorldView = require("Features/Front/WorldView")

local CLASS_PRIORITY = { Infantry = 1, AntiTank = 2, Tanks = 3 }
local HARD_COUNTER = {
	AntiTank = { Tanks = true },
	Infantry = { AntiTank = true },
	Tanks = { Infantry = true },
}

function Tactics.mayAttackFrom(world, originTile, targetOwner)
	local originOwner = world.tileOwner[originTile]
	if originOwner == nil or originOwner == world.me then return true end
	return GameModules.get("DiplomacyCommon").CanAttackFromCountryTile(world.meInstance, originOwner, targetOwner)
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.Tactics", Tactics)

function Tactics.search(world, plan, unit, maxSeconds)
	local blocked = {}
	for tileName in pairs(plan.locks) do blocked[tileName] = true end
	for tileName, claimant in pairs(plan.claims) do
		if claimant ~= unit.id then blocked[tileName] = true end
	end
	return Pathing.search(world.paths, unit, { maxSeconds = maxSeconds, blocked = blocked })
end

function Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, units, goalTile, maxSeconds)
	local ranked = {}
	local goalPosition = MapGraph.tiles[goalTile].position
	local ordered = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(units) do
		if unit.tile ~= goalTile then
			local straight = (MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].position - goalPosition).Magnitude
			table.insert(ordered, { unit = unit, lowerBound = straight / Pathing.fastestSpeed(world.paths, unit) })
		end
	end
	table.sort(ordered, function(first, second)
		if first.lowerBound ~= second.lowerBound then return first.lowerBound < second.lowerBound end
		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)
	local bestSeconds = maxSeconds or math.huge
	local skipped = 0
	for _, candidate in ipairs(ordered) do
		if candidate.lowerBound > bestSeconds then
			skipped += 1
		else
			local unit = candidate.unit
			local result = Tactics.search(world, plan, unit, bestSeconds)
			local seconds = result.times[goalTile]
			if seconds ~= nil then
				local path = Pathing.pathTo(result, goalTile)
				if path ~= nil then
					table.insert(ranked, { id = unit.id, score = seconds, facts = { tile = unit.tile, troop = unit.troopName, hops = #path - 1, lowerBound = candidate.lowerBound }, unit = unit, path = path })
					if seconds < bestSeconds then bestSeconds = seconds end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if skipped > 0 then
		table.insert(ranked, { id = "boundSkipped", score = math.huge, facts = { candidates = skipped, reason = "lower bound above the best arrival" } })
	end
	table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
		if first.score ~= second.score then return first.score < second.score end
		return first.id < second.id
	end)
	local best = ranked[1]
	if best == nil or best.unit == nil then return nil, ranked end
	return { unit = best.unit, path = best.path, seconds = best.score }, ranked
end

function Tactics.existingAttackers(world, targetTile)
	local attackers = {}
	local friends = world.friends
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[targetTile].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]
		if unit ~= nil and friends[unit.owner] and unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile == targetTile then
			table.insert(attackers, { unit = unit, fromTile = neighbourName, existing = true })
		end
	end
	return attackers
end

local function unitPriority(class, defenderClass, mode)
	if mode == "critical" then return 0 end
	local row = HARD_COUNTER[class]
	if row ~= nil and row[defenderClass] then return 0 end
	return CLASS_PRIORITY[class] or 2
end

function Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, targetTile, candidates, mode)
	local defender = world.unitsByTile[targetTile]
	if defender == nil then return nil, { reason = "no defender" } end
	local chosenMode = mode or "standard"
	local existing = Tactics.existingAttackers(world, targetTile)
	local scored = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(candidates) do
		if unit.tile ~= targetTile and Tactics.mayAttackFromHot(world, unit.tile, defender.owner) then
			local alone = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, { { unit = unit, fromTile = unit.tile } }, defender, targetTile, "attacker")
			table.insert(scored, {
				unit = unit, alone = alone.ratio,
				priority = unitPriority(unit.class, defender.class, chosenMode),
			})
		end
	end
	table.sort(scored, function(first, second)
		if first.priority ~= second.priority then return first.priority < second.priority end
		if first.alone ~= second.alone then return first.alone > second.alone end
		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)
	local entries = table.clone(existing)
	local added = {}
	local lastResult = nil
	if #entries > 0 then
		lastResult = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, targetTile, "attacker")
		if lastResult.breaks and not lastResult.tie then
			return { attackers = added, all = entries, result = lastResult }, { existing = #existing, ratio = lastResult.ratio }
		end
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(scored) do
		local newEntry = { unit = entry.unit, fromTile = entry.unit.tile }
		table.insert(entries, newEntry)
		table.insert(added, newEntry)
		lastResult = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, targetTile, "attacker")
		if lastResult.breaks and not lastResult.tie then
			return { attackers = added, all = entries, result = lastResult }, {
				existing = #existing, added = #added, ratio = lastResult.ratio, mode = chosenMode,
			}
		end
	end
	return nil, {
		existing = #existing, candidates = #scored,
		bestRatio = lastResult and lastResult.ratio or 0,
		tie = lastResult and lastResult.tie or false, mode = chosenMode,
	}
end

function Tactics.mayAttackFromHot(world, originTile, targetOwner)
	local originOwner = world.tileOwner[originTile]
	if originOwner == nil or originOwner == world.me then return true end
	return GameModules.get("DiplomacyCommon").CanAttackFromCountryTile(world.meInstance, originOwner, targetOwner)
end

function Tactics.safeAfterFight(world, attackerEntry, predictedHealth, beatenTile)
	local weakened = CombatModel.placedOn(attackerEntry.unit, attackerEntry.fromTile)
	weakened.health = predictedHealth
	local attackers = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(CombatModel.potentialAttackers(world, attackerEntry.fromTile, world.enemies)) do
		if entry.fromTile ~= beatenTile then table.insert(attackers, entry) end
	end
	if #attackers == 0 then return true, { tile = attackerEntry.fromTile, threats = 0 } end
	local threat = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, attackers, weakened, attackerEntry.fromTile, "defender")
	return not threat.breaks, { tile = attackerEntry.fromTile, threats = #attackers, ratio = threat.ratio, predictedHealth = predictedHealth }
end

local function touchesEnemy(world, tileName)
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local occupant = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]
		if occupant ~= nil and world.enemies[occupant.owner] then return true end
	end
	return false
end

function Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, accept, avoidEnemyContact)
	local allowed = world.paths.movement[unit.owner]
	if allowed == nil then
		allowed = Ownership.movementCountries(unit.owner)
		world.paths.movement[unit.owner] = allowed
	end
	local previous = { [unit.tile] = false }
	local queue = { unit.tile }
	local head = 1
	while head <= #queue do
		local current = queue[head]
		head += 1
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
			if previous[neighbourName] == nil then
				local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
				local passable = owner ~= nil and allowed[owner]
					and world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil
					and plan.claims[neighbourName] == nil
					and plan.locks[neighbourName] == nil
					and not (avoidEnemyContact and touchesEnemy(world, neighbourName))
				if passable then
					previous[neighbourName] = current
					if accept(neighbourName) then
						local reversed = { neighbourName }
						local step = current
						while step ~= false and step ~= nil do
							table.insert(reversed, step)
							step = previous[step]
						end
						local path = {}
						for index = #reversed, 1, -1 do table.insert(path, reversed[index]) end
						return path
					end
					table.insert(queue, neighbourName)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function Tactics.truncatePath(path)
	if #path <= Pathing.MAX_PATH_TILES then return path end
	local cut = {}
	for index = 1, Pathing.MAX_PATH_TILES do cut[index] = path[index] end
	return cut
end

function Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, tileName)
	local units = {}
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]
		if unit ~= nil and plan:isAvailable(unit) then table.insert(units, unit) end
	end
	return units
end

function Tactics.isEnemyTile(world, tileName)
	local owner = world.tileOwner[tileName]
	return owner ~= nil and world.enemies[owner] == true
end

function Tactics.friendsOf(world, countryName)
	return WorldView.friendsOf(world, countryName)
end

function Tactics.isAI(countryName)
	return Ownership.isAI(countryName)
end

return Tactics
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/WorldView"] = [=[
local WorldView = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")
local Units = require("Game/Units")
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

local DEAD_STATE = "Dead"

function WorldView.build(ourCountry, cycleLabel)
	local me = ourCountry.Name
	local world = {
		cycleLabel = cycleLabel, me = me, meInstance = ourCountry,
		serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), tileOwner = Ownership.tileOwner,
		ourTiles = Ownership.countryTiles[me] or {}, enemies = Ownership.enemiesOf(me),
		friends = Ownership.friendsOf(me), coastalThreats = nil, unitsByTile = {}, unitsById = {}, unitsOf = {},
		ourUnits = {}, enemyUnits = {}, enemyCapitals = {},
		supplyCache = {}, friendCache = {}, enemyCache = {}, duplicateTiles = {},
	}
	for record in pairs(Units.records) do
		if not record.superseded and record.model.Parent ~= nil then
			local unit = Units.snapshotNow(record)
			if unit.state ~= DEAD_STATE then
				local existing = world.unitsByTile[unit.tile]
				if existing ~= nil then
					table.insert(world.duplicateTiles, { tile = unit.tile, ids = { existing.id, unit.id } })
				end
				if existing == nil or existing.id < unit.id then
					world.unitsByTile[unit.tile] = unit
				end
				world.unitsById[unit.id] = unit
				local owned = world.unitsOf[unit.owner or "?"]
				if owned == nil then owned = {}; world.unitsOf[unit.owner or "?"] = owned end
				table.insert(owned, unit)
				if unit.owner == me then
					table.insert(world.ourUnits, unit)
				elseif unit.owner ~= nil and world.enemies[unit.owner] then
					table.insert(world.enemyUnits, unit)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local sortById = function(first, second) return first.id < second.id end
	table.sort(world.ourUnits, sortById)
	table.sort(world.enemyUnits, sortById)
	local capitalTile, capitalReason = Ownership.capitalTile(me)
	world.capitalTile = capitalTile
	world.capitalReason = capitalReason
	for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
		local enemyCapital, enemyReason = Ownership.capitalTile(enemyName)
		world.enemyCapitals[enemyName] = enemyCapital
		if enemyCapital == nil then
			Logger.warn("Features.Front.WorldView", "enemyCapitalUnknown", { enemy = enemyName, reason = enemyReason })
		end
	end
	world.combat = CombatModel.newContext(world)
	world.paths = Pathing.newContext(world)
	if #world.duplicateTiles > 0 then
		Logger.debug("Features.Front.WorldView", "replacementOverlap", { tiles = world.duplicateTiles })
	end
	Logger.debug("Features.Front.WorldView", "built", {
		cycle = cycleLabel, me = me, enemies = world.enemies,
		ourUnits = #world.ourUnits, enemyUnits = #world.enemyUnits,
		capitalTile = capitalTile, enemyCapitals = world.enemyCapitals,
	})
	return world
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.WorldView", WorldView)

function WorldView.friendsOf(world, countryName)
	local friends = world.friendCache[countryName]
	if friends == nil then
		friends = Ownership.friendsOf(countryName)
		world.friendCache[countryName] = friends
	end
	return friends
end

function WorldView.enemiesOf(world, countryName)
	local enemies = world.enemyCache[countryName]
	if enemies == nil then
		enemies = Ownership.enemiesOf(countryName)
		world.enemyCache[countryName] = enemies
	end
	return enemies
end

return WorldView
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Movement/Pathing"] = [=[
local Pathing = {}
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

local NO_SUPPLY_SPEED_COEF = 0.3
local RIVER_SPEED_BASE = -0.05
local RIVER_SPEED_PER_MODIFIER = -0.45
local RECORDED_STEP_OVERHEAD_SECONDS = 0.18
local MAX_PATH_TILES = 99
local HEAP_ARITY = 2
local FIRST_HOP_INDEX = 2
local RETREAT_SPEED_MULTIPLIER = 2
local ABSENT_COEFFICIENT = 1

Pathing.MAX_PATH_TILES = MAX_PATH_TILES

Formula.define({
	id = "move.hopSeconds",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "TroopCommon.GetTroopSpeedAndDesc lines 114-214; MoveArrow endTime - startTime matched 5,286/5,286",
	expression = "distance / (speed * (0.3 if unsupplied) * armySpeedCoef * groupSpeedCoef * terrainSpeedCoef(tile left) * (1 - 0.05 - 0.45 r if r > 0))",
	unit = "s",
	inputs = {
		distance = "studs (Utils.GetDistanceBetweenTiles)", speed = "speed attribute",
		unsupplied = "boolean: noSupply or noMoney at hop start",
		armySpeedCoef = "Army.<Class>SpeedCoef",
		groupSpeedCoef = "army group <Class>SpeedCoef (1 outside a group)",
		terrainSpeedCoef = "terrainModifiers[terrain of the tile left].<Class>SpeedCoef (1 when absent)",
		riverModifier = "river modifier tile left -> tile entered",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		local speed = inputs.speed
		if inputs.unsupplied then speed *= NO_SUPPLY_SPEED_COEF end
		speed = speed * inputs.armySpeedCoef * inputs.groupSpeedCoef * inputs.terrainSpeedCoef
		if inputs.riverModifier > 0 then speed *= 1 + RIVER_SPEED_BASE + inputs.riverModifier * RIVER_SPEED_PER_MODIFIER end
		return inputs.distance / speed
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { distance = 10.8430, speed = 1.1, unsupplied = false, armySpeedCoef = 1, groupSpeedCoef = 1.1, terrainSpeedCoef = 0.85, riverModifier = 0.2 }, expected = 12.2588, tolerance = 1e-3 },
		{ inputs = { distance = 10.8430, speed = 1.1, unsupplied = true, armySpeedCoef = 1, groupSpeedCoef = 1.1, terrainSpeedCoef = 0.85, riverModifier = 0.2 }, expected = 12.2588 / 0.3, tolerance = 1e-2 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "move.retreatSeconds",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "recorded retreat hops, 295/295 (map.md §3.16)",
	expression = "distance / (2 * speed)",
	unit = "s",
	inputs = { distance = "studs", speed = "speed attribute" },
	compute = function(inputs) return inputs.distance / (RETREAT_SPEED_MULTIPLIER * inputs.speed) end,
	examples = { { inputs = { distance = 8.72, speed = 1 }, expected = 4.36, tolerance = 1e-9 } },
})

function Pathing.newContext(world)
	return { world = world, speedCoefs = {}, movement = {} }
end

function Pathing.stepOverheadSeconds()
	return RECORDED_STEP_OVERHEAD_SECONDS, "recorded server dwell per intermediate tile (map.md §3.12)"
end

local function movementSet(context, countryName)
	local allowed = context.movement[countryName]
	if allowed == nil then
		allowed = Ownership.movementCountries(countryName)
		context.movement[countryName] = allowed
	end
	return allowed
end

local function classSpeedCoef(context, unit)
	local key = tostring(unit.owner) .. "|" .. unit.class .. "|" .. tostring(unit.army)
	local coef = context.speedCoefs[key]
	if coef ~= nil then return coef end
	local country = Ownership.country(unit.owner)
	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Pathing: unknown country '%s' of unit %s", tostring(unit.owner), tostring(unit.id)))
	end
	local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
	local armyCoef = Resolver.optionalAttribute(army, unit.class .. "SpeedCoef", "number", "TroopCommon.lua:146 reads it with default 1") or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	local groupCoef = ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	if unit.army ~= nil then
		local armies = army:FindFirstChild("Armies")
		local group = armies and armies:FindFirstChild(unit.army)
		if group ~= nil then
			groupCoef = Resolver.optionalAttribute(group, unit.class .. "SpeedCoef", "number", "TroopCommon.lua:160 reads it with default 1") or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
		end
	end
	coef = { army = armyCoef, group = groupCoef }
	context.speedCoefs[key] = coef
	return coef
end

local function terrainSpeed(unitClass, tileName)
	local terrain = MapGraph.tiles[tileName].terrain
	if terrain == nil then return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT end
	local modifiers = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").terrainModifiers[terrain]
	local value = modifiers and modifiers[unitClass .. "SpeedCoef"]
	if value == nil then return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT end
	return value
end

local function hopSecondsFor(context, unit, fromName, toName)
	local coefs = classSpeedCoef(context, unit)
	return Formula.evaluate("move.hopSeconds", {
		distance = MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName),
		speed = unit.speed,
		unsupplied = unit.noSupply or unit.noMoney,
		armySpeedCoef = coefs.army, groupSpeedCoef = coefs.group,
		terrainSpeedCoef = terrainSpeed(unit.class, fromName),
		riverModifier = MapGraph.edgeRiver(fromName, toName),
	})
end

function Pathing.hopSeconds(context, unit, fromName, toName)
	return hopSecondsFor(context, unit, fromName, toName)
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Movement.Pathing", Pathing)

local function heapPush(heap, node, priority)
	local entry = { node = node, priority = priority }
	table.insert(heap, entry)
	local index = #heap
	while index > 1 do
		local parentIndex = index // HEAP_ARITY
		if heap[parentIndex].priority <= priority then break end
		heap[index] = heap[parentIndex]
		index = parentIndex
	end
	heap[index] = entry
end

local function heapPop(heap)
	local count = #heap
	if count == 0 then return nil end
	local top = heap[1]
	local last = table.remove(heap)
	if count > 1 then
		local index = 1
		local size = #heap
		while true do
			local child = index * HEAP_ARITY
			if child > size then break end
			if child + 1 <= size and heap[child + 1].priority < heap[child].priority then child += 1 end
			if heap[child].priority >= last.priority then break end
			heap[index] = heap[child]
			index = child
		end
		heap[index] = last
	end
	return top
end

function Pathing.search(context, unit, options)
	local world = context.world
	local maxSeconds = options and options.maxSeconds or math.huge
	local blocked = options and options.blocked or nil
	local isOccupied = options and options.isOccupied or function(tileName) return world.unitsByTile[tileName] ~= nil end
	local allowed = movementSet(context, unit.owner)
	local overhead = RECORDED_STEP_OVERHEAD_SECONDS
	local times = { [unit.tile] = 0 }
	local previous = {}
	local hops = { [unit.tile] = 0 }
	local occupiedGoals = {}
	local settled = {}
	local heap = {}
	heapPush(heap, unit.tile, 0)
	while true do
		local entry = heapPop(heap)
		if entry == nil then break end
		local current = entry.node
		if not settled[current] and entry.priority <= times[current] then
			settled[current] = true
			local expandable = current == unit.tile or not occupiedGoals[current]
			if expandable and hops[current] + 1 <= MAX_PATH_TILES - 1 then
				local stepCost = 0
				if current ~= unit.tile then stepCost = overhead end
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
					local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
					if not settled[neighbourName] and owner ~= nil and allowed[owner] and not (blocked and blocked[neighbourName]) then
						local arrival = times[current] + stepCost + hopSecondsFor(context, unit, current, neighbourName)
						if arrival <= maxSeconds and arrival < (times[neighbourName] or math.huge) then
							times[neighbourName] = arrival
							previous[neighbourName] = current
							hops[neighbourName] = hops[current] + 1
							if isOccupied(neighbourName) then occupiedGoals[neighbourName] = true
							else occupiedGoals[neighbourName] = nil end
							heapPush(heap, neighbourName, arrival)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return { times = times, previous = previous, hops = hops, occupiedGoals = occupiedGoals, start = unit.tile }
end

function Pathing.pathTo(searchResult, goalName)
	if searchResult.times[goalName] == nil then return nil end
	local reversed = { goalName }
	local current = goalName
	while current ~= searchResult.start do
		current = searchResult.previous[current]
		if current == nil then return nil end
		table.insert(reversed, current)
	end
	local path = {}
	for index = #reversed, 1, -1 do table.insert(path, reversed[index]) end
	return path
end

function Pathing.pathSeconds(context, unit, path)
	local total = 0
	for index = FIRST_HOP_INDEX, #path do
		total += hopSecondsFor(context, unit, path[index - 1], path[index])
		if index > FIRST_HOP_INDEX then total += RECORDED_STEP_OVERHEAD_SECONDS end
	end
	return total
end

function Pathing.fastestSpeed(context, unit)
	local coefs = classSpeedCoef(context, unit)
	local best = ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	for _, modifiers in pairs(GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").terrainModifiers) do
		local value = modifiers[unit.class .. "SpeedCoef"]
		if value ~= nil and value > best then best = value end
	end
	return unit.speed * coefs.army * coefs.group * best
end

function Pathing.distanceField(sources, passable)
	local distance = {}
	local toward = {}
	local settled = {}
	local heap = {}
	for _, source in ipairs(sources) do
		distance[source] = 0
		heapPush(heap, source, 0)
	end
	while true do
		local entry = heapPop(heap)
		if entry == nil then break end
		local current = entry.node
		if not settled[current] and entry.priority <= distance[current] then
			settled[current] = true
			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
				if not settled[neighbourName] and passable(neighbourName) then
					local candidate = distance[current] + MapGraph.edgeLength(current, neighbourName)
					if candidate < (distance[neighbourName] or math.huge) then
						distance[neighbourName] = candidate
						toward[neighbourName] = current
						heapPush(heap, neighbourName, candidate)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return { distance = distance, towardSource = toward }
end

function Pathing.retreatSeconds(unit, fromName, toName)
	return Formula.evaluate("move.retreatSeconds", {
		distance = MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName), speed = unit.speed,
	})
end

return Pathing
]=]

-- <<< PART 3/3 END

-- SISA MODUL — tempel tepat setelah PART 3/3 END (sebelum epilogue loader)
-- ===============================================================================================

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Navy/NavyPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Navy/NavyPlanner

	Navy, lower priority than land (players.md §10.13: few naval actions by anyone; the server AI's
	Italy made 8 landings on undefended coasts). v1 does two things (DESIGN §8):
	- coast watch: every enemy fleet carrying divisions is logged with its tile and the coastal tiles
	  of ours next to its sea tile, so the front's gap tier sees landings as they happen;
	- docking: an idle damaged fleet of ours sails to the nearest own port city (ships repair only
	  while docked, +8 % max health per day, and cost no upkeep there, navy.md §3).
	Invasions stay off unless navy.invasions is on (not built in v1: the setting only reports).
]]

local NavyPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Fleets
local Fleets = require("Game/Fleets")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local TIER = "N"
local IDLE_STATE = "Idle"
local MAX_PATH_TILES = 99

---Distance-ordered search over sea tiles from a fleet's tile to the first sea tile next to one of
---the goal land tiles.
---@param startTile string
---@param goalLandTiles table set of land tile names
---@return table?
local function navalPathToAny(startTile, goalLandTiles)
	local distance = { [startTile] = 0 }
	local previous = {}
	local settled = {}
	local frontier = { startTile }

	while #frontier > 0 do
		local bestIndex = 1
		for index, tileName in ipairs(frontier) do
			if distance[tileName] < distance[frontier[bestIndex]] then
				bestIndex = index
			end
		end

		local current = table.remove(frontier, bestIndex)

		if not settled[current] then
			settled[current] = true

			for _, landName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
				if goalLandTiles[landName] then
					local reversed = { landName, current }
					local step = previous[current]

					while step ~= nil do
						table.insert(reversed, step)
						step = previous[step]
					end

					local path = {}
					for index = #reversed, 1, -1 do
						table.insert(path, reversed[index])
					end

					if #path > MAX_PATH_TILES then
						return nil
					end

					return path
				end
			end

			for _, seaName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].seaNeighbours) do
				if not settled[seaName] then
					local candidate = distance[current] + MapGraph.edgeLength(current, seaName)

					if candidate < (distance[seaName] or math.huge) then
						distance[seaName] = candidate
						previous[seaName] = current
						table.insert(frontier, seaName)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

---Run the navy for one tick.
---@param world table
local coastGuardState = {
	lastInvasionTick = -100,
	pendingLandingSites = {},
}

function NavyPlanner.run(world)
	local fleets = Fleets.scan()
	local ourPorts = {}

	for _, city in ipairs(CountryState.cities(world.meInstance)) do
		if city.isPort and world.tileOwner[city.tileName] == world.me then
			ourPorts[city.tileName] = city.name
		end
	end

	local threats = {}
	local docked = {}

	for _, fleet in ipairs(fleets) do
		if fleet.country ~= nil and world.enemies[fleet.country] and fleet.loadedDivisions > 0 then
			local coast = {}
			for _, landName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[fleet.tile].landNeighbours) do
				if world.tileOwner[landName] == world.me then
					table.insert(coast, landName)
				end
			end

			table.insert(threats, { country = fleet.country, tile = fleet.tile, divisions = fleet.loadedDivisions, ourCoastNearby = coast, state = fleet.state })
		end

		if fleet.country == world.me and fleet.state == IDLE_STATE and fleet.damaged then
			local path = navalPathToAny(fleet.tile, ourPorts)

			if path ~= nil then
				local instances = {}
				for _, tileName in ipairs(path) do
					table.insert(instances, MapGraph.tiles[tileName].instance)
				end

				ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
					remote = "MoveFleet",
					args = { instances },
					key = "dock:" .. fleet.tile .. ":" .. path[#path],
					reason = string.format("damaged idle fleet docks at %s to repair", ourPorts[path[#path]]),
					tier = TIER,
				})

				table.insert(docked, { from = fleet.tile, port = ourPorts[path[#path]] })
			end
		end
	end

	if #threats > 0 then
		Logger.warn("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", "enemyTransports", { fleets = threats })

		-- Bangun kandidat landing site: laut yang bersebelahan dengan daratan kita.
		local landingSites = {}
		for _, threat in ipairs(threats) do
			local seaTile = MapGraph.tiles[threat.tile]
			if seaTile ~= nil then
				for _, landName in ipairs(seaTile.landNeighbours) do
					if world.tileOwner[landName] == world.me then
						-- Skor: jumlah unit musuh di tile * (1 + bonus kalau kosong)
						local garrison = world.unitsByTile[landName]
						local defenseScore = garrison ~= nil and (garrison.health or 100) or 0
						local site = landingSites[landName]
						if site == nil then
							site = {
								tile = landName,
								divisions = 0,
								seas = {},
								garrisonHealth = defenseScore,
							}
							landingSites[landName] = site
						end
						site.divisions += threat.divisions
						table.insert(site.seas, threat.tile)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		-- Prioritas: site dengan divisions terbanyak & garrison terlunak.
		local siteList = {}
		for _, site in pairs(landingSites) do
			table.insert(siteList, site)
		end
		table.sort(siteList, function(first, second)
			-- Peluang lebih besar mendarat di tile tanpa garrison.
			if first.garrisonHealth ~= second.garrisonHealth then
				return first.garrisonHealth < second.garrisonHealth
			end
			if first.divisions ~= second.divisions then
				return first.divisions > second.divisions
			end
			return first.tile < second.tile
		end)

		-- Simpan untuk DefensePlanner via world (state lintas modul).
		world.coastalThreats = siteList

		-- Tandai tick invasi.
		coastGuardState.lastInvasionTick = world.tickNumber or 0

		Logger.warn("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", "coastalThreats", {
			sites = siteList,
			reason = "invasi terdeteksi, defense akan memprioritaskan tile ini",
		})
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", "run", { fleets = #fleets, enemyLoadedFleets = #threats, docking = docked })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", NavyPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher

	The single door for every non-movement remote (economy, research, focus, army groups, navy,
