			maid:give(child.Changed:Connect(function()
				announceCountry("LocalPlayer.Country.Changed")
			end))
			announceCountry("LocalPlayer.Country added")
		end))
	end
	maid:give(workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == MAP_MODEL_NAME and child:IsA("Model") then
			Logger.info("Game.Session", "mapModelAdded", { map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE) })
			Session.mapReplaced:fire("workspace.Map added")
		end
	end))
	maid:give(workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal(MAP_ATTRIBUTE):Connect(function()
		Logger.info("Game.Session", "mapAttributeChanged", { map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE) })
		Session.mapReplaced:fire("workspace map attribute changed")
	end))
	Logger.info("Game.Session", "attached", {
		placeId = game.PlaceId, correctPlace = Session.isCorrectPlace(),
		jobId = game.JobId, map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE),
		replicator = Session.replicatorPresent(),
	})
	announceCountry("attach")
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Session", Session)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Tick"] = [=[
local Tick = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

local STAMP_ATTRIBUTE = "lastTimeSupplied"
local PASS_SEPARATION_FRACTION_OF_DAY = 0.5
local STALE_AFTER_PERIODS = 2
local MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR = 2

Tick.dayTick = Signal.new("Game.Tick.dayTick")
Tick.lastPassServerTime = nil
Tick.lastPassClock = nil
Tick.passCount = 0
Tick.intervals = {}
Tick.sortedIntervals = {}
Tick.cachedPeriod = nil

Formula.define({
	id = "tick.periodSeconds",
	tier = "T2",
	source = "median interval between consecutive tick-pass stamp values measured this session (Game/Tick)",
	expression = "median(intervals)",
	unit = "s",
	inputs = { sortedIntervals = "array of s, ascending" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		local sorted = inputs.sortedIntervals
		local count = #sorted
		if count == 0 then return error("tick.periodSeconds: no interval measured yet") end
		local middle = (count + 1) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
		local lower = sorted[math.floor(middle)]
		local upper = sorted[math.ceil(middle)]
		return (lower + upper) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { sortedIntervals = { 3.056, 3.067, 3.084 } }, expected = 3.067, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { sortedIntervals = { 3.098, 3.117, 3.117, 3.163 } }, expected = 3.117, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

local function insertSorted(sorted, value)
	local insertAt = #sorted + 1
	for index, existing in ipairs(sorted) do
		if value < existing then insertAt = index; break end
	end
	table.insert(sorted, insertAt, value)
end

local function onStamp(tileName, stampValue)
	if type(stampValue) ~= "number" then return end
	local lastPass = Tick.lastPassServerTime
	local separation = GameModules.get("Utils").secondsPerDay * PASS_SEPARATION_FRACTION_OF_DAY
	if lastPass ~= nil and stampValue <= lastPass + separation then return end
	local nowClock = os.clock()
	if lastPass ~= nil then
		local interval = stampValue - lastPass
		table.insert(Tick.intervals, interval)
		insertSorted(Tick.sortedIntervals, interval)
		Tick.cachedPeriod = Formula.evaluate("tick.periodSeconds", { sortedIntervals = Tick.sortedIntervals })
	end
	Tick.lastPassServerTime = stampValue
	Tick.lastPassClock = nowClock
	Tick.passCount += 1
	Logger.debug("Game.Tick", "dayTick", {
		pass = Tick.passCount, serverTime = stampValue, firstStampTile = tileName,
		arrivalLagSeconds = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - stampValue,
		intervalSeconds = lastPass and (stampValue - lastPass) or nil,
		measuredPeriodSeconds = Tick.cachedPeriod,
	})
	Tick.dayTick:fire({ pass = Tick.passCount, tickNumber = Tick.passCount, serverTime = stampValue })
end

function Tick.periodSeconds() return Tick.cachedPeriod end

function Tick.isFresh()
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()
	if period == nil or Tick.lastPassClock == nil then
		return false, string.format("tick period not measured yet (%d passes seen)", Tick.passCount)
	end
	local sinceLast = os.clock() - Tick.lastPassClock
	local bound = period * STALE_AFTER_PERIODS
	if sinceLast > bound then
		return false, string.format("no supply pass for %.2f s (bound %.2f s = %d periods of %.3f s)", sinceLast, bound, STALE_AFTER_PERIODS, period)
	end
	return true, string.format("last pass %.2f s ago", sinceLast)
end

function Tick.attach(mapGraph, maid)
	maid:give(mapGraph.tileAttributeChanged:connect(function(tileName, attributeName, value)
		if attributeName == STAMP_ATTRIBUTE then onStamp(tileName, value) end
	end))
	Logger.info("Game.Tick", "attached", { attribute = STAMP_ATTRIBUTE })
end

function Tick.reset(reason)
	Logger.info("Game.Tick", "reset", { reason = reason, passes = Tick.passCount, intervals = #Tick.intervals })
	Tick.lastPassServerTime = nil
	Tick.lastPassClock = nil
	Tick.passCount = 0
	Tick.cachedPeriod = nil
	table.clear(Tick.intervals)
	table.clear(Tick.sortedIntervals)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Tick", Tick)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Units"] = [=[
local Units = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

local FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND = 41.652
local DEAD_STATE = "Dead"
local RETREATING_STATE = "Retreating"
local DIGITS_PATTERN = "%d"
local LEVEL_PATTERN = "%d+"

Units.mapModel = nil
Units.records = {}
Units.recordByModel = {}
Units.tileRecords = {}
Units.nextId = 0
Units.flagRecords = {}
Units.ourCountryName = nil
Units.unitAdded = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitAdded")
Units.unitMoved = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitMoved")
Units.unitRemoved = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitRemoved")
Units.unitChanged = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitChanged")

local function classAndLevel(troopName)
	local troopClass = string.gsub(troopName, DIGITS_PATTERN, "")
	local levelText = string.match(troopName, LEVEL_PATTERN)
	if levelText == nil then
		return error(string.format("Units: troop name '%s' carries no level digits", troopName))
	end
	return troopClass, tonumber(levelText)
end

local function tileList(tileName)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[tileName]
	if list == nil then list = {}; Units.tileRecords[tileName] = list end
	return list
end

local function detachFromTile(record)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[record.tile]
	if list == nil then return end
	local index = table.find(list, record)
	if index ~= nil then table.remove(list, index) end
	if #list == 0 then Units.tileRecords[record.tile] = nil end
end

local function isTroopModel(model)
	if not model:IsA("Model") then return false end
	local parent = model.Parent
	return parent ~= nil and parent.Parent == Units.mapModel and MapGraph.isLand(parent.Name)
end

local function updateFlagRecord(record, attributeName, value)
	if record.owner ~= Units.ourCountryName then return end
	local flag = Units.flagRecords[record.id]
	if attributeName == "state" and value == RETREATING_STATE and flag == nil then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = { sinceServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), tile = record.tile }
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagRecorded", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, troop = record.troopName })
		return
	end
	if attributeName == "dieOnNextRetreat" and value == true and flag == nil then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = { sinceServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), tile = record.tile }
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagRecorded", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, troop = record.troopName, origin = "live attribute" })
		return
	end
	if flag == nil then return end
	if attributeName == "dieOnNextRetreat" and value == false then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagCleared", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, origin = "live attribute false" })
		return
	end
	if attributeName == "health" and type(value) == "number" and value >= FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagCleared", {
			unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, health = value,
			bound = FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND, origin = "health reached the recorded clear bound",
		})
	end
end

local function track(model, maid, origin)
	if Units.recordByModel[model] ~= nil then return end
	local tileName = model.Parent.Name
	local troopName = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "troopName", "string")
	local troopClass, level = classAndLevel(troopName)
	local owner = Ownership.troopOwner(model)
	local predecessor = nil
	for _, existing in ipairs(tileList(tileName)) do
		if existing.owner == owner and not existing.superseded then predecessor = existing end
	end
	local unitId
	if predecessor ~= nil then
		unitId = predecessor.id
		predecessor.superseded = true
	else
		Units.nextId += 1
		unitId = Units.nextId
	end
	local record = {
		id = unitId, model = model, tile = tileName, owner = owner,
		troopName = troopName, class = troopClass, level = level,
		superseded = false, addedServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
	}
	Units.records[record] = true
	Units.recordByModel[model] = record
	table.insert(tileList(tileName), record)
	local connections = {}
	table.insert(connections, model.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attributeName)
		local value = model:GetAttribute(attributeName)
		updateFlagRecord(record, attributeName, value)
		Units.unitChanged:fire(record, attributeName, value)
	end))
	table.insert(connections, model:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
		local parent = model.Parent
		if parent ~= nil and parent.Parent == Units.mapModel and MapGraph.isLand(parent.Name) then
			local fromTile = record.tile
			detachFromTile(record)
			record.tile = parent.Name
			table.insert(tileList(parent.Name), record)
			Units.unitMoved:fire(record, fromTile, parent.Name)
			return
		end
		for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
		detachFromTile(record)
		Units.records[record] = nil
		Units.recordByModel[model] = nil
		local reason = parent == nil and "destroyed" or ("left the map for " .. parent:GetFullName())
		if not record.superseded then Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil end
		Units.unitRemoved:fire(record, record.tile, reason)
	end))
	for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do maid:give(connection) end
	if predecessor ~= nil then
		Logger.debug("Game.Units", "unitReplaced", { unit = unitId, tile = tileName, from = predecessor.troopName, to = troopName })
	end
	Units.unitAdded:fire(record, origin)
end

function Units.reset(reason)
	local tracked = 0
	for _ in pairs(Units.records) do tracked += 1 end
	table.clear(Units.records)
	table.clear(Units.recordByModel)
	table.clear(Units.tileRecords)
	table.clear(Units.flagRecords)
	Units.mapModel = nil
	Logger.info("Game.Units", "reset", { reason = reason, forgotten = tracked })
end

function Units.attach(mapModel, ourCountryName, maid)
	Units.mapModel = mapModel
	Units.ourCountryName = ourCountryName
	local tracked = 0
	for _, tileName in ipairs(MapGraph.tileOrder) do
		local tileRecord = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
		if not tileRecord.isSea then
			for _, child in ipairs(tileRecord.instance:GetChildren()) do
				if isTroopModel(child) then
					track(child, maid, "initial scan")
					tracked += 1
				end
			end
		end
	end
	maid:give(mapModel.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
		if isTroopModel(descendant) then track(descendant, maid, "DescendantAdded") end
	end))
	Logger.info("Game.Units", "attached", { tracked = tracked, ourCountry = ourCountryName })
end

function Units.setOurCountry(countryName)
	if Units.ourCountryName ~= countryName then
		table.clear(Units.flagRecords)
	end
	Units.ourCountryName = countryName
end

function Units.unitOn(tileName) return Units.liveUnitOn(tileName) end

function Units.unitsOf(countryName)
	local result = {}
	for record in pairs(Units.records) do
		if record.owner == countryName and not record.superseded and record.model:GetAttribute("state") ~= DEAD_STATE then
			table.insert(result, record)
		end
	end
	table.sort(result, function(first, second) return first.id < second.id end)
	return result
end

function Units.isFlagged(record) return Units.flaggedNow(record) end
function Units.snapshot(record) return Units.snapshotNow(record) end

function Units.stats()
	local total = 0
	local byOwner = {}
	for record in pairs(Units.records) do
		if not record.superseded then
			total += 1
			byOwner[record.owner or "?"] = (byOwner[record.owner or "?"] or 0) + 1
		end
	end
	local flagged = 0
	for _ in pairs(Units.flagRecords) do flagged += 1 end
	return { total = total, byOwner = byOwner, ourFlagRecords = flagged }
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.Units", Units)

function Units.liveUnitOn(tileName)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[tileName]
	if list == nil then return nil end
	for index = #list, 1, -1 do
		local record = list[index]
		if not record.superseded and record.model.Parent ~= nil and record.model:GetAttribute("state") ~= DEAD_STATE then
			return record
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function Units.flaggedNow(record)
	return Units.flagRecords[record.id] ~= nil or record.model:GetAttribute("dieOnNextRetreat") == true
end

function Units.snapshotNow(record)
	local model = record.model
	return {
		id = record.id, tile = record.tile, owner = record.owner,
		troopName = record.troopName, class = record.class, level = record.level, model = model,
		state = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "state", "string"),
		health = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "health", "number"),
		attack = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "attack", "number"),
		defense = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "defense", "number"),
		speed = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "speed", "number"),
		experience = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "experience", "number", "absent on a fresh Model before its first attack") or 0,
		targetTile = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "targetTile", "string", "set only while moving, waiting, attacking or retreating"),
		currentPath = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "currentPath", "string", "set only while an order has tiles left"),
		attackerCount = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "attackerCount", "number", "set only once the unit has been attacked"),
		noSupply = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "noSupply", "boolean", "written from the first supply pass on") == true,
		noMoney = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "noMoney", "boolean", "written from the first upkeep pass on") == true,
		liveFlag = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "dieOnNextRetreat", "boolean", "written from the first retreat on") == true,
		flagged = Units.flaggedNow(record),
		replenishConsumption = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "replenishConsumption", "number", "TroopCommon reads it with a default of 1"),
		replenishSuppliesName = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "replenishSuppliesName", "string", "set by the server with the base stats"),
		army = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "army", "string", "set only for members of an army group"),
	}
end

return Units
]=]

-- ===============================================================================================
-- [PORT-1] GUI/Rayfield — pengganti GUI/Linoria
-- ===============================================================================================
MODULE_SOURCES["GUI/Rayfield"] = [=[
--[[
	GUI/Rayfield

	Memuat Rayfield Gen2 dari sirius.menu dan menyimpannya sebagai singleton,
	supaya Menu tidak perlu tahu URL-nya.
]]

local Rayfield = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

local RAYFIELD_URL = "https://sirius.menu/gen2"
local CHUNK_NAME = "=RayfieldGen2"

Rayfield.instance = nil

---Download + compile Rayfield Gen2. Return tabel Rayfield.
---@return table
function Rayfield.load()
	if Rayfield.instance ~= nil then
		return Rayfield.instance
	end

	local fetchOk, body = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, RAYFIELD_URL)
	if not fetchOk then
		return error(string.format("Rayfield.load: download of '%s' failed: %s", RAYFIELD_URL, tostring(body)))
	end

	local chunk, compileError = loadstring(body, CHUNK_NAME)
	if chunk == nil then
		return error(string.format("Rayfield.load: '%s' failed to compile: %s", RAYFIELD_URL, tostring(compileError)))
	end

	local instance = chunk()
	if instance == nil then
		return error(string.format("Rayfield.load: '%s' returned nothing", RAYFIELD_URL))
	end

	Rayfield.instance = instance
	Logger.info("GUI.Rayfield", "loaded", { url = RAYFIELD_URL, bytes = #body })
	return instance
end

return Tracer.instrument("GUI.Rayfield", Rayfield)
]=]

-- ===============================================================================================
-- [PORT-2] Menu — ditulis ulang untuk Rayfield Gen2
-- ===============================================================================================
MODULE_SOURCES["Menu"] = [=[
--[[
	Menu — Rayfield Gen2 (sesuai dokumentasi resmi).

	API yang dipakai:
	- window:CreateTab({ name = "..." })
	- tab:CreateSection({ name = "..." })
	- tab:CreateToggle({ name, default, callback })
	- tab:CreateSlider({ name, default, min, max, step, callback })
	- tab:CreateDropdown({ name, options, default, callback })
	- tab:CreateButton({ name, callback })
	- tab:CreateText({ name, text })     <- untuk status (BUKAN CreateParagraph/CreateLabel)
	- toggle:Set(value)                   <- set toggle dari kode
	- text:Set(body) / text:SetTitle(title)
]]

local Menu = {}
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Rayfield = require("GUI/Rayfield")

local WINDOW_TITLE = "CTW War AI"
local WINDOW_SUBTITLE = "AI front & economy"
local STATUS_TAB_NAME = "Status"
local UI_SETTINGS_TAB_NAME = "UI Settings"
local SLIDER_STEP_BASE = 10
local HIDDEN_TABS = { Debug = true }
local FORCE_OFF_KEYS = { ["ai.enabled"] = true, ["ai.sendOrders"] = true }

local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

Menu.rayfield = nil
Menu.window = nil
Menu.unloading = false
Menu.toggleRefs = {}
Menu.pendingStatus = {}
Menu.statusStore = {}
Menu.statusText = nil
Menu.lastFlush = 0
Menu.lastBlob = nil

local function createSection(tab, title)
	for _, attempt in ipairs({
		function() return tab:CreateSection({ name = title }) end,
		function() return tab:CreateSection({ Name = title }) end,
		function() return tab:CreateSection(title) end,
	}) do
		if pcall(attempt) then return end
	end
end

---Bangun status widget pakai CreateText (resmi Rayfield Gen2).
local function buildStatusArea(tab)
	local placeholder = "Menunggu siklus pertama..."

	local ok, textHandle = pcall(function()
		return tab:CreateText({ name = "AI state", text = placeholder })
	end)
	if ok and textHandle ~= nil then
		Menu.statusText = textHandle
		print("[CTW_WarAI] status widget: CreateText OK")
		return
	end

	-- Fallback: Console (kalau CreateText tidak ada di build tertentu).
	local ok2, consoleHandle = pcall(function()
		return tab:CreateConsole({ name = "AI state", height = 180, follow = true, maxLines = 500 })
	end)
	if ok2 and consoleHandle ~= nil then
		Menu.statusText = consoleHandle
		print("[CTW_WarAI] status widget: CreateConsole fallback")
		return
	end

	Menu.statusText = nil
	print("[CTW_WarAI] status widget: none -> console only")
end

local function roundingToStep(rounding)
	rounding = tonumber(rounding) or 0
	if rounding <= 0 then return 1 end
	return 1 / (SLIDER_STEP_BASE ^ rounding)
end

local function makeCallback(definition)
	return function(value)
		Settings.set(definition.key, value, "menu")
	end
end

function Menu.addControl(tab, definition)
	local onValue = makeCallback(definition)

	if definition.kind == "toggle" then
		local ok, toggle = pcall(function()
			return tab:CreateToggle({ name = definition.label, default = definition.default, callback = onValue })
		end)
		if ok and toggle ~= nil then
			Menu.toggleRefs[definition.key] = toggle
		end
		return
	end
	if definition.kind == "slider" then
		pcall(function()
			tab:CreateSlider({
				name = string.format("%s (%s)", definition.label, definition.unit),
				default = definition.default, min = definition.min, max = definition.max,
				step = roundingToStep(definition.rounding), callback = onValue,
			})
		end)
		return
	end
	if definition.kind == "dropdown" then
		pcall(function()
			tab:CreateDropdown({
				name = definition.label, options = definition.choices,
				default = definition.default, callback = onValue,
			})
		end)
		return
	end
end

local function notify(title, content)
	local rayfield = Menu.rayfield
	if rayfield == nil then return end
	for _, attempt in ipairs({
		function() return rayfield:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = 5 }) end,
		function() return rayfield:Notify({ title = title, content = content, duration = 5 }) end,
		function() return rayfield:Notify(title, content, 5) end,
	}) do
		if pcall(attempt) then return end
	end
end

---Set toggle pakai method resmi :Set(value).
local function setToggle(toggle, value)
	if toggle == nil then return end
	local ok = pcall(function() toggle:Set(value) end)
	if not ok then
		-- Fallback ke :SetValue / properti.
		if type(toggle.SetValue) == "function" then
			pcall(function() toggle:SetValue(value) end)
		end
		pcall(function() toggle.Value = value end)
	end
end

function Menu.build(_, onUnload)
	Menu.rayfield = Rayfield.load()
	local rayfield = Menu.rayfield

	local window = rayfield:CreateWindow({
		name = WINDOW_TITLE,
		subtitle = WINDOW_SUBTITLE,
		sidebarLayout = true,
	})
	Menu.window = window

	-- Status tab
	local statusTab = window:CreateTab({ name = STATUS_TAB_NAME })
	createSection(statusTab, "AI state")
	buildStatusArea(statusTab)

	-- Tab dari Settings.layout(), kecuali Debug
	for _, tabLayout in ipairs(Settings.layout()) do
		if not HIDDEN_TABS[tabLayout.name] then
			local tab = window:CreateTab({ name = tabLayout.name })
			for _, section in ipairs(tabLayout.sections) do
				createSection(tab, section.name)
				for _, definition in ipairs(section.definitions) do
					Menu.addControl(tab, definition)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- UI Settings
	local uiTab = window:CreateTab({ name = UI_SETTINGS_TAB_NAME })
	createSection(uiTab, "Menu")
	pcall(function()
		uiTab:CreateButton({
			name = "Unload CTW War AI",
			callback = function()
				notify("CTW War AI", "Unloading...")
				pcall(onUnload)
			end,
		})
	end)

	-- Force OFF untuk ai.enabled + ai.sendOrders, ON untuk sisanya.
	local onCount, offCount = 0, 0
	for key, toggle in pairs(Menu.toggleRefs) do
		local want = not FORCE_OFF_KEYS[key]
		Settings.set(key, want, "auto-boot")
		setToggle(toggle, want)
		if want then onCount += 1 else offCount += 1 end
	end

	-- Retry setelah 0.5s untuk handle yang di-init asinkron.
	task.delay(0.5, function()
		for key, toggle in pairs(Menu.toggleRefs) do
			setToggle(toggle, not FORCE_OFF_KEYS[key])
		end
	end)

	notify("CTW War AI", string.format("Loaded. %d ON, %d OFF.", onCount, offCount))
end

function Menu.setStatus(key, text)
	Menu.pendingStatus[key] = text
end

function Menu.applyPending()
	if next(Menu.pendingStatus) == nil then return end
	for k, v in pairs(Menu.pendingStatus) do
		Menu.statusStore[k] = v
	end
	table.clear(Menu.pendingStatus)

	local keys = {}
	for k in pairs(Menu.statusStore) do table.insert(keys, k) end
	table.sort(keys)
	local lines = {}
	for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
		table.insert(lines, k .. ": " .. tostring(Menu.statusStore[k]))
	end
	local blob = table.concat(lines, "\n")

	-- Update widget HANYA kalau blob berubah (hemat Rayfield :Set yang mahal)
	if Menu.statusText ~= nil and Menu.lastBlob ~= blob then
		Menu.lastBlob = blob
		local ok = pcall(function() Menu.statusText:Set(blob) end)
		if not ok then
			pcall(function() Menu.statusText:Append(blob) end)
		end
	end
	-- Tidak ada console mirror lagi — log sudah ke file via Logger.
end

function Menu.startPump()
	return task.spawn(function()
		while Menu.window ~= nil do
			task.wait(0.25)  -- 4 Hz, bukan setiap Heartbeat
			local ok = xpcall(Menu.applyPending, function(failure)
				Logger.error("Menu", "statusPumpFailed", { message = tostring(failure) })
				return failure
			end)
			if not ok then break end
		end
	end)
end

local function tryUnloadRayfield(rayfield, window)
	-- 1) Method resmi Rayfield (kalau ada).
	if rayfield ~= nil then
		if type(rayfield.Unload) == "function" then
			local ok = pcall(function() rayfield:Unload() end)
			if ok then return true, "rayfield:Unload" end
		end
		if type(rayfield.Destroy) == "function" then
			local ok = pcall(function() rayfield:Destroy() end)
			if ok then return true, "rayfield:Destroy" end
		end
	end

	-- 2) window.screenGui (field internal Rayfield).
	if window ~= nil then
		local ok, sg = pcall(function() return window.screenGui end)
		if ok and sg ~= nil and typeof(sg) == "Instance" and sg.Parent ~= nil then
			pcall(function() window:Destroy() end)
			pcall(function() window:Close() end)
			pcall(function() sg:Destroy() end)
			return true, "window.screenGui destroyed"
		end
	end

	-- 3) Fallback: scan SEMUA ScreenGui di CoreGui + PlayerGui.
	--    Marker yang dipakai: ada TextLabel dengan tulisan tepat "CTW War AI" persis judul window kita.
	local destroyedNames = {}

	local function containsOurWindow(gui)
		if not gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then return false end
		-- Jangan destroy UI lain yang kebetulan ada (AetherUI, dsb).
		if gui.Name == "AetherUI" then return false end
		for _, desc in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
			if desc:IsA("TextLabel") and desc.Text == "CTW War AI" then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local containers = {
		game:GetService("CoreGui"),
		game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"),
	}

	for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
		if container ~= nil then
			for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
				if containsOurWindow(child) then
					table.insert(destroyedNames, child.Name)
					pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if #destroyedNames > 0 then
		return true, string.format("destroyed %d ScreenGui(s): %s",
			#destroyedNames, table.concat(destroyedNames, ", "))
	end

	return false, "no Rayfield ScreenGui matched"
end

function Menu.destroy()
	if Menu.window == nil and Menu.rayfield == nil then return end
	if not Menu.unloading then
		Menu.unloading = true
		local ok, how = tryUnloadRayfield(Menu.rayfield, Menu.window)
		print("[CTW_WarAI] rayfield unload:", ok, how or "(no detail)")
	end
	Menu.window = nil
	Menu.rayfield = nil
	Menu.statusText = nil
	Menu.advisorText = nil
	table.clear(Menu.toggleRefs)
	table.clear(Menu.pendingStatus)
	table.clear(Menu.statusStore)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Menu", Menu)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/FileSink"] = [=[
local FileSink = {}
FileSink.__index = FileSink
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

function FileSink.new(filePath)
	writefile(filePath, "")
	if not isfile(filePath) then
		return error(string.format("FileSink.new: file '%s' could not be created", filePath))
	end
	local self = setmetatable({}, FileSink)
	self.filePath = filePath
	self.buffer = {}
	self.recordsWritten = 0
	self.bytesWritten = 0
	self.flushCount = 0
	return self
end

function FileSink:push(record)
	table.insert(self.buffer, Serializer.encode(record))
end

function FileSink:flush()
	if #self.buffer == 0 then return end
	local chunk = table.concat(self.buffer, "\n") .. "\n"
	appendfile(self.filePath, chunk)
	self.recordsWritten += #self.buffer
	self.bytesWritten += #chunk
	self.flushCount += 1
	table.clear(self.buffer)
end

function FileSink:stats()
	return {
		filePath = self.filePath, recordsWritten = self.recordsWritten,
		bytesWritten = self.bytesWritten, flushCount = self.flushCount,
		buffered = #self.buffer,
	}
end

return FileSink
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/FileSink"] = [=[
local FileSink = {}
FileSink.__index = FileSink
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

function FileSink.new(filePath)
	writefile(filePath, "")
	if not isfile(filePath) then
		return error(string.format("FileSink.new: file '%s' could not be created", filePath))
	end
	local self = setmetatable({}, FileSink)
	self.filePath = filePath
	self.buffer = {}
	self.recordsWritten = 0
	self.bytesWritten = 0
	self.flushCount = 0
	return self
end

function FileSink:push(record)
	table.insert(self.buffer, Serializer.encode(record))
end

function FileSink:flush()
	if #self.buffer == 0 then return end
	local chunk = table.concat(self.buffer, "\n") .. "\n"
	appendfile(self.filePath, chunk)
	self.recordsWritten += #self.buffer
	self.bytesWritten += #chunk
	self.flushCount += 1
	table.clear(self.buffer)
end

function FileSink:stats()
	return {
		filePath = self.filePath, recordsWritten = self.recordsWritten,
		bytesWritten = self.bytesWritten, flushCount = self.flushCount,
		buffered = #self.buffer,
	}
end

return FileSink
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Formula"] = [=[
local Formula = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

local ALLOWED_TIERS = { T0 = true, T1 = true, T2 = true, T3 = true, T4 = true, T5 = true }

Formula.definitions = {}
Formula.logEveryEvaluation = false
Formula.loggedByTrace = {}
Formula.suppressedByTrace = {}

function Formula.define(definition)
	assert(definition.id, "Formula.define: id is required")
	assert(ALLOWED_TIERS[definition.tier], string.format("Formula.define: '%s' has no allowed derivation tier", tostring(definition.id)))
	assert(definition.source, "Formula.define: source of the inputs is required")
	assert(definition.expression, "Formula.define: symbolic expression is required")
	assert(definition.unit, "Formula.define: output unit is required")
	assert(definition.inputs, "Formula.define: input units are required")
	assert(definition.compute, "Formula.define: compute function is required")
	assert(definition.examples and #definition.examples > 0, "Formula.define: at least one worked example is required")
	if Formula.definitions[definition.id] then
		return error(string.format("Formula.define: '%s' is already defined", definition.id))
	end
	Formula.definitions[definition.id] = definition
	for exampleIndex, example in ipairs(definition.examples) do
		local produced = definition.compute(example.inputs)
		local passed
		if type(example.expected) == "number" then
			local tolerance = example.tolerance
			if tolerance == nil then tolerance = 0 end
			passed = type(produced) == "number" and (produced == example.expected or math.abs(produced - example.expected) <= tolerance)
		else
			passed = produced == example.expected
		end
		Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "example", {
			id = definition.id, expression = definition.expression,
			exampleIndex = exampleIndex, inputs = example.inputs,
			expected = example.expected, produced = produced,
			unit = definition.unit, passed = passed,
		})
		if not passed then
			return error(string.format(
				"Formula '%s' example %d drifted: expected %s, produced %s (%s)",
				definition.id, exampleIndex, tostring(example.expected),
				tostring(produced), definition.unit
			))
		end
	end
	return definition
end

function Formula.flushSuppressed(traceId)
	local suppressed = Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId]
	if suppressed ~= nil then
		Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "suppressedEvaluations", {
			traceId = traceId, counts = suppressed,
			reason = "first evaluation per formula and trace is logged in full; enable formula.logEveryEvaluation to log all",
		})
	end
	Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId] = nil
	Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId] = nil
end

function Formula.describeRejected(candidates, winner, context)
	local rejected = {}
	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		if candidate ~= winner then
			table.insert(rejected, {
				id = candidate.id,
				reason = candidate.rejectReason and candidate.rejectReason(context) or "applies() returned false or ranked lower",
			})
		end
	end
	return rejected
end

function Formula.select(decisionName, candidates, context)
	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		if candidate.applies(context) then
			Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "branch", {
				decision = decisionName, chosen = candidate.id, reason = candidate.reason,
				rejected = Formula.describeRejected(candidates, candidate, context), context = context,
			})
			return candidate
		end
	end
	return error(string.format("Formula.select: no candidate applies for '%s'", decisionName))
end

function Formula.recordChoice(decisionName, ranked, decidingFact)
	if #ranked == 0 then
		return error(string.format("Formula.recordChoice: '%s' has no candidates", decisionName))
	end
	local losers = {}
	for index, entry in ipairs(ranked) do
		if index > 1 then
			table.insert(losers, { id = entry.id, score = entry.score, facts = entry.facts })
		end
	end
	Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "branch", {
		decision = decisionName, chosen = ranked[1].id, chosenScore = ranked[1].score,
		chosenFacts = ranked[1].facts, rejected = losers, reason = decidingFact,
	})
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Formula", Formula)

function Formula.evaluate(id, inputs)
	local definition = Formula.definitions[id]
	if not definition then
		return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: unknown formula '%s'", id))
	end
	for inputName in pairs(inputs) do
		if not definition.inputs[inputName] then
			return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: '%s' received undeclared input '%s'", id, inputName))
		end
	end
	for inputName in pairs(definition.inputs) do
		if inputs[inputName] == nil then
			return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: '%s' is missing input '%s'", id, inputName))
		end
	end
	local value = definition.compute(inputs)
	if Logger.accepts("DEBUG") then
		local traceId = "none"
		if Logger.contextProvider then traceId = Logger.contextProvider() end
		local logged = Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId]
		if logged == nil then
			logged = {}
			Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId] = logged
		end
		if Formula.logEveryEvaluation or not logged[id] then
			logged[id] = true
			Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "formula", {
				id = id, expression = definition.expression, tier = definition.tier,
				source = definition.source, inputs = inputs, inputUnits = definition.inputs,
				output = value, unit = definition.unit,
			})
		else
			local suppressed = Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId]
			if suppressed == nil then
				suppressed = {}
				Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId] = suppressed
			end
			suppressed[id] = (suppressed[id] or 0) + 1
		end
	end
	return value
end

return Formula
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Logger"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Logger — file-buffered + rate-limited (anti-lag).

	KENAPA LAG: setiap log call membangun string payload lewat flatten()
	(iterasi table + concat). Kalau 10-15 log per detik, itu 10-15
	alokasi string/tabel per detik → GC pressure → ngelag.

	SOLUSI:
	1. Default level = WARN. INFO/DEBUG tidak dibangun sama sekali.
	2. Dedup per-event: event yang sama hanya di-log 1x per 5 detik.
	3. Buffer 500 baris + flush tiap 5 detik (bukan 200/2s).
	4. Perbandingan level pakai integer, bukan tabel lookup.

	Kalau perlu detail untuk debugging, set Logger.activeLevel = "INFO".
	Kalau perlu tiap kejadian, set Logger.dedupInterval = 30.
]]

local Logger = {}

Logger.LEVELS = { "TRACE", "DEBUG", "INFO", "WARN", "ERROR" }
Logger.activeLevel = "INFO"           -- [DEBUG] sementara untuk analisis
Logger.counts = nil
Logger.lastByLevel = {}
Logger.sinks = {}
Logger.pendingRecords = {}
Logger.contextProvider = nil

-- Level thresholds (integer)
local TRACE_LVL, DEBUG_LVL, INFO_LVL, WARN_LVL, ERROR_LVL = 1, 2, 3, 4, 5
local LEVEL_RANK = { TRACE = TRACE_LVL, DEBUG = DEBUG_LVL, INFO = INFO_LVL, WARN = WARN_LVL, ERROR = ERROR_LVL }
-- [FIX] activeRank otomatis ikut activeLevel; tidak bisa lupa sinkron lagi
Logger.activeRank = LEVEL_RANK[Logger.activeLevel] or INFO_LVL

-- File sink config
Logger.filePath = "CTW_WarAI_log.txt"
Logger.maxBuffer = 500
Logger.flushInterval = 5.0
Logger.dedupInterval = 0              -- [DEBUG] matikan dedup
Logger.buffer = {}
Logger.bufferSize = 0
Logger.lastFlush = 0
Logger.fileOk = false
Logger.fallbackToPrint = false
Logger.flusherRunning = false

-- Deteksi dukungan executor
local hasWriteFile = type(writefile) == "function"
local hasAppendFile = type(appendfile) == "function"

if hasWriteFile then
	local ok = pcall(writefile, Logger.filePath, "")
	Logger.fileOk = ok
end
if not Logger.fileOk then
	Logger.fallbackToPrint = true
end

local function accepts(rank) return rank >= Logger.activeRank end

function Logger.accepts(level)
	local rank = ({TRACE=1, DEBUG=2, INFO=3, WARN=4, ERROR=5})[level] or 5
	return rank >= Logger.activeRank
end

function Logger.setLevel(level)
	Logger.activeLevel = level
	Logger.activeRank = ({TRACE=1, DEBUG=2, INFO=3, WARN=4, ERROR=5})[level] or 4
end

function Logger.addSink() end
function Logger.removeSink() end

-- [OPTIMASI] Dedup: event sama dari modul sama hanya di-log 1x per interval.
-- Key = module|event|tile (kalau payload punya .tile).
local recentEmits = {}
local function shouldEmit(moduleName, eventName, payload)
	if Logger.dedupInterval <= 0 then return true end
	local key = moduleName .. "|" .. eventName
	if type(payload) == "table" and payload.tile ~= nil then
		key = key .. "|" .. tostring(payload.tile)
	end
	local now = os.clock()
	local last = recentEmits[key]
	if last ~= nil and now - last < Logger.dedupInterval then
		return false
	end
	recentEmits[key] = now
	return true
end

-- [OPTIMASI] flatten lebih murah: hindari string.format, batasi depth.
local function flatten(payload, depth)
	depth = depth or 0
	if depth > 1 then return "..." end
	local t = type(payload)
	if t ~= "table" then return tostring(payload) end
	local parts = {}
	local n = 0
	for k, v in pairs(payload) do
		n = n + 1
		if n > 12 then parts[#parts + 1] = "..."; break end
		local vt = type(v)
		if vt == "table" then
			parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(k) .. "=" .. flatten(v, depth + 1)
		else
			parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(k) .. "=" .. tostring(v)
		end
	end
	return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. "}"
end

local function flushBuffer()
	if Logger.bufferSize == 0 then return end
	local chunk = table.concat(Logger.buffer, "\n") .. "\n"

	if Logger.fileOk and hasAppendFile then
		local ok = pcall(appendfile, Logger.filePath, chunk)
		if not ok then
			pcall(writefile, Logger.filePath, chunk)
		end
	elseif Logger.fallbackToPrint then
		print(chunk)
	end

	table.clear(Logger.buffer)
	Logger.bufferSize = 0
	Logger.lastFlush = os.clock()
end

function Logger.flush() flushBuffer() end
function Logger.write(...) Logger.info(...) end

local function emit(prefix, moduleName, eventName, payload)
	local line = "[CTW_WarAI][" .. prefix .. "] " .. moduleName .. " / " .. eventName
	if payload ~= nil then
		line = line .. " " .. flatten(payload)
	end
	Logger.buffer[#Logger.buffer + 1] = line
	Logger.bufferSize = Logger.bufferSize + 1
	if Logger.bufferSize >= Logger.maxBuffer then
		flushBuffer()
	elseif (os.clock() - Logger.lastFlush) >= Logger.flushInterval then
		flushBuffer()
	end
end

-- Background flusher
if not Logger.flusherRunning then
	Logger.flusherRunning = true
	task.spawn(function()
		while true do
			task.wait(Logger.flushInterval)
			pcall(flushBuffer)
		end
	end)
end

-- [OPTIMASI] Perbandingan level pakai integer langsung, bukan tabel.
function Logger.trace(m, e, p)
	if accepts(TRACE_LVL) and shouldEmit(m, e, p) then emit("TRACE", m, e, p) end
end
function Logger.debug(m, e, p)
	if accepts(DEBUG_LVL) and shouldEmit(m, e, p) then emit("DEBUG", m, e, p) end
end
function Logger.info(m, e, p)
	if accepts(INFO_LVL) and shouldEmit(m, e, p) then emit("INFO", m, e, p) end
end
function Logger.warn(m, e, p)
	if accepts(WARN_LVL) and shouldEmit(m, e, p) then emit("WARN", m, e, p) end
end
function Logger.error(m, e, p)
	-- ERROR selalu emit (tidak di-dedup), supaya tidak ada yang hilang
	if accepts(ERROR_LVL) then emit("ERROR", m, e, p) end
end

return Logger
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Maid"] = [=[
local Maid = {}
Maid.__index = Maid
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

function Maid.new(ownerName)
	local self = setmetatable({}, Maid)
	self.ownerName = ownerName
	self.tasks = {}
	return self
end

function Maid:clean()
	local releasedCounts = {}
	local errors = {}
	for index = #self.tasks, 1, -1 do
		local ownedTask = self.tasks[index]
		local taskType = typeof(ownedTask)
		-- Bungkus pcall supaya error di satu task tidak membatalkan task lainnya.
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			if taskType == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				ownedTask:Disconnect()
			elseif taskType == "thread" then
				if coroutine.status(ownedTask) ~= "dead" then
					task.cancel(ownedTask)
				end
			elseif taskType == "Instance" then
				ownedTask:Destroy()
			elseif taskType == "function" then
				ownedTask()
			elseif type(ownedTask) == "table" and type(ownedTask.Destroy) == "function" then
				ownedTask:Destroy()
			end
		end)
		if not ok then
			table.insert(errors, tostring(err))
		end
		releasedCounts[taskType] = (releasedCounts[taskType] or 0) + 1
	end
	table.clear(self.tasks)
	if #errors > 0 then
		print("[Maid] clean errors:", table.concat(errors, " | "))
	end
	return releasedCounts
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Maid", Maid)

function Maid:give(ownedTask)
	local taskType = typeof(ownedTask)
	local supported = taskType == "RBXScriptConnection"
		or taskType == "thread" or taskType == "Instance" or taskType == "function"
		or (taskType == "table" and type(ownedTask.Destroy) == "function")
	if not supported then
		return error(string.format("Maid:give(%s): unsupported task type '%s'", self.ownerName, taskType))
	end
	table.insert(self.tasks, ownedTask)
	return ownedTask
end

function Maid:count() return #self.tasks end

return Maid
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Profiler"] = [=[
local Profiler = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX = 2
Profiler.stats = {}

function Profiler.run(label, callee, ...)
	local startClock = os.clock()
	local results = table.pack(pcall(callee, ...))
	local durationSeconds = os.clock() - startClock
	local labelStats = Profiler.stats[label]
	if labelStats == nil then
		labelStats = { count = 0, totalSeconds = 0, maxSeconds = 0, lastSeconds = 0, failures = 0 }
		Profiler.stats[label] = labelStats
	end
	labelStats.count += 1
	labelStats.totalSeconds += durationSeconds
	labelStats.lastSeconds = durationSeconds
	labelStats.maxSeconds = math.max(labelStats.maxSeconds, durationSeconds)
	if not results[1] then labelStats.failures += 1 end
	if Logger.accepts("DEBUG") then
		Logger.debug("Utility.Profiler", "profile", {
			label = label, durationSeconds = durationSeconds, succeeded = results[1],
		})
	end
	if not results[1] then
		return error(results[PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX], 0)
	end
	return table.unpack(results, PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX, results.n)
end

function Profiler.snapshot()
	local copy = {}
	for label, labelStats in pairs(Profiler.stats) do
		copy[label] = table.clone(labelStats)
	end
	return copy
end

return Profiler
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Resolver"] = [=[
local Resolver = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

Resolver.cache = {}
Resolver.cacheHits = 0
Resolver.cacheMisses = 0

function Resolver.requireService(serviceName)
	local serviceOk, service = pcall(game.GetService, game, serviceName)
	if not serviceOk or service == nil then
		return error(string.format("Resolver: service '%s' unavailable: %s", serviceName, tostring(service)))
	end
	return service
end

function Resolver.requireDescendant(root, path)
	local cacheKey = root:GetFullName() .. "|" .. path
	local cached = Resolver.cache[cacheKey]
	if cached ~= nil and cached:IsDescendantOf(game) then
		Resolver.cacheHits += 1
		return cached
	end
	Resolver.cacheMisses += 1
	local current = root
	for _, segment in ipairs(string.split(path, "/")) do
		local nextInstance = current:FindFirstChild(segment)
		if nextInstance == nil then
			return error(string.format("Resolver: missing '%s' under '%s' (stopped at '%s')", path, root:GetFullName(), current:GetFullName()))
		end
		current = nextInstance
	end
	Resolver.cache[cacheKey] = current
	Logger.debug("Utility.Resolver", "resolve", {
		root = root:GetFullName(), path = path,
		className = current.ClassName, cachedPreviously = cached ~= nil,
	})
	return current
end

function Resolver.awaitChild(parent, childName, timeoutSeconds, timeoutSource)
	local existing = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if existing ~= nil then return existing end
	local arrivedSignal = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local arrived = nil
	local connection = parent.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == childName then
			arrived = child
			arrivedSignal:Fire()
		end
	end)
	local timeoutThread = task.delay(timeoutSeconds, function() arrivedSignal:Fire() end)
	arrivedSignal.Event:Wait()
	connection:Disconnect()
	if coroutine.status(timeoutThread) == "suspended" then
		task.cancel(timeoutThread)
	end
	arrivedSignal:Destroy()
	if arrived == nil then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: '%s' did not appear under '%s' within %s s (timeout from %s)",
			childName, parent:GetFullName(), tostring(timeoutSeconds), timeoutSource
		))
	end
	return arrived
end

function Resolver.invalidate(reason)
	local droppedCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(Resolver.cache) do droppedCount += 1 end
	table.clear(Resolver.cache)
	Logger.info("Utility.Resolver", "invalidated", { reason = reason, dropped = droppedCount })
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Resolver", Resolver)

function Resolver.requireChild(parent, childName)
	local child = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if child == nil then
		return error(string.format("Resolver: missing '%s' under '%s'", childName, parent:GetFullName()))
	end
	return child
end

function Resolver.optionalChild(parent, childName, absenceReason)
	assert(type(absenceReason) == "string" and absenceReason ~= "", "Resolver.optionalChild: absenceReason is required")
	return parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
end

function Resolver.requireAttribute(instance, attributeName, expectedType)
	local value = instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)
	if typeof(value) ~= expectedType then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: attribute '%s' on '%s' is %s, expected %s",
			attributeName, instance:GetFullName(), typeof(value), expectedType
		))
	end
	return value
end

function Resolver.optionalAttribute(instance, attributeName, expectedType, absenceReason)
	assert(type(absenceReason) == "string" and absenceReason ~= "", "Resolver.optionalAttribute: absenceReason is required")
	local value = instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)
	if value ~= nil and typeof(value) ~= expectedType then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: attribute '%s' on '%s' is %s, expected %s or absent (%s)",
			attributeName, instance:GetFullName(), typeof(value), expectedType, absenceReason
		))
	end
	return value
end

return Resolver
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Serializer"] = [=[
local Serializer = {}
local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

function Serializer.toJsonSafe(value, seen)
	local valueType = typeof(value)
	if valueType == "nil" or valueType == "boolean" then return value end
	if valueType == "number" then
		if value ~= value then return { ["$number"] = "NaN" } end
		if value == math.huge then return { ["$number"] = "Infinity" } end
		if value == -math.huge then return { ["$number"] = "-Infinity" } end
		return value
	end
	if valueType == "string" then
		if utf8.len(value) then return value end
		local hexBytes = string.gsub(value, ".", function(character)
			return string.format("%02x", string.byte(character))
		end)
		return { ["$bytesHex"] = hexBytes }
	end
	if valueType == "Instance" then
		return { ["$instance"] = value:GetFullName(), ["$class"] = value.ClassName }
	end
	if valueType == "Vector3" then return { ["$Vector3"] = { value.X, value.Y, value.Z } } end
	if valueType == "Vector2" then return { ["$Vector2"] = { value.X, value.Y } } end
	if valueType == "CFrame" then return { ["$CFrame"] = { value:GetComponents() } } end
	if valueType == "Color3" then return { ["$Color3"] = { value.R, value.G, value.B } } end
	if valueType == "BrickColor" then return { ["$BrickColor"] = value.Name } end
	if valueType == "EnumItem" then return { ["$Enum"] = tostring(value) } end
	if valueType == "UDim2" then
		return { ["$UDim2"] = { value.X.Scale, value.X.Offset, value.Y.Scale, value.Y.Offset } }
	end
	if valueType == "function" then
		local sourceName, lineNumber, functionName = debug.info(value, "sln")
		return { ["$function"] = { source = sourceName, line = lineNumber, name = functionName } }
	end
	if valueType == "table" then
		if seen[value] then return { ["$cycle"] = true } end
		seen[value] = true
		local keyCount = 0
		for _ in pairs(value) do keyCount += 1 end
		local isArray = keyCount == #value
		if isArray then
			for index = 1, #value do
				if value[index] == nil then isArray = false; break end
			end
		end
		local converted = {}
		if isArray then
			for index = 1, #value do
				converted[index] = Serializer.toJsonSafe(value[index], seen)
			end
		else
			for key, entry in pairs(value) do
				local keyText = type(key) == "string" and key or ("[" .. typeof(key) .. "]" .. tostring(key))
				converted[keyText] = Serializer.toJsonSafe(entry, seen)
			end
		end
		seen[value] = nil
		return converted
	end
	return { ["$" .. valueType] = tostring(value) }
end

function Serializer.encode(value)
	return httpService:JSONEncode(Serializer.toJsonSafe(value, {}))
end

function Serializer.decode(text, sourceName)
	local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(httpService.JSONDecode, httpService, text)
	if not decodeOk then
		return error(string.format("Serializer.decode: '%s' is not valid JSON: %s", sourceName, tostring(decoded)))
	end
	return decoded
end

return Serializer
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/IntelManager"] = [=[
local IntelManager = {}
IntelManager.__index = IntelManager

function IntelManager.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, IntelManager)
	self.sightings = {}
	self.threatMap = {}
	self.forceEstimate = {}
	self.pattern = { aggressiveness = 0.5, buildPhase = false, lastUnitCount = 0, unitGrowthRate = 0 }
	self.fogMemory = {}
	self.SIGHTING_EXPIRY = 45
	self.SIGHTING_LONG_EXPIRY = 180
	self.REGION_SIZE = 6
	self.THREAT_DECAY = 0.92
	self.MIN_THREAT = 0.01
	self.lastScanTime = 0
	self.SCAN_INTERVAL = 1.0
	self.lastPatternUpdate = 0
	self.PATTERN_INTERVAL = 5.0
	return self
end

function IntelManager:regionKey(tx, tz)
	return math.floor(tx / self.REGION_SIZE) .. "," .. math.floor(tz / self.REGION_SIZE)
end

function IntelManager:regionCenter(key)
	local rx, rz = key:match("(-?%d+),(-?%d+)")
	rx, rz = tonumber(rx), tonumber(rz)
	return rx * self.REGION_SIZE + 3, rz * self.REGION_SIZE + 3
end

function IntelManager:unitThreatValue(typeId)
	local tv = {
		[1] = 1.0, [2] = 1.5, [3] = 3.0, [4] = 2.0,
		[5] = 0.8, [6] = 1.2, [7] = 4.0, [8] = 2.5, [9] = 1.8,
	}
	return tv[typeId] or 1.0
end

function IntelManager:isInOurVision(tx, tz)
	if not warState or not warState.ourUnits then return false end
	local VR = 18
	for _, u in pairs(warState.ourUnits) do
		if u and u.Health > 0 then
			local ux, uz = getUnitTile(u)
			if distance(ux, uz, tx, tz) <= VR then return true end
		end
	end
	return false
end

function IntelManager:getVisibleEnemies()
	local list = {}
	if not warState or not warState.units then return list end
	for uid, u in pairs(warState.units) do
		if u.NationId ~= ourNationId and u.Health > 0 then
			local tx, tz = getUnitTile(u)
			if self:isInOurVision(tx, tz) then
				table.insert(list, {
					unitId = uid, tileX = tx, tileZ = tz,
					typeId = u.TypeId, hp = u.Health,
					maxHp = u.MaxHealth, nationId = u.NationId,
				})
			end
		end
	end
	return list
end

function IntelManager:scan()
	local now = tick()
	if now - self.lastScanTime < self.SCAN_INTERVAL then return end
	self.lastScanTime = now

	for _, e in ipairs(self:getVisibleEnemies()) do
		local old = self.sightings[e.unitId]
		if old then
			old.lastSeen = now
			old.tileX = e.tileX
			old.tileZ = e.tileZ
			old.hp = e.hp
			old.isStale = false
		else
			self.sightings[e.unitId] = {
				firstSeen = now, lastSeen = now,
				tileX = e.tileX, tileZ = e.tileZ,
				typeId = e.typeId, hp = e.hp, maxHp = e.maxHp,
				isStale = false, staleness = 0,
			}
		end
	end

	for uid, s in pairs(self.sightings) do
		local age = now - s.lastSeen
		if age > self.SIGHTING_LONG_EXPIRY then
			self.sightings[uid] = nil
		else
			s.isStale = age > self.SIGHTING_EXPIRY
			s.staleness = math.min(age / self.SIGHTING_EXPIRY, 1.0)
		end
	end

	self:rebuildThreatMap()
	self:estimateForces()
	self:updatePattern()
end

function IntelManager:rebuildThreatMap()
	local now = tick()

	for k, r in pairs(self.threatMap) do
		r.score = r.score * self.THREAT_DECAY
		r.enemyCount = r.enemyCount * self.THREAT_DECAY
		if r.score < self.MIN_THREAT then self.threatMap[k] = nil end
	end

	for uid, s in pairs(self.sightings) do
		local k = self:regionKey(s.tileX, s.tileZ)
		if not self.threatMap[k] then
			self.threatMap[k] = {score = 0, enemyCount = 0, friendlyCount = 0, lastUpdate = now}
		end
		local r = self.threatMap[k]
		local w = s.isStale and (1.0 - s.staleness * 0.7) or 1.0
		r.score = r.score + self:unitThreatValue(s.typeId) * w
		r.enemyCount = r.enemyCount + w
		r.lastUpdate = now
	end

	if warState and warState.ourUnits then
		for _, u in pairs(warState.ourUnits) do
			if u and u.Health > 0 then
				local ux, uz = getUnitTile(u)
				local k = self:regionKey(ux, uz)
				if not self.threatMap[k] then
					self.threatMap[k] = {score = 0, enemyCount = 0, friendlyCount = 0, lastUpdate = now}
				end
				self.threatMap[k].friendlyCount = self.threatMap[k].friendlyCount + 1
			end
		end
	end
end

function IntelManager:estimateForces()
	local counts = {}
	for uid, s in pairs(self.sightings) do
		if not s.isStale then
			counts[s.typeId] = (counts[s.typeId] or 0) + 1
		end
	end

	for tid, count in pairs(counts) do
		if not self.forceEstimate[tid] then
			self.forceEstimate[tid] = {estimated = count, peak = count, trend = 0}
		end
		local e = self.forceEstimate[tid]
		e.peak = math.max(e.peak, count)
		e.estimated = math.max(count, math.floor(e.peak * 1.5))
	end

	local total = 0
	for _, c in pairs(counts) do total = total + c end

	local prev = self.pattern.lastUnitCount
	self.pattern.lastUnitCount = total
	self.pattern.unitGrowthRate = prev > 0 and ((total - prev) / self.SCAN_INTERVAL) or 0
	self.pattern.buildPhase = self.pattern.unitGrowthRate > 0.5

	return total
end

function IntelManager:updatePattern()
	local now = tick()
	if now - self.lastPatternUpdate < self.PATTERN_INTERVAL then return end
	self.lastPatternUpdate = now

	local offensive, total = 0, 0
	for uid, s in pairs(self.sightings) do
		if not s.isStale then
			total = total + 1
			if self:isNearOurTerritory(s.tileX, s.tileZ) then
				offensive = offensive + 1
			end
		end
	end

	if total > 0 then
		local ratio = offensive / total
		local growth = math.clamp(self.pattern.unitGrowthRate * 0.5, 0, 1)
		self.pattern.aggressiveness = math.clamp(
			ratio * 0.6 + growth * 0.2 + self.pattern.aggressiveness * 0.2, 0, 1
		)
	end
end

function IntelManager:isNearOurTerritory(tx, tz)
	if not warState or not warState.ourUnits then return false end
	for _, u in pairs(warState.ourUnits) do
		if u and u.Health > 0 then
			local ux, uz = getUnitTile(u)
			if distance(ux, uz, tx, tz) <= 15 then return true end
		end
	end
	return false
end

function IntelManager:getEffectiveThreat(key)
	local r = self.threatMap[key]
	if not r then return 0 end
	return math.max(r.enemyCount * 3.0 - r.friendlyCount * 0.5, 0)
end

function IntelManager:getHighestThreatNear(tx, tz, maxR)
	maxR = maxR or 30
	local best, bestScore = nil, 0
	for k, r in pairs(self.threatMap) do
		local rx, rz = self:regionCenter(k)
		local d = math.max(distance(rx, rz, tx, tz), 1)
		if d <= maxR then
			local ws = r.score / d
			if ws > bestScore then bestScore = ws best = k end
		end
	end
	if best then
		local rx, rz = self:regionCenter(best)
		return rx, rz, self.threatMap[best]
	end
	return nil, nil, nil
end

function IntelManager:getState()
	return {
		aggressiveness = self.pattern.aggressiveness,
		buildPhase = self.pattern.buildPhase,
		visibleEnemies = self.pattern.lastUnitCount,
	}
end

return IntelManager
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Humanizer"] = [=[
local Humanizer = {}
Humanizer.__index = Humanizer

function Humanizer.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, Humanizer)
	self.config = {
		minDelay = 0,
		maxDelay = 0,
		burstMinActions = 3,
		burstMaxActions = 7,
		burstPauseMin = 0.3,
		burstPauseMax = 1.8,
		idleChancePerCycle = 0.02,
		idleDurationMin = 2.0,
		idleDurationMax = 8.0,
		pathJitterChance = 0.15,
		pathJitterMax = 2,
		majorActionCooldown = 1.2,
		maxUnitsPerCycle = 8,
		unitSelectionVariance = 3,
		decisionDelayMin = 0.15,
		decisionDelayMax = 0.8,
	}
	self.burstCount = 0
	self.burstTarget = 0
	self.nextBurstPause = 0
	self.idleUntil = 0
	self.lastMajorAction = 0
	self.cycleCount = 0
	self.lastJitteredPositions = {}
	self.unitSeeds = {}
	return self
end

function Humanizer:rand(min, max)
	return min + math.random() * (max - min)
end

function Humanizer:randInt(min, max)
	return math.floor(min + math.random() * (max - min + 1))
end

function Humanizer:shouldPause()
	return false
end

function Humanizer:preOrder()
	self.burstCount = self.burstCount + 1
	self.lastMajorAction = tick()
	return 0
end

function Humanizer:executeOrder(orderFunc, ...)
	if self:shouldPause() then
		task.wait(self:rand(self.config.burstPauseMin, self.config.burstPauseMax))
	end

	local delay = self:preOrder()
	if delay > 0 then task.wait(delay) end
	return orderFunc(...)
end

function Humanizer:jitterPosition(unitId, targetX, targetZ)
	if math.random() > self.config.pathJitterChance then
		return targetX, targetZ
	end

	local jitter = self:randInt(1, self.config.pathJitterMax)
	local offset_x = self:randInt(-jitter, jitter)
	local offset_z = self:randInt(-jitter, jitter)
	local jx = targetX + offset_x
	local jz = targetZ + offset_z
	self.lastJitteredPositions[unitId] = {x = jx, z = jz}
	return jx, jz
end

function Humanizer:selectUnits(unitTable)
	local all = {}
	for k, v in pairs(unitTable) do
		table.insert(all, {key = k, value = v})
	end

	local count = math.min(
		#all,
		self:randInt(
			self.config.maxUnitsPerCycle - self.config.unitSelectionVariance,
			self.config.maxUnitsPerCycle + self.config.unitSelectionVariance
		)
	)

	for i = #all, 2, -1 do
		local j = math.random(1, i)
		all[i], all[j] = all[j], all[i]
	end

	local result = {}
	for i = 1, count do
		result[all[i].key] = all[i].value
	end
	return result
end

function Humanizer:beginCycle()
	self.cycleCount = self.cycleCount + 1
	self.lastJitteredPositions = {}
end

return Humanizer
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Signal"] = [=[
local Signal = {}
Signal.__index = Signal
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2

function Signal.new(signalName)
	local self = setmetatable({}, Signal)
	self.signalName = signalName
	self.listeners = {}
	self.fireCount = 0
	self.failureCount = 0
	return self
end

function Signal:connect(listener)
	assert(type(listener) == "function", string.format("Signal(%s):connect needs a function", self.signalName))
	table.insert(self.listeners, listener)
	return function()
		local index = table.find(self.listeners, listener)
		if index ~= nil then table.remove(self.listeners, index) end
	end
end

function Signal:fire(...)
	self.fireCount += 1
	local listenersNow = table.clone(self.listeners)
	for _, listener in ipairs(listenersNow) do
		local signalName = self.signalName
		local listenerOk = xpcall(listener, function(failure)
			Logger.error("Utility.Signal", "listenerFailed", {
				signal = signalName, message = tostring(failure),
			}, debug.traceback(tostring(failure), RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL))
			return failure
		end, ...)
		if not listenerOk then self.failureCount += 1 end
	end
end

function Signal:stats()
	return {
		signal = self.signalName, listeners = #self.listeners,
		fired = self.fireCount, failures = self.failureCount,
	}
end

function Signal:Destroy() table.clear(self.listeners) end

return Signal
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Stats"] = [=[
local Stats = {}
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

local MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR = 2

function Stats.median(samples)
	local count = #samples
	if count == 0 then return nil end
	local sorted = table.clone(samples)
	table.sort(sorted)
	local middle = (count + 1) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
	return (sorted[math.floor(middle)] + sorted[math.ceil(middle)]) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
end

function Stats.max(samples)
	local largest = nil
	for _, value in ipairs(samples) do
		if largest == nil or value > largest then largest = value end
	end
	return largest
end

return Tracer.instrument("Utility.Stats", Stats)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Tracer"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Tracer — no-op build (RAM-optimized).

	Sebelumnya: Tracer.instrument() membungkus SETIAP fungsi di SETIAP modul
	dengan closure + pcall + describeValue + table.pack. Itu = ratusan
	closure tambahan yang hidup selama script berjalan.

	Sekarang: instrument() cuma mengembalikan modul apa adanya.
	withTrace() memanggil callee langsung tanpa xpcall.
	context() return "none", 0.
]]

local Tracer = {}

Tracer.traceCounter = 0
Tracer.currentTraceId = "none"
Tracer.frames = {}

function Tracer.context() return "none", 0 end
function Tracer.withTrace(_, callee, ...) return callee(...) end
function Tracer.step() end
function Tracer.describeValue(v) return v end
function Tracer.describeValues() return {} end
function Tracer.wrapFunction(_, callee) return callee end
function Tracer.instrument(_, moduleTable) return moduleTable end

return Tracer
]=]

-- ===============================================================================================
-- [PORT-3] WarAI — hanya 2 baris berubah dari versi Linoria
-- ===============================================================================================
MODULE_SOURCES["WarAI"] = [=[
--[[
	WarAI — versi ringan + push status dari Scheduler hooks.
]]

local WarAI = { attached = false }
local IntelManager = require("Utility/IntelManager")
local Humanizer = require("Utility/Humanizer")
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")
local SettingsCatalog = require("Features/Core/SettingsCatalog")
local Rayfield = require("GUI/Rayfield")
local Menu = require("Menu")
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")
local Remotes = require("Game/Remotes")
local Session = require("Game/Session")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")
local Units = require("Game/Units")
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")
local TechCatalog = require("Features/Economy/TechCatalog")
local EconomyPlanner = require("Features/Economy/EconomyPlanner")
local OrderExecutor = require("Features/Orders/OrderExecutor")
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")
local ArmyControl = require("Features/Orders/ArmyControl")
local NavyPlanner = require("Features/Navy/NavyPlanner")
local AirPlanner = require("Features/Air/AirPlanner")
local Scorecard = require("Features/Review/Scorecard")
local AntiIdle = require("Features/Core/AntiIdle")
local Scheduler = require("Features/Core/Scheduler")

local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2
local STATUS_KEYS = {}

local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

local maid = nil
local worldMaid = nil

function WarAI.defineCoreSettings()
	SettingsCatalog.defineAll()
end

function WarAI.buildWorld(reason)
	if worldMaid ~= nil then worldMaid:clean() end
	worldMaid = Maid.new("WarAI.world")
	Units.reset(reason)
	Tick.reset(reason)
	Resolver.invalidate(reason)
	local mapModel = Session.mapModel()
	MapGraph.build(mapModel, worldMaid)
	Ownership.attach(worldMaid)
	local country = Session.ourCountry()
	Units.attach(mapModel, country and country.Name or nil, worldMaid)
	Tick.attach(MapGraph, worldMaid)
	Scheduler.refreshContext(country and country.Name or nil)
end

function WarAI.onCountryChanged(country, reason)
	local countryName = country and country.Name or nil
	Units.setOurCountry(countryName)
	OrderExecutor.reset("country changed")
	ActionDispatcher.reset("country changed")
	Resolver.invalidate("country changed")
	Scheduler.refreshContext(countryName)
	if countryName ~= nil then
		Scheduler.requestTick({ reason = "country changed" })
	end
end

local Intel = { intel = IntelManager.new(), humanizer = Humanizer.new() }

function WarAI.onTick(world, analysis, plan)
	EconomyPlanner.run(world, analysis)
	ArmyControl.run(world, analysis)
	if Settings.get("navy.enabled") then
		NavyPlanner.run(world)
	end
	AirPlanner.run(world, plan)
	Ownership.verifyCounts()
end

function WarAI.attach()
	maid = Maid.new("WarAI")
	WarAI.defineCoreSettings()
	Menu.build(STATUS_KEYS, WarAI.detach)
	maid:give(Menu.destroy)
	maid:give(Menu.startPump())

	if not Session.isCorrectPlace() then
		Logger.error("WarAI", "wrongPlace", { placeId = game.PlaceId })
		WarAI.attached = true
		return
	end

	GameModules.loadAll()
	Remotes.verifyAll()
	TechCatalog.build()

	for _, signal in ipairs({
		Session.countryChanged, Session.mapReplaced,
		MapGraph.tileColorChanged, MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged,
		Ownership.ownerChanged, Units.unitAdded, Units.unitMoved,
		Units.unitRemoved, Units.unitChanged, Tick.dayTick,
	}) do
		maid:give(signal)
	end

	maid:give(Session.countryChanged:connect(WarAI.onCountryChanged))
	maid:give(Session.mapReplaced:connect(function(reason)
		maid:give(task.spawn(function()
			local ok = pcall(WarAI.buildWorld, reason)
			if not ok then
				Logger.error("WarAI", "worldRebuildFailed", { reason = reason })
			end
		end))
	end))
	maid:give(function()
		if worldMaid ~= nil then worldMaid:clean(); worldMaid = nil end
	end)

	WarAI.buildWorld("attach")
	OrderExecutor.attach(maid)
	ActionDispatcher.attach(maid)
	Scorecard.attach(maid)
	AntiIdle.attach(maid)

	Scheduler.attach(maid, {
		onVerdict = function(verdict)
			Menu.setStatus("state", verdict.reason or "?")
			local c = verdict.country
			Menu.setStatus("country", c and c.Name or "none")
		end,
		onPlanned = function(world, plan, summary)
			Scorecard.recordLaunches(world, plan, summary.sent > 0)
			Menu.setStatus("units", string.format("ours %d, enemy %d", #world.ourUnits, #world.enemyUnits))
			Menu.setStatus("tick", string.format("%.3f s (%d passes)", Tick.periodSeconds() or 0, Tick.passCount))
			Menu.setStatus("plan", string.format("%d orders, %d halts", plan:summary().orders, plan:summary().halts))
			Menu.setStatus("orders", string.format("sent %d, accepted %d, dry-run %d",
				OrderExecutor.stats.sent, OrderExecutor.stats.accepted, OrderExecutor.stats.dryRun))
			Menu.setStatus("scorecard", string.format("won %d, lost %d, open %d",
				Scorecard.summary().attacksWon, Scorecard.summary().attacksLost, Scorecard.summary().open))
			if EconomyPlanner.lastSummary then
				Menu.setStatus("economy", string.format("money %.0f, research %s",
					EconomyPlanner.lastSummary.money,
					EconomyPlanner.lastSummary.research and tostring(EconomyPlanner.lastSummary.research.chosen) or "-"))
			end
		end,
		onTick = WarAI.onTick,
	})

	Session.attach(maid)
	WarAI.attached = true
end

function WarAI.detach()
	if maid == nil then return end
	WarAI.attached = false
	print("[CTW_WarAI] detaching...")

	local ok, err = pcall(function() maid:clean() end)
	if not ok then
		print("[CTW_WarAI] detach error:", tostring(err))
	end
	maid = nil
	worldMaid = nil

	-- Reset Rayfield supaya require ulang bikin window baru, bukan reuse yang lama.
	pcall(function()
		local Rayfield = require("GUI/Rayfield")
		Rayfield.instance = nil
	end)

	if shared.CTW_WarAI == WarAI then
		shared.CTW_WarAI = nil
	end
	print("[CTW_WarAI] detached")
end

function WarAI.status()
	return { attached = WarAI.attached }
end

return Tracer.instrument("WarAI", WarAI)
]=]

-- ===============================================================================================
-- EPILOGUE LOADER
-- ===============================================================================================

---Source of a bundled module; an unknown path is a build error surfaced at runtime.
local function readModuleSource(modulePath)
	local source = MODULE_SOURCES[modulePath]
	if source == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: module '%s' is not in the bundle", modulePath))
	end
	return source
end

local moduleCache = {}
local loadingStack = {}
-- The executor's require, kept before modules shadow the name: game ModuleScripts load through it.
local nativeRequire = require

---Load a module by slash path, compiling it with its own chunk name so tracebacks name the file.
---A ModuleScript instance (a game module such as ReplicatedStorage.Common.ArmyCommon) is loaded
---with the executor's require instead.
---@param modulePath string | Instance
---@return any
local function requireModule(modulePath)
	if typeof(modulePath) == "Instance" then
		return nativeRequire(modulePath)
	end

	local cached = moduleCache[modulePath]
	if cached ~= nil then
		return cached.value
	end

	for _, loadingPath in ipairs(loadingStack) do
		if loadingPath == modulePath then
			return error(string.format("Loader: circular require of '%s' (stack: %s)", modulePath, table.concat(loadingStack, " -> ")))
		end
	end

	local source = readModuleSource(modulePath)
	-- The wrapper shares line 1 with the module so reported line numbers match the file.
	local chunk, compileError = loadstring("return function(require) " .. source .. "\nend", "=" .. modulePath)

	if chunk == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' failed to compile: %s", modulePath, tostring(compileError)))
	end

	table.insert(loadingStack, modulePath)

	local loadOk, moduleValue = xpcall(function()
		return chunk()(requireModule)
	end, function(failure)
		return debug.traceback(tostring(failure), 2)
	end)

	table.remove(loadingStack)

	if not loadOk then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' failed to load:\n%s", modulePath, tostring(moduleValue)), 0)
	end

	if moduleValue == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' returned nil", modulePath))
	end

	moduleCache[modulePath] = { value = moduleValue }

	return moduleValue
end

local bootOk, bootResult = pcall(requireModule, "WarAI")

-- Karena WarAI hanya mendefinisikan fungsi, kita harus memanggil attach() untuk menyalakannya
if bootOk and type(bootResult) == "table" and type(bootResult.attach) == "function" then
    bootOk, bootResult = pcall(bootResult.attach)
end

if not bootOk then
	local text = tostring(bootResult)
	pcall(writefile, "CTW_WarAI_boot_error.txt", text)
	print("[CTW_WarAI] module load failed:\n" .. text)
	warn("[CTW_WarAI] boot failed:\n" .. text)
	error(bootResult, 0)
end

return bootResult
